        const resolved=learningSuggestionResolvedRowRefs(item,state);
        if(!resolved.size)return;
        const hasReopened=state.candidates.some(candidate=>
          candidate?.status==='pending'&&(candidate.rowRefs||[]).some(value=>resolved.has(String(value)))
        );
        if(!hasReopened)return;
        buildLearningSuggestions(item,state);
        changed=true;
      });
      return changed;
    }

    function rollbackLegacyGlobalQuestionReconciliation(){
      let changed=false;

      // Restaura preguntas de learnings modificadas por la reconciliación global anterior.
      learnings.forEach(learning=>{
        const history=Array.isArray(learning?.questionHistory)?learning.questionHistory:[];
        if(!history.length)return;

        const current=Array.isArray(learning.open)?[...learning.open]:[];
        const removeKeys=new Set(
          history.map(entry=>normalizeText(entry?.to||'')).filter(Boolean)
        );
        const restored=current.filter(value=>!removeKeys.has(normalizeText(value)));

        history.forEach(entry=>{
          const original=String(entry?.from||'').trim();
          if(original&&!restored.some(value=>normalizeText(value)===normalizeText(original))){
            restored.push(original);
          }
        });

        learning.open=restored;
        delete learning.questionHistory;
        changed=true;
      });

      // Las preguntas de análisis/documento son histórico: nunca deben cerrarse por matching global.
      openQuestionItems.forEach(question=>{
        const history=Array.isArray(question?.questionHistory)?question.questionHistory:[];
        if(!history.length)return;
        const original=String(history[0]?.from||'').trim();
        if(original)question.text=original;
        delete question.questionHistory;
        delete question.resolvedAt;
        delete question.resolvedBy;
        changed=true;
      });

      return changed;
    }

    function reconcileConfirmedLearningQuestions(){
      // Solo revisa preguntas dentro del learning que el usuario vinculó explícitamente.
      let changed=false;
      sourceItems.forEach(item=>{
        const candidates=item?.insightRegister?.learningSuggestions?.candidates||[];
        candidates.forEach(candidate=>{
          if(candidate?.status!=='confirmed')return;
          if(!['Reforzar learning existente','Ampliar learning'].includes(candidate.action))return;

          const learningId=String(candidate.appliedLearningId||candidate.targetLearningId||'').trim();
          if(!learningId)return;
          const learning=learnings.find(value=>String(value?.id||'')===learningId);
          if(!learning)return;

          const rows=learningSuggestionRows(item,candidate);
          if(!rows.length)return;
          if(reconcileLearningQuestions(learning,rows,item))changed=true;
        });
      });
      return changed;
    }

    function applyFinalLearningNaturalCopy(){
      let changed=false;
      learnings.forEach(item=>{
        if(!item||item.copyRevision===FINAL_LEARNING_COPY_REVISION)return;
        const patch=FINAL_LEARNING_COPY[item.id];
        if(patch){
          ['title','know','text'].forEach(key=>{
            if(item[key]!==patch[key]){item[key]=patch[key];changed=true;}
          });
          item.copyRevision=FINAL_LEARNING_COPY_REVISION;
          item.updatedAt='2026-10-22';
          changed=true;
          return;
        }

        const title=String(item.title||'').trim();
        const component=String(item.component||'').trim();

        if(
          title==='La identificación SUMA aporta valor cuando reduce esfuerzo y aprovecha información ya conocida' ||
          title==='Estar identificado debe ahorrar pasos y aprovechar datos ya conocidos' ||
          title==='Si el usuario ya está identificado, aprovechemos los datos que ya tenemos' ||
          title==='Si el usuario ya está identificado, no le pidamos los mismos datos otra vez'
        ){
          item.title='Si el usuario ya está logado en SUMA, aprovechemos los datos que ya tenemos';
          item.know='Si ya conocemos sus datos y beneficios, volver a pedirlos añade esfuerzo.';
          item.text='Podemos recuperar la información disponible y aplicar sus ventajas, dejando claro qué se ha recuperado o aplicado.';
          item.copyRevision=FINAL_LEARNING_COPY_REVISION;
          item.updatedAt='2026-10-22';
          changed=true;
          return;
        }

        if(
          title==='El mensaje inicial no deja claro si las Millas mostradas son saldo total o máximo aplicable a la reserva' ||
          title==='No queda claro si las Millas son el saldo total o el máximo que se puede usar' ||
          title==='No queda claro qué representa la cifra de Millas' ||
          component==='Slider de Millas'
        ){
          item.title='No queda claro qué representa la cifra de Millas';
          item.know='La cifra puede parecer el saldo total cuando quizá sea el máximo que se puede usar en esa reserva. Esa diferencia cambia cómo se entiende el canje.';
          item.text='Antes de usar el slider, tiene que quedar claro qué significa esa cifra y qué cantidad puede aplicarse realmente.';
          item.copyRevision=FINAL_LEARNING_COPY_REVISION;
          item.updatedAt='2026-10-22';
          changed=true;
        }
      });
      return changed;
    }

    function finalizeLearningStateAfterRestore(){
      const rollbackQuestions=rollbackLegacyGlobalQuestionReconciliation();
      const migrated=migrateWorkspaceToSchema3AfterRestore();
      const deskRepair=repairBuscadorDeskResearchLearningDecisions();
      const stateRepair=repairBuscadorAuditAndRestoreQMLearningDecisions();
      const componentRepair=normalizeLearningSingleComponentState();
      const metadataRepair=repairKnownLearningMetadata();
      const suggestionLockRepair=stabilizeResolvedLearningSuggestionRows();
      // restoreProjectHistory puede reponer una copia anterior del workspace después
      // de la limpieza inicial. Por eso la deduplicación definitiva se ejecuta aquí,
      // cuando ya han terminado project history, IndexedDB, Excel sync y reparaciones.
      const historyChanged=normalizeLearningHistoryState();
      refreshCatalogMetadata();
      const targetedQuestionRepair=reconcileConfirmedLearningQuestions();
      const naturalCopyRepair=applyFinalLearningNaturalCopy();
      if(rollbackQuestions||migrated||deskRepair||stateRepair||componentRepair||metadataRepair||suggestionLockRepair||targetedQuestionRepair||historyChanged||naturalCopyRepair)safeStorageWrite({project:false});
      // Si el histórico de proyecto está disponible, guarda también el estado saneado
      // para que los errores corregidos no reaparezcan en la siguiente restauración.
      if((rollbackQuestions||deskRepair||stateRepair||componentRepair||metadataRepair||suggestionLockRepair||targetedQuestionRepair||historyChanged||naturalCopyRepair)&&typeof projectHistory!=='undefined'&&projectHistory?.ready&&!projectHistory?.error&&typeof queueProjectHistory==='function'){
        Promise.resolve(queueProjectHistory()).catch(error=>console.warn('[UX Intel learning repair] No se pudo persistir el saneado en history',error));
      }
      refreshAllTaxonomyFilters?.();
      renderOverview();
      renderLearnings();
      return rollbackQuestions||migrated||deskRepair||stateRepair||componentRepair||metadataRepair||suggestionLockRepair||targetedQuestionRepair||historyChanged||naturalCopyRepair;
    }

    const cleanProof=value=>String(value||'').replace(/\s*·\s*L-\d+/g,'').replace(/L-\d+/g,'').trim();
    function sourceMatch(name,item=null){
      const candidates=sourceCandidates(name,item);
      if(!candidates.length)return null;
      if(candidates.length===1)return candidates[0];
      const structured=candidates.find(source=>source.simpleKnowledgeActive&&source.insightRegister);
      return structured||candidates[0];
    }
    function contextMetadata(item){
      return `${item.zone?`<div><strong>Zona:</strong> ${escapeHtml(item.zone)}</div>`:''}${item.scope==='Transversal'?'<div><strong>Alcance:</strong> Transversal</div>':''}${item.initiatives?.length?`<div><strong>Iniciativa:</strong> ${item.initiatives.map(escapeHtml).join(' · ')}</div>`:''}`;
    }
    function searchContext(item){return [pathOf(item),item.zone,item.scope,...(item.contextConcepts||[]),...(item.initiatives||[])].filter(Boolean).join(' ');}
    function sourceRawFileName(source,fallback=''){
      return String(source?.originalName||source?.fileMeta?.name||fallback||'').trim();
    }
    function sourceReferences(names,contextItem=null){
      const references=[],seen=new Set();
      [...new Set(names||[])].forEach(name=>{
        const source=sourceMatch(name,contextItem);
        const type=source?.type||sourceType({name});
        const rawFile=sourceRawFileName(source,name);
        const key=source?.id||normalizeText(rawFile||source?.name||name);
        if(seen.has(key))return;
        seen.add(key);
        references.push(`<div class="source-reference"><strong>${escapeHtml(source?.name||name)}</strong><span>${escapeHtml(humanKnowledgeLabel(type))}</span>${rawFile?`<span class="source-file-name"><b>Archivo:</b> ${escapeHtml(rawFile)}</span>`:''}</div>`);
      });
      // Publication history can point to an exact document even when sourceNames contains a generic label.
      (contextItem?.publicationHistory||[]).forEach(entry=>{
        const source=sourceItems.find(value=>String(value?.id||'')===String(entry?.documentId||''));
        if(!source)return;
        const rawFile=sourceRawFileName(source);
        const key=source.id||normalizeText(rawFile||source.name);
        if(seen.has(key))return;
        seen.add(key);
        const type=source.type||sourceType(source);
        references.push(`<div class="source-reference"><strong>${escapeHtml(source.name||rawFile)}</strong><span>${escapeHtml(humanKnowledgeLabel(type))}</span>${rawFile?`<span class="source-file-name"><b>Archivo:</b> ${escapeHtml(rawFile)}</span>`:''}</div>`);
      });
      return `<div class="source-reference-list">${references.join('')}</div>`;
    }
    function qualityList(item){return `<dl class="quality-list">${Object.entries(item.quality||{}).map(([label,value])=>`<div class="quality-item"><dt>${label}</dt><dd>${value}</dd></div>`).join('')}</dl>`;}
    function knowledgeStateDisclosure(item,{result=false}={}){
      const state=UXKnowledge.knowledgeState(item),rows=[['Confianza',state.confidence],['Validación interna',state.internalValidation],result?['Resultado del experimento',state.experimentResult]:null,['Recomendación',state.recommendation],['Implementación',state.implementation]].filter(Boolean);
      return `<details class="knowledge-state-disclosure"><summary>Estado del conocimiento</summary><dl>${rows.map(([label,value])=>`<div><dt>${escapeHtml(label)}</dt><dd>${escapeHtml(value)}</dd></div>`).join('')}</dl></details>`;
    }
    function confidenceExplanation(item){if(item.confidence==='Alta')return 'Varias fuentes internas y externas coinciden, con evidencia cercana al comportamiento o a la experimentación.';if(item.confidence==='Media')return 'Hay señales consistentes, pero faltan datos, validación específica o capacidad para generalizar el resultado.';return 'El aprendizaje se apoya en señales iniciales o indirectas y necesita más contraste antes de aplicarse.';}
    function unknownScope(item){if(item.market==='No especificado')return 'La documentación disponible no identifica el mercado o segmento, por lo que no sabemos si el resultado cambia entre contextos.';if(item.market.includes('ES/ES'))return 'No está confirmado que se comporte igual fuera de ES/ES, en otros segmentos o en otros momentos del journey.';return 'Debe comprobarse en cada flujo y contexto concreto antes de tratarlo como una regla general.';}
    function learningRelations(items){return `<div class="relation-list">${items.map(item=>`<div class="relation-row"><div class="relation-copy"><strong>${escapeHtml(item.title)}</strong><span>${escapeHtml(pathOf(item))} · Confianza ${escapeHtml(item.confidence.toLowerCase())}</span></div><button class="relation-action" type="button" data-learning="${item.id}">Ver learning</button><span></span></div>`).join('')}</div>`;}
    function experimentRelations(items){return `<div class="relation-list">${items.map(item=>`<div class="relation-row"><div class="relation-copy"><strong>${item.title}</strong><span>${item.device} · Resultado ${item.outcome.toLowerCase()}</span></div><button class="relation-action" type="button" data-detail="experiment" data-id="${item.id}">Ver test</button><span></span></div>`).join('')}</div>`;}
    function patternRelations(items){return `<div class="relation-list">${items.map(item=>`<div class="relation-row"><div class="relation-copy"><strong>${item.title}</strong><span>${pathOf(item)} · Confianza ${item.confidence.toLowerCase()}</span></div><button class="relation-action" type="button" data-detail="pattern" data-id="${item.id}">Ver patrón</button><span></span></div>`).join('')}</div>`;}
    const focusableSelector='a[href],button:not([disabled]),input:not([disabled]),select:not([disabled]),textarea:not([disabled]),[tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])';
    function keepFocusInside(container,event){const focusables=[...container.querySelectorAll(focusableSelector)].filter(item=>!item.hidden&&item.getClientRects().length);if(!focusables.length)return;const first=focusables[0],last=focusables.at(-1);if(event.shiftKey&&document.activeElement===first){event.preventDefault();last.focus();}else if(!event.shiftKey&&document.activeElement===last){event.preventDefault();first.focus();}}
    let drawerReturnFocus=null,drawerReturnScroll=0;
    function openDrawer(header,body){if(!drawer.classList.contains('open')){drawerReturnFocus=document.activeElement;drawerReturnScroll=window.scrollY;}drawer.innerHTML=`<button class="drawer-close" type="button" aria-label="Cerrar detalle">×</button><div class="drawer-inner"><header class="drawer-header">${header}</header><div class="drawer-body">${body}</div></div>`;drawer.inert=false;drawer.classList.add('open');drawerBackdrop.classList.add('open');drawer.setAttribute('aria-hidden','false');drawer.querySelector('.drawer-close').focus({preventScroll:true});}
    function closeDrawer(){if(!drawer.classList.contains('open'))return;drawer.classList.remove('open');drawerBackdrop.classList.remove('open');drawer.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');drawer.inert=true;if(drawerReturnFocus?.isConnected)drawerReturnFocus.focus({preventScroll:true});const returnScroll=drawerReturnScroll;requestAnimationFrame(()=>window.scrollTo({top:returnScroll,left:0,behavior:'auto'}));drawerReturnFocus=null;}
    function learningCompactList(items){
      const values=(items||[]).map(value=>String(value||'').trim()).filter(Boolean);
      return values.length?`<ul class="drawer-list learning-compact-list">${values.map(value=>`<li>${escapeHtml(value)}</li>`).join('')}</ul>`:'<p class="scope-note">Sin información adicional.</p>';
    }
    function openLearning(id){
      const item=learnings.find(value=>value.id===id);if(!item)return;
      const relatedTests=experiments.filter(test=>test.learningIds.includes(id));
      const relatedPatterns=patternItems.filter(pattern=>pattern.proof.includes(id)||pattern.component===item.component).slice(0,3);
      const impactMarkup=item.impactUx?`<span class="status-badge ${impactClass(item.impactUx)}">Impacto UX ${escapeHtml(item.impactUx.toLowerCase())}</span>`:'';
      const initiativeLabel=(item.initiatives||[]).length?(item.initiatives||[]).join(' · '):(item.initiative||'');
      const header=`<div class="drawer-kicker">Learning${item.component?` · ${escapeHtml(item.component)}`:item.screen?` · ${escapeHtml(item.screen)}`:''}</div><h2>${escapeHtml(item.title)}</h2>${item.know?`<p class="drawer-lead">${escapeHtml(item.know)}</p>`:''}<div class="drawer-tags">${impactMarkup}</div><div class="drawer-context learning-drawer-context"><div><strong>Dónde aplica:</strong> ${escapeHtml(pathOf(item))}</div><div><strong>Dispositivo:</strong> ${escapeHtml(item.device)}${item.market&&item.market!=='No especificado'?` · <strong>Mercado:</strong> ${escapeHtml(item.market)}`:''}</div>${initiativeLabel?`<div><strong>Iniciativa:</strong> ${escapeHtml(initiativeLabel)}</div>`:''}</div>`;
      const openMarkup=(item.open||[]).length?`<section class="drawer-section learning-visible-section"><h3>Qué falta por resolver</h3>${learningCompactList(item.open)}</section>`:'';
      const evidenceMarkup=`<details class="learning-detail-group"><summary>Ver evidencia y límites</summary><div class="learning-detail-group-body"><section><h4>Evidencia que lo respalda</h4>${learningCompactList(item.forEvidence)}</section>${(item.contrary||[]).length?`<section><h4>Matices o datos que no encajan del todo</h4>${learningCompactList(item.contrary)}</section>`:''}${(item.noProof||[]).length?`<section><h4>Qué no demuestra</h4>${learningCompactList(item.noProof)}</section>`:''}<section><h4>Dónde no sabemos si aplica igual</h4><p class="scope-note">${escapeHtml(unknownScope(item))}</p></section></div></details>`;
      const applicationMarkup=`<details class="learning-detail-group"><summary>Ver aplicación y relaciones</summary><div class="learning-detail-group-body">${(item.applications||[]).length?`<section><h4>Aplicaciones</h4>${learningCompactList(item.applications)}</section>`:''}${relatedPatterns.length?`<section><h4>Patrones relacionados</h4>${patternRelations(relatedPatterns)}</section>`:''}${relatedTests.length?`<section><h4>Test A/B relacionados</h4>${experimentRelations(relatedTests)}</section>`:''}</div></details>`;
      const sourcesMarkup=`<details class="learning-detail-group"><summary>Ver fuentes y trazabilidad</summary><div class="learning-detail-group-body"><section><h4>Fuentes y archivos de origen</h4>${sourceReferences(item.sourceNames,item)}</section><p class="learning-update-meta">Creado ${escapeHtml(formatDateEs(item.createdAt))} · Última actualización ${escapeHtml(formatDateEs(item.updatedAt))}</p></div></details>`;
      const body=`${taxonomyLegacyDisclosure(item,'learning')}<section class="drawer-section learning-visible-section"><h3>Qué implica para UX</h3><p>${escapeHtml(item.text)}</p></section>${openMarkup}<div class="learning-collapsed-groups">${evidenceMarkup}${applicationMarkup}${sourcesMarkup}</div><div class="form-actions"><button class="secondary-button" type="button" data-learning-component-edit="${escapeHtml(item.id)}">Editar clasificación</button><button class="secondary-button" type="button" data-close-editor>Cerrar</button></div>`;
      openDrawer(header,body);
    }
    function openLearningComponentEditor(id){
      const item=learnings.find(value=>value.id===id);if(!item)return;
      const options=learningComponentOptions(item);
      const current=canonicalComponentName(item.component);
      const legacy=String(item.component||'').trim();
      const deviceOptions=['Desktop','Mobile','Desktop + mobile','No especificado'];
      const currentDevice=deviceOptions.includes(String(item.device||'').trim())?String(item.device).trim():'No especificado';
      const header=`<div class="drawer-kicker">Learning · clasificación</div><h2>Editar clasificación</h2><p class="drawer-lead">Puedes corregir manualmente la clasificación del learning cuando conoces mejor su contexto. Puedes reutilizar un componente existente o escribir uno nuevo. El dispositivo queda como metadata gobernada del learning.</p>`;
      const currentLabel=current||legacy;
      const componentListId=`learning-edit-component-options-${String(item.id||'learning').replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g,'-')}`;
      const componentHelp=currentLabel?(canonicalComponentName(currentLabel)?`Componente existente: ${canonicalComponentName(currentLabel)}.`:'Componente manual. Se guardará con este nombre.'):'Escribe un componente o elige uno existente. Un learning tiene un único componente principal.';
      const body=`<form id="learning-component-edit-form" data-learning-id="${escapeHtml(item.id)}"><div class="form-grid"><label class="form-field full">Componente<input name="component" type="text" list="${escapeHtml(componentListId)}" value="${escapeHtml(currentLabel)}" placeholder="Escribe o selecciona un componente" data-component-free-input><datalist id="${escapeHtml(componentListId)}">${options.map(value=>`<option value="${escapeHtml(value)}"></option>`).join('')}</datalist><span class="form-help" data-component-free-help>${escapeHtml(componentHelp)}</span></label><label class="form-field full">Dispositivo<select name="device" required>${deviceOptions.map(value=>`<option value="${escapeHtml(value)}" ${value===currentDevice?'selected':''}>${escapeHtml(value)}</option>`).join('')}</select><span class="form-help">Puedes corregir este dato manualmente. Las nuevas evidencias no sobrescriben el dispositivo de un learning ya consolidado.</span></label></div><div class="form-actions"><button class="secondary-button" type="button" data-learning-component-cancel="${escapeHtml(item.id)}">Cancelar</button><button class="action-button" type="submit">Guardar clasificación</button></div></form>`;
      openDrawer(header,body);
    }
    function openFullLearning(id){
      const item=learnings.find(value=>value.id===id);if(!item)return;
      closeDrawer();showPanel('learnings');resetFilterGroup('learning');
      for(const level of ['product','component']){
        const select=document.getElementById(`learning-${level}`),value=level==='component'?componentsOf(item)[0]:item[level];
        if(value&&[...select.options].some(option=>option.value===value)){select.value=value;refreshHierarchy('learning',level);}
      }
      requestAnimationFrame(()=>document.querySelector(`[data-learning="${CSS.escape(id)}"]`)?.scrollIntoView({block:'center'}));
    }
    function evidenceLimit(item){if(item.kind==='Quantum Metric')return 'Describe el comportamiento observado en el periodo y contexto analizado, pero no explica por sí sola la causa.';if(item.kind==='Test A/B')return 'El resultado se limita a la variante, métricas, periodo y dispositivo analizados.';if(['Benchmark','Desk research'].includes(item.kind))return 'Ayuda a contrastar patrones y generar hipótesis, pero no valida por sí sola el comportamiento en Air Europa.';if(['Análisis heurístico','Análisis conductual','Análisis UX de CRO','Auditoría UX'].includes(item.kind))return 'Es una interpretación experta que debe leerse junto con datos internos, research o experimentación.';return 'Debe interpretarse dentro del contexto en el que se obtuvo y contrastarse antes de generalizar.';}
    function openEvidence(id){relatedEvidenceDrawer(id);}
    function openKnowledgeUnit(id){const item=knowledgeUnits.find(value=>value.id===id);if(!item)return;const header=`<div class="drawer-kicker">${escapeHtml(item.type)}</div><h2>${escapeHtml(item.text)}</h2><div class="drawer-tags"><span class="status-badge ${confidenceClass(item.confidence)}">Confianza ${escapeHtml(item.confidence.toLowerCase())}</span></div><div class="drawer-context"><div><strong>Dónde aplica:</strong> ${escapeHtml(pathOf(item))}</div><div><strong>Dispositivo:</strong> ${escapeHtml(item.device)} · <strong>Mercado:</strong> ${escapeHtml(item.market)}</div></div>`;const body=`<section class="drawer-section"><h3>Clasificación</h3><p>${escapeHtml(item.type)}. ${escapeHtml(item.confidenceWhy||'')}</p></section><section class="drawer-section"><h3>Origen</h3><p>${escapeHtml(item.sourceName)} · pág. ${item.source?.page||'No especificada'} · ${escapeHtml(item.source?.section||'Sección no especificada')}</p></section><section class="drawer-section"><h3>Trazabilidad</h3><p>Fuente → hallazgo revisado → conocimiento publicado.</p></section>`;openDrawer(header,body);}
    const outcomeDefinitions={Positivo:'Mejora clara en una métrica relevante para la hipótesis.',Neutral:'No hay evidencia suficiente de mejora ni empeoramiento.',Negativo:'Se observa un empeoramiento relevante.',Mixto:'Las métricas, dispositivos, segmentos o KPIs no apuntan en la misma dirección.'};
    function experimentLineageMarkup(item){
      const lineage=UXKnowledge.experimentLineage(item),parts=[];
      if(lineage.lineId)parts.push(`<p><strong>Línea experimental:</strong> ${escapeHtml(lineage.lineId)}</p>`);
      if(lineage.iteration)parts.push(`<p><strong>Iteración:</strong> ${escapeHtml(lineage.iteration)}</p>`);
      if(lineage.previousExperimentIds.length)parts.push(`<p><strong>Parte de:</strong> ${escapeHtml(lineage.previousExperimentIds.join(', '))}</p>`);
      if(lineage.nextExperimentIds.length)parts.push(`<p><strong>Continúa en:</strong> ${escapeHtml(lineage.nextExperimentIds.join(', '))}</p>`);
      if(lineage.derivedFromLearningIds.length)parts.push(`<p><strong>Nace de:</strong> ${escapeHtml(lineage.derivedFromLearningIds.join(', '))}</p>`);
      if(lineage.multiChangeLimitation)parts.push(`<p><strong>Límite de atribución:</strong> ${escapeHtml(lineage.multiChangeLimitation)}</p>`);
      return parts.length?`<section class="drawer-section related-section"><h3>Genealogía experimental</h3>${parts.join('')}</section>`:'';
    }
    function openExperiment(id){const item=experiments.find(value=>value.id===id);if(!item)return;const relatedLearnings=item.learningIds.map(learningId=>learnings.find(value=>value.id===learningId)).filter(Boolean);const sourceName=`Header · Análisis test A/B ${item.device.toLowerCase()}`;const header=`<div class="drawer-kicker">Test A/B · ${item.device}</div><h2>${item.title}</h2><p class="drawer-lead">${item.result}</p><div class="drawer-tags"><span class="status-badge ${outcomeClass(item.outcome)}">Resultado ${item.outcome.toLowerCase()}</span></div><div class="drawer-context"><div><strong>Dónde aplica:</strong> ${pathOf(item)}</div><div><strong>Dispositivo:</strong> ${item.device} · ${item.duration}</div>${contextMetadata(item)}</div>`;const body=`${knowledgeStateDisclosure(item,{result:true})}${taxonomyLegacyDisclosure(item,'experiment')}<section class="drawer-section"><h3>Hipótesis</h3><p>${item.hypothesis}</p></section><section class="drawer-section"><h3>Resultado observado</h3><p>${item.result}</p><div class="metric-list">${item.metrics.map(metric=>`<div class="metric-row"><span>${metric.label}</span><strong>${metric.value}</strong></div>`).join('')}</div><div class="detail-note"><strong>Qué significa “${item.outcome}”:</strong> ${outcomeDefinitions[item.outcome]}</div></section><section class="drawer-section"><h3>Cómo interpretarlo</h3><p>${item.interpretation}</p></section><section class="drawer-section"><h3>Qué haríamos después</h3><p>${item.recommendation}</p></section><section class="drawer-section"><h3>Límites</h3>${list(item.limits)}</section><section class="drawer-section"><h3>Estado de implementación</h3><p>${item.pro}</p></section>${experimentLineageMarkup(item)}<section class="drawer-section related-section"><h3>Learnings relacionados</h3>${learningRelations(relatedLearnings)}</section><section class="drawer-section related-section"><h3>Fuentes</h3>${sourceReferences([sourceName],item)}</section>`;openDrawer(header,body);}
    function openPattern(id){const item=patternItems.find(value=>value.id===id);if(!item)return;const relatedLearnings=learnings.filter(learning=>item.proof.includes(learning.id)||(learning.component&&learning.product===item.product&&learning.screen===item.screen&&componentsOf(learning).some(component=>componentsOf(item).includes(component)))).slice(0,4);const relatedSources=[...new Set(relatedLearnings.flatMap(learning=>learning.sourceNames))].slice(0,6);const header=`<div class="drawer-kicker">Patrón · ${componentsOf(item).join(' · ')}</div><h2>${item.title}</h2><p class="drawer-lead">${item.description}</p><div class="drawer-tags"><span class="status-badge ${confidenceClass(item.confidence)}">Confianza ${item.confidence.toLowerCase()}</span></div><div class="drawer-context"><div><strong>Dónde aplica:</strong> ${pathOf(item)}</div><div><strong>Dispositivo:</strong> ${item.device}</div>${contextMetadata(item)}</div>`;const body=`${knowledgeStateDisclosure(item)}${taxonomyLegacyDisclosure(item,'pattern')}<section class="drawer-section"><h3>Qué es</h3><p>${item.description}</p></section><section class="drawer-section"><h3>Cómo aplicarlo</h3><p>${item.do}</p></section><section class="drawer-section"><h3>Qué evitar</h3><p>${item.avoid}</p></section><section class="drawer-section"><h3>En qué se basa</h3><p>${cleanProof(item.proof)}</p></section>${relatedLearnings.length?`<section class="drawer-section related-section"><h3>Learnings relacionados</h3>${learningRelations(relatedLearnings)}</section>`:''}${relatedSources.length?`<section class="drawer-section related-section"><h3>Fuentes</h3>${sourceReferences(relatedSources,item)}</section>`:''}`;openDrawer(header,body);}
    function documentLearningRelations(item){
      const linkDecisions=new Set(['Reforzar learning','Reforzar learning existente','Ampliar learning','Contradice learning','Crear nuevo learning']);
      const operations=(item.analysis?.publication?.operations||[]).filter(operation=>linkDecisions.has(operation.decision));
      const used=learnings.filter(learning=>
        catalogSources(learning).some(source=>source.id===item.id)||
        (learning.publicationHistory||[]).some(entry=>entry.documentId===item.id)||
        learning.documentId===item.id||operations.some(operation=>operation.learningId===learning.id)
      );
      const proposedIds=hasDocumentAnalysis(item)&&!isDocumentPublished(item)?new Set(buildReviewClusters(item.analysis).filter(insight=>linkDecisions.has(insight.decision)).map(insight=>insight.learningId)):new Set();
      return {used,proposed:learnings.filter(learning=>proposedIds.has(learning.id)&&!used.some(existing=>existing.id===learning.id))};
    }
    function insightRegisterSection(item){
      if(item.supportingDocument)return `<section class="drawer-section"><h3>Uso en UX Intel</h3><p>Este PDF se conserva como fuente de apoyo para consulta y trazabilidad. No genera conocimiento estructurado hasta que exista una síntesis revisada.</p></section>`;
      const register=item.insightRegister;
      if(!register)return `<section class="drawer-section"><h3>Síntesis UX</h3><p>Este documento todavía no tiene una síntesis UX estructurada. Añade un Excel revisado para incorporar conocimiento curado.</p><button class="relation-action" type="button" data-add-insight-register="${escapeHtml(item.id)}">Añadir Excel revisado</button></section>`;
      const canPrepare=item.analysis?.documentFirstV2?.mode==='active-new-ingest'||item.analysis?.documentFirstV2?.mode==='insight-register-primary';
      return `<section class="drawer-section"><h3>Síntesis UX</h3><p><strong>${escapeHtml(register.file?.name||'Excel revisado')}</strong> · ${register.rows?.length||0} filas curadas · ${register.reviewedByUx?'Revisado por UX':'Revisión UX no confirmada'}</p><p>Es la capa editorial del mismo documento; no crea otra fuente ni otra Knowledge Base.</p><div class="form-actions"><button class="relation-action" type="button" data-view-insight-register="${escapeHtml(item.id)}">Abrir Excel revisado</button><button class="relation-action" type="button" data-add-insight-register="${escapeHtml(item.id)}">Actualizar Excel revisado</button>${canPrepare?`<button class="action-button" type="button" data-analyze-insight-register="${escapeHtml(item.id)}">Analizar con síntesis UX</button>`:''}</div></section>`;
    }
    function processedDocumentResult(item){
      const related=documentKnowledgeRelations(item),publication=item.analysis?.publication||{},counts=publication.counts||{};
      const created=related.learnings.filter(learning=>learning.documentId===item.id&&learning.publicationOperationId);
      const updated=related.learnings.filter(learning=>(learning.publicationHistory||[]).some(entry=>entry.documentId===item.id));
      const recommendations=related.units.filter(unit=>['Recomendación','Recomendación de sistema'].includes(unit.type));
      const limits=item.analysis?.documentFirstV2?.documentLimits||item.analysis?.documentEditorial?.limits||item.analysis?.limits||[];
      const recommendationCount=recommendations.length||counts.recommendations||0,questionCount=related.questions.length||counts.questions||0;
      const learningLinks=(records,label)=>records.length?`<div class="document-result-list"><p><strong>${escapeHtml(label)}</strong></p>${records.map(learning=>`<p><button class="text-button" type="button" data-learning="${escapeHtml(learning.id)}">${escapeHtml(learning.title)}</button></p>`).join('')}</div>`:'';
      return `<section class="document-result-panel"><header><h3>Qué conocimiento aportó</h3><p>${escapeHtml(documentKnowledgeOverview(item).text)}</p></header><div class="document-result-stats"><div class="document-result-stat"><strong>${created.length||counts.newLearnings||0}</strong><span>Learnings creados</span></div><div class="document-result-stat"><strong>${updated.length||(counts.reinforced||0)+(counts.expanded||0)}</strong><span>Learnings reforzados o ampliados</span></div><div class="document-result-stat"><strong>${recommendationCount}</strong><span>Recomendaciones</span></div><div class="document-result-stat"><strong>${questionCount}</strong><span>Preguntas abiertas</span></div><div class="document-result-stat"><strong>${counts.context||0}</strong><span>Bloques de evidencia/contexto</span></div><div class="document-result-stat"><strong>${limits.length}</strong><span>Límites documentales</span></div></div>${learningLinks(created,'Learnings creados')}${learningLinks(updated,'Learnings reforzados o ampliados')}<div class="document-result-actions"><button class="action-button" type="button" data-source-knowledge="${escapeHtml(item.id)}">Ver conocimiento relacionado</button>${documentCanUpdateSource(item)?`<button class="relation-action" type="button" data-source-update="${escapeHtml(item.id)}">Actualizar fuente documental</button>`:''}</div></section>`;
    }
    function documentDrawerMeta(item,deviceScope='No especificado'){
      const location=documentApplicationPath(item)||'Pendiente de clasificar';
      return `<div class="document-drawer-meta">
        <div><span>Dónde aplica</span><strong>${escapeHtml(location)}</strong></div>
        <div><span>Dispositivo</span><strong>${escapeHtml(deviceScope||'No especificado')}</strong></div>
        <div><span>Fecha del documento</span><strong>${escapeHtml(documentDate(item))}</strong></div>
        <div><span>Ingesta</span><strong>${escapeHtml(formatDateEs(item.ingestedAt))}</strong></div>
        <div><span>Iniciativa</span><strong>${escapeHtml(item.initiative||'Sin clasificar')}</strong></div>
      </div>`;
    }
    function drawerFileFooter(item,kind){
      const downloadAttr=kind==='excel'?`data-download-insight-register="${escapeHtml(item.id)}"`:`data-doc-download-direct="${escapeHtml(item.id)}"`;
      const label=kind==='excel'?'Descargar Excel':'Descargar PDF';
      return `<div class="drawer-footer"><button class="secondary-button" type="button" data-close-editor>Cerrar</button><button class="action-button" type="button" ${downloadAttr}>${label}</button></div>`;
    }
    function openSource(id){
      const item=sourceItems.find(value=>value.id===id);if(!item)return;
      if(item.insightRegister){openInsightRegister(item);return;}
      item.documentProfile=UXInsightRegister.documentProfile(item,item.documentProfile||{});
      const related=documentLearningRelations(item),analysis=item.analysis,hasAnalysis=hasDocumentAnalysis(item);
      const interpretation=hasAnalysis?documentInterpretation(item):null,insights=hasAnalysis?reviewInsightsForDocument(item,interpretation):[];
      const deviceScope=analysis?.metadata?.devices?.join(', ')||'No especificado',primary=documentPrimaryAction(item),knowledge=documentKnowledgeOverview(item);

      if(item.supportingDocument){
        const filename=item.originalName||item.fileMeta?.name||'';
        const header=`<div class="drawer-kicker" data-document-detail="${escapeHtml(item.id)}">PDF de apoyo</div><h2>${escapeHtml(item.name)}</h2>${filename?`<p class="drawer-file-name">${escapeHtml(filename)}</p>`:''}${documentDrawerMeta(item,deviceScope)}`;
        const body=`<section class="drawer-section drawer-section-emphasis"><h3>Uso en UX Intel</h3><p>Este PDF se conserva como documentación de apoyo y fuente de consulta. No genera registros de conocimiento automáticamente.</p></section><section class="drawer-section"><h3>Archivo original</h3><p>${escapeHtml(filename||item.name)}</p></section>${drawerFileFooter(item,'pdf')}`;
        openDrawer(header,body);return;
      }

      const filename=item.originalName||item.fileMeta?.name||'';
      const header=`<div class="drawer-kicker" data-document-detail="${escapeHtml(item.id)}">${escapeHtml(item.type||'Documento')}</div><h2>${escapeHtml(item.name)}</h2>${filename?`<p class="drawer-file-name">${escapeHtml(filename)}</p>`:''}${documentDrawerMeta(item,deviceScope)}`;
      const analysisCopy=documentAnalysisRecoverable(item)?'Hay información histórica trazable disponible para recuperar este análisis.':!documentHydrationComplete?'Recuperando el análisis guardado…':item.status==='Analizando'?'El análisis está en curso. La revisión estará disponible cuando termine.':hasAnalysis?`${insights.length} insights · ${analysis.findings.length} registros originales · ${interpretation.units.length} unidades válidas · ${analysis.pages??'No disponible'} páginas.`:documentHistoricalUnavailable(item)?'Análisis histórico no disponible en esta copia. Se conservan las relaciones existentes. No se ha vuelto a analizar el archivo.':'Este documento no tiene un análisis guardado.';
      const sourceUpdateAction=documentCanUpdateSource(item)?`<button class="relation-action" type="button" data-source-update="${item.id}">Actualizar fuente documental</button>`:'';
      const action=hasAnalysis||primary?.action==='view'?`<div class="form-actions"><button class="relation-action" type="button" data-source-view="${item.id}">Ver ficha</button>${sourceUpdateAction}</div>`:primary?`<div class="form-actions"><button class="relation-action" type="button" ${primary.action==='knowledge'?'data-source-knowledge':'data-source-analyze'}="${item.id}">${primary.label}</button>${sourceUpdateAction}</div>`:sourceUpdateAction;
      const analysisSection=`<section class="drawer-section"><h3>Ficha del documento</h3><p>${escapeHtml(analysisCopy)}</p><p class="document-knowledge-note document-knowledge-${knowledge.kind}">${escapeHtml(knowledge.text)}</p>${isDocumentPublished(item)?`<p>Publicado · ${escapeHtml(formatDateEs(analysis.publication.publishedAt))}</p>`:''}${action}</section>`;
      const technicalBody=`<section class="drawer-section"><h3>Clasificación</h3><p>${escapeHtml(item.type)} · Iniciativa ${escapeHtml((item.initiatives||[]).join(' · ')||item.initiative||'sin clasificar')}</p></section>${taxonomyLegacyDisclosure(item,'document')}${documentSourceHistoryMarkup(item)}${analysisSection}${insightRegisterSection(item)}${documentSourceGenealogyMarkup(item)}${related.used.length?`<section class="drawer-section"><h3>Learnings que utilizan esta fuente</h3>${learningRelations(related.used)}</section>`:''}${related.proposed.length?`<section class="drawer-section"><h3>Relaciones propuestas</h3><p>Pendientes de consolidación; todavía no se han incorporado al conocimiento.</p>${learningRelations(related.proposed)}</section>`:''}${documentExtraRelations(item)}${item.fileMeta?drawerFileFooter(item,'pdf'):`<div class="drawer-footer"><button class="secondary-button" type="button" data-close-editor>Cerrar</button></div>`}`;
      const processed=isDocumentPublished(item)||['Procesado','Publicado'].includes(item.status)&&hasRelatedDocumentKnowledge(item);
      const body=processed?`${processedDocumentResult(item)}<details class="document-trace-disclosure"><summary>Ver ficha completa y trazabilidad</summary>${technicalBody}</details>`:technicalBody;
      openDrawer(header,body);
    }

    const panelNames={overview:'Resumen',explore:'Explorar conocimiento',learnings:'Learnings',evidence:'Evidencias',experiments:'Test A/B',patterns:'Patrones',sources:'Documentos',taxonomy:'Taxonomía y glosario',review:'Revisión del documento'};
    const mobileMenuButton=document.querySelector('.mobile-menu');
    function setMobileMenu(open,returnFocus=false){document.body.classList.toggle('menu-open',open);mobileMenuButton.setAttribute('aria-expanded',String(open));mobileMenuButton.setAttribute('aria-label',open?'Cerrar menú':'Abrir menú');if(open)document.querySelector('.nav-button.active')?.focus();else if(returnFocus)mobileMenuButton.focus();}
    function renderExplore(){
      try{
        globalThis.__uxSimpleExploreRefresh?.();
      }catch(error){
        console.error("Error al refrescar Explorar", error);
      }
    }

    function showPanel(id){const menuWasOpen=document.body.classList.contains('menu-open');document.querySelectorAll('.panel').forEach(panel=>panel.classList.toggle('active',panel.id===id));document.querySelectorAll('.nav-button').forEach(button=>{const current=button.dataset.panel===id;button.classList.toggle('active',current);if(current)button.setAttribute('aria-current','page');else button.removeAttribute('aria-current');});document.getElementById('breadcrumb-current').textContent=panelNames[id];setMobileMenu(false);if(id==='review')renderReview();if(id==='explore')renderExplore();if(menuWasOpen){const heading=document.getElementById(id)?.querySelector('h1');if(heading){heading.tabIndex=-1;heading.focus({preventScroll:true});}}window.scrollTo({top:0,behavior:'smooth'});}
    function activateReviewTab(key,moveFocus=false){
      const workspace=document.getElementById('review-workspace');if(!workspace)return;
      const tabs=[...workspace.querySelectorAll('[role="tab"]')],panes=[...workspace.querySelectorAll('[role="tabpanel"]')];
      const tab=tabs.find(item=>item.dataset.reviewTab===key),panel=panes.find(item=>item.dataset.reviewPane===key);if(!tab||!panel)return;
      tabs.forEach(item=>{const active=item.dataset.reviewTab===key;item.classList.toggle('active',active);item.setAttribute('aria-selected',String(active));item.tabIndex=active?0:-1;});
      panes.forEach(pane=>{const active=pane.dataset.reviewPane===key;pane.classList.toggle('active',active);pane.hidden=!active;pane.setAttribute('aria-hidden',String(!active));});
      if(moveFocus){tab.focus();tab.scrollIntoView({block:'nearest',inline:'nearest'});}
    }
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-panel]').forEach(button=>button.addEventListener('click',()=>showPanel(button.dataset.panel)));
    document.addEventListener('click',event=>{const reviewTab=event.target.closest?.('[data-review-tab]');if(!reviewTab)return;event.preventDefault();activateReviewTab(reviewTab.dataset.reviewTab,true);});
    document.addEventListener('keydown',event=>{const currentTab=event.target.closest?.('[role="tab"]');if(!currentTab)return;if(['ArrowLeft','ArrowRight','Home','End'].includes(event.key)){event.preventDefault();const tabs=[...currentTab.closest('[role="tablist"]').querySelectorAll('[role="tab"]')],index=tabs.indexOf(currentTab);const next=event.key==='Home'?tabs[0]:event.key==='End'?tabs[tabs.length-1]:event.key==='ArrowRight'?tabs[(index+1)%tabs.length]:tabs[(index-1+tabs.length)%tabs.length];activateReviewTab(next.dataset.reviewTab,true);}else if(['Enter',' '].includes(event.key)){event.preventDefault();activateReviewTab(currentTab.dataset.reviewTab,true);}});
    document.addEventListener('click',event=>{
      if(event.target.closest('[data-start-review]')){event.preventDefault();startPendingReview();return;}
      if(event.target.closest('[data-approve-all-findings],[data-approve-routine-clusters],[data-cluster-approve],[data-finding-approve],[data-finding-discard],[data-finding-edit],[data-insight-edit],[data-relation-approve]')&&!reviewCanEdit(activeReviewDocument()))return;
      const sourceDetail=event.target.closest('[data-open-source-detail]');if(sourceDetail)openSource(sourceDetail.dataset.openSourceDetail);
      const jump=event.target.closest('[data-panel-jump]');if(jump){showPanel(jump.dataset.panelJump);if(jump.dataset.resetFilters)resetFilterGroup(jump.dataset.resetFilters);}
      const product=event.target.closest('[data-product-jump]');if(product)goToKnowledgeContext({product:product.dataset.productJump});
      const contextLink=event.target.closest('[data-knowledge-context]');if(contextLink){const [product,screen,component]=JSON.parse(contextLink.dataset.knowledgeContext);goToKnowledgeContext({product,screen,component});}
      const fullLearning=event.target.closest('[data-open-full-learning]');if(fullLearning){openFullLearning(fullLearning.dataset.openFullLearning);return;}
      const learning=event.target.closest('[data-learning]');if(learning){openLearning(learning.dataset.learning);taxonomyAddDrawerAction('learning',learning.dataset.learning);}
      const detail=event.target.closest('[data-detail]');if(detail?.dataset.detail==='evidence'){openEvidence(detail.dataset.id);taxonomyAddDrawerAction('evidence',detail.dataset.id);}if(detail?.dataset.detail==='experiment'){openExperiment(detail.dataset.id);taxonomyAddDrawerAction('experiment',detail.dataset.id);}if(detail?.dataset.detail==='pattern'){openPattern(detail.dataset.id);taxonomyAddDrawerAction('pattern',detail.dataset.id);}
      const relatedSource=event.target.closest('[data-source-name]');if(relatedSource){const name=decodeURIComponent(relatedSource.dataset.sourceName);closeDrawer();showPanel('sources');const initiative=document.getElementById('source-initiative');initiative.value=normalizeText(name).includes('header')?'Header':normalizeText(name).includes('buscador')?'Buscador':'Todas';renderSources();updateActiveFilters('source');showToast('Documento localizado',name);}
      const relatedDownload=event.target.closest('[data-related-download]');if(relatedDownload)showToast('Descargar documento',decodeURIComponent(relatedDownload.dataset.relatedDownload));
      const directDownload=event.target.closest('[data-doc-download-direct]');if(directDownload){const item=sourceItems.find(value=>value.id===directDownload.dataset.docDownloadDirect);if(item)downloadDocument(item);}
      const addRegister=event.target.closest('[data-add-insight-register]');if(addRegister){insightRegisterTargetId=addRegister.dataset.addInsightRegister;insightRegisterUploadIntent='attach';insightRegisterInput.click();return;}
const viewRegister=event.target.closest('[data-view-insight-register]');if(viewRegister){const item=sourceItems.find(value=>value.id===viewRegister.dataset.viewInsightRegister);if(item)openInsightRegister(item);return;}
      const downloadRegister=event.target.closest('[data-download-insight-register]');if(downloadRegister){const item=sourceItems.find(value=>value.id===downloadRegister.dataset.downloadInsightRegister);if(item)downloadInsightRegister(item);return;}
      const analyzeRegister=event.target.closest('[data-analyze-insight-register]');if(analyzeRegister){const item=sourceItems.find(value=>value.id===analyzeRegister.dataset.analyzeInsightRegister);if(item)analyzeWithInsightRegister(item);return;}
      const sourceAnalyze=event.target.closest('[data-source-analyze]');
      if(sourceAnalyze){const item=sourceItems.find(record=>record.id===sourceAnalyze.dataset.sourceAnalyze);if(item&&!isDocumentPublished(item)&&documentHydrationComplete){uploadTargetId=item.id;uploadIntent='analyze';uploadInput.click();}}
      const sourceUpdate=event.target.closest('[data-source-update]');
      if(sourceUpdate){const item=sourceItems.find(record=>record.id===sourceUpdate.dataset.sourceUpdate);if(documentCanUpdateSource(item)){uploadTargetId=item.id;uploadIntent='link';uploadInput.click();}}
      const sourceUpdateChoice=event.target.closest('[data-source-update-choice]');
      if(sourceUpdateChoice&&pendingSourceUpdate?.file){const item=sourceItems.find(record=>record.id===sourceUpdateChoice.dataset.sourceUpdateChoice);if(item)openSourceUpdateConfirmation(pendingSourceUpdate.file,item);}
      if(event.target.closest('[data-cancel-source-update]')){pendingSourceUpdate=null;closeDrawer();}
      if(event.target.closest('[data-confirm-source-update]')&&pendingSourceUpdate?.file&&pendingSourceUpdate.documentId){const pending=pendingSourceUpdate;pendingSourceUpdate=null;closeDrawer();processDocumentFile(pending.file,pending.documentId,{linkExisting:true});}
      if(event.target.closest('[data-select-pilot-file]')){uploadTargetId=activeReviewDocument()?.id||PILOT_SOURCE_ID;uploadInput.click();}
      if(event.target.closest('[data-approve-routine-clusters]')){(async()=>{const documentItem=activeReviewDocument(),clusters=reviewInsightsForDocument(documentItem).filter(cluster=>!cluster.needsDecision&&!insightIsReviewed(cluster));let count=0;for(const cluster of clusters){if(!await setClusterReviewDecision(documentItem,cluster.id,cluster.decision,'approved'))break;count++;}renderReview();showToast('Propuestas rutinarias revisadas',`${count} insights confirmados sin publicar conocimiento.`);})();}
      const clusterApprove=event.target.closest('[data-cluster-approve]');if(clusterApprove){const documentItem=activeReviewDocument(),cluster=reviewInsightsForDocument(documentItem).find(item=>item.id===clusterApprove.dataset.clusterApprove);if(cluster)setClusterReviewDecision(documentItem,cluster.id,cluster.decision,'approved').then(saved=>{if(saved){renderReview();showToast('Decisión revisada','El insight queda revisado. No se ha publicado nada.');}});}


      const insightEdit=event.target.closest('[data-insight-edit]');if(insightEdit){event.preventDefault();openInsightEditor(insightEdit.dataset.insightEdit);}
      const relationApprove=event.target.closest('[data-relation-approve]');if(relationApprove){const documentItem=activeReviewDocument(),relation=documentItem?.analysis?.relations?.find(item=>item.id===relationApprove.dataset.relationApprove);if(relation){relation.status='approved';touchReview(documentItem);safeStorageWrite();renderReview();}}
      if(event.target.closest('[data-open-publish-preview]'))openPublishPreview();
      const previewExpand=event.target.closest('#publication-preview [data-preview-expand]');if(previewExpand)previewExpand.closest('#publication-preview').querySelectorAll('details').forEach(details=>{details.open=previewExpand.dataset.previewExpand==='true';});
      if(event.target.closest('[data-confirm-publish]'))publishReview();
      if(event.target.closest('[data-back-to-review]'))closeDrawer();
      if(event.target.closest('[data-view-published-knowledge]')){closeDrawer();showPanel('learnings');showToast('Conocimiento publicado','Puedes consultar los learnings, evidencias, preguntas y patterns incorporados.');}
      if(event.target.closest('[data-close-editor]'))closeDrawer();
      if(event.target.closest('.drawer-close'))closeDrawer();
      const menuButton=event.target.closest('[data-action-menu]');
      if(menuButton){event.stopPropagation();const menu=document.querySelector(`[data-menu="${menuButton.dataset.actionMenu}"]`);document.querySelectorAll('.action-menu').forEach(value=>value!==menu&&value.classList.remove('open'));menu.classList.toggle('open');}
      else if(!event.target.closest('.action-menu'))document.querySelectorAll('.action-menu').forEach(value=>value.classList.remove('open'));
    });
    const importWorkspaceModal=document.getElementById('import-workspace-modal');
    document.addEventListener('keydown',event=>{const target=event.target.closest?.('[data-learning],[data-detail]');if(target&&(event.key==='Enter'||event.key===' ')){event.preventDefault();if(target.dataset.learning){openLearning(target.dataset.learning);taxonomyAddDrawerAction('learning',target.dataset.learning);}else if(target.dataset.detail==='evidence'){openEvidence(target.dataset.id);taxonomyAddDrawerAction('evidence',target.dataset.id);}else if(target.dataset.detail==='pattern'){openPattern(target.dataset.id);taxonomyAddDrawerAction('pattern',target.dataset.id);}else{openExperiment(target.dataset.id);taxonomyAddDrawerAction('experiment',target.dataset.id);}}if(event.key==='Tab'){if(drawer.classList.contains('open'))keepFocusInside(drawer,event);else if(!importWorkspaceModal.hidden)keepFocusInside(importWorkspaceModal,event);else if(!deleteModal.hidden)keepFocusInside(deleteModal,event);else if(document.body.classList.contains('menu-open'))keepFocusInside(document.getElementById('primary-navigation'),event);}if(event.key==='Escape'){setMobileMenu(false,true);closeDrawer();closeSearch();closeImportWorkspaceModal();closeDelete();}});
    drawerBackdrop.addEventListener('click',closeDrawer);
    mobileMenuButton.addEventListener('click',()=>setMobileMenu(mobileMenuButton.getAttribute('aria-expanded')!=='true'));
    document.querySelector('.sidebar-backdrop').addEventListener('click',()=>setMobileMenu(false,true));

    const toast=document.getElementById('toast');
    function showToast(title,copy){toast.querySelector('strong').textContent=title;toast.querySelector('span').textContent=copy;toast.classList.add('show');clearTimeout(window.toastTimer);window.toastTimer=setTimeout(()=>toast.classList.remove('show'),3200);}
    function workspaceBackupFilename(date=new Date(),prefix='UX_Intelligence_workspace'){
      const pad=value=>String(value).padStart(2,'0');
      return `${prefix}_${date.getFullYear()}-${pad(date.getMonth()+1)}-${pad(date.getDate())}_${pad(date.getHours())}-${pad(date.getMinutes())}.json`;
    }
    function downloadWorkspaceBackup(state,filename,{notify=false}={}){
      try{
        const blob=new Blob([JSON.stringify(state,null,2)],{type:'application/json;charset=utf-8'});
        const url=URL.createObjectURL(blob);
        const link=document.createElement('a');
        link.href=url;
        link.download=filename;
        link.click();
        setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),1000);
        if(notify)showToast('Copia de seguridad creada','Se ha exportado el conocimiento y el estado de UX Intelligence. Los archivos PDF y Excel físicos no están incluidos.');
        return true;
      }catch(error){
        console.error('No se pudo exportar el workspace',error);
        if(notify)showToast('No se ha podido exportar','No se ha podido crear la copia de seguridad del workspace.');
        return false;
      }
    }
    function exportWorkspaceBackup(){
      const historyChanged=normalizeLearningHistoryState();
      if(historyChanged)safeStorageWrite({project:false});
      const backupState={...buildWorkspaceState(),exportedAt:new Date().toISOString()};
      downloadWorkspaceBackup(backupState,workspaceBackupFilename(),{notify:true});
    }
    function workspaceStructuredRecordCount(documents){
      try{
        const records=globalThis.UXSimpleKnowledge?.allRecords?.(documents);
        if(Array.isArray(records))return records.length;
      }catch(error){console.warn('[UX Intel backup] No se pudo contar con UXSimpleKnowledge',error);}
      return (documents||[]).reduce((total,item)=>total+(item?.simpleKnowledgeActive&&Array.isArray(item?.insightRegister?.rows)?item.insightRegister.rows.length:0),0);
    }
    function validateWorkspaceBackup(state){
      if(!state||typeof state!=='object'||Array.isArray(state))throw new Error('El archivo no contiene un workspace válido.');
      const version=Number(state.workspaceSchemaVersion);
      if(!Number.isFinite(version))throw new Error('El archivo no indica una versión de workspace válida.');
      if(version!==WORKSPACE_SCHEMA_VERSION)throw new Error(`Este backup usa el esquema ${version}. UX Intel necesita el esquema ${WORKSPACE_SCHEMA_VERSION}.`);
      ['documents','knowledgeUnits','openQuestionItems','learningState','patternState'].forEach(key=>{if(!Array.isArray(state[key]))throw new Error(`El backup no contiene ${key} en el formato esperado.`);});
      return state;
    }
    function replaceWorkspaceState(state){
      const clone=value=>JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(value||[]));
      sourceItems.splice(0,sourceItems.length,...clone(state.documents));
      knowledgeUnits.splice(0,knowledgeUnits.length,...clone(state.knowledgeUnits));
      openQuestionItems.splice(0,openQuestionItems.length,...clone(state.openQuestionItems));
      learnings.splice(0,learnings.length,...clone(state.learningState));
      patternItems.splice(0,patternItems.length,...clone(state.patternState));
      learnings.forEach(item=>{
        if(!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(item,'createdAt'))item.createdAt='';
        if(!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(item,'updatedAt'))item.updatedAt='';
      });
      normalizeLearningHistoryState();
      activeReviewDocumentId=null;
    }
    function refreshWorkspaceAfterImport(){
      refreshCatalogMetadata();
      refreshDocumentDateFilter();
      refreshAllTaxonomyFilters?.();
      optionSet(document.getElementById('learning-market'),'Todos',[...new Set([...learnings.map(item=>item.market),...sourceItems.flatMap(item=>(item.insightRegister?.rows||[]).map(row=>row.market))].filter(Boolean))]);
      optionSet(document.getElementById('evidence-kind'),'Todos',[...new Set(evidenceItems.map(item=>item.kind))]);
      setDocumentAnalysisTypeFilter();
      refreshInitiativeFilters();
      renderOverview();renderLearnings();renderEvidence();renderExperiments();renderPatterns();renderSources();renderReview();renderExplore();renderTaxonomy();
    }
    let pendingWorkspaceImport=null,importWorkspaceReturnFocus=null;
    function closeImportWorkspaceModal(){
      if(importWorkspaceModal.hidden)return;
      importWorkspaceModal.hidden=true;
      pendingWorkspaceImport=null;
      if(importWorkspaceReturnFocus?.isConnected)importWorkspaceReturnFocus.focus();
      importWorkspaceReturnFocus=null;
    }
    function workspaceComparable(value,{ignoreTimestamps=true}={}){
      const ignored=ignoreTimestamps?new Set(['createdAt','updatedAt','attachedAt','ingestedAt','exportedAt']):new Set();
      const normalize=input=>{
        if(Array.isArray(input))return input.map(normalize);
        if(input&&typeof input==='object'){
          const output={};
          Object.keys(input).sort().forEach(key=>{if(!ignored.has(key))output[key]=normalize(input[key]);});
          return output;
        }
        return input??null;
      };
      return JSON.stringify(normalize(value));
    }
    function workspaceDocumentComparable(item){
      const normalized={...(item||{})};
      normalized.funnel=String(item?.funnel||'');
      normalized.minifunnel=String(item?.minifunnel||'');
      normalized.documentScopeMode=String(item?.documentScopeMode||'');
      normalized.components=Array.isArray(item?.components)?item.components:[];
      normalized.initiatives=Array.isArray(item?.initiatives)?item.initiatives:[];
      normalized.zone=String(item?.zone||'');
      normalized.scope=String(item?.scope||'');
      normalized.market=String(item?.market||'');
      return workspaceComparable(normalized);
    }
    function workspaceEntityDiff(currentItems,nextItems,comparable=item=>workspaceComparable(item)){
      const currentMap=new Map((currentItems||[]).map(item=>[String(item?.id||''),item]).filter(([id])=>id));
      const nextMap=new Map((nextItems||[]).map(item=>[String(item?.id||''),item]).filter(([id])=>id));
      const added=[...nextMap.keys()].filter(id=>!currentMap.has(id));
      const removed=[...currentMap.keys()].filter(id=>!nextMap.has(id));
      const modified=[...currentMap.keys()].filter(id=>nextMap.has(id)&&comparable(currentMap.get(id))!==comparable(nextMap.get(id)));
      return {added,removed,modified,currentMap,nextMap};
    }
    function workspaceRecordMap(documents){
      const map=new Map();
      (documents||[]).forEach(documentItem=>{
        (documentItem?.insightRegister?.rows||[]).forEach((row,index)=>{
          const rowId=String(row?.id||row?.sourceRow||index+1);
          map.set(`${documentItem.id}::${rowId}`,row);
        });
      });
      return map;
    }
    function workspaceRecordDiff(currentDocuments,nextDocuments){
      const currentMap=workspaceRecordMap(currentDocuments),nextMap=workspaceRecordMap(nextDocuments);
      const added=[...nextMap.keys()].filter(id=>!currentMap.has(id));
      const removed=[...currentMap.keys()].filter(id=>!nextMap.has(id));
      const modified=[...currentMap.keys()].filter(id=>nextMap.has(id)&&workspaceComparable(currentMap.get(id))!==workspaceComparable(nextMap.get(id)));
      return {added,removed,modified,currentMap,nextMap};
    }
    function workspaceInitiativeMembership(state){
      const membership=new Map(),add=(name,member)=>{
        const value=String(name||'').trim();if(!value||normalizeText(value)==='sin clasificar'||normalizeText(value)==='documentacion')return;
        if(!membership.has(value))membership.set(value,new Set());
        membership.get(value).add(member);
      };
      (state?.documents||[]).forEach(item=>{
        const names=[item?.initiative,...(item?.initiatives||[])];
        [...new Set(names.filter(Boolean))].forEach(name=>add(name,`D:${item.id}`));
      });
      (state?.learningState||[]).forEach(item=>{
        const names=[item?.initiative,...(item?.initiatives||[])];
        [...new Set(names.filter(Boolean))].forEach(name=>add(name,`L:${item.id}`));
      });
      return membership;
    }
    function inferWorkspaceInitiativeRenames(currentState,nextState){
      const current=workspaceInitiativeMembership(currentState),next=workspaceInitiativeMembership(nextState);
      const removed=[...current.keys()].filter(name=>![...next.keys()].some(candidate=>normalizeText(candidate)===normalizeText(name)));
      const added=[...next.keys()].filter(name=>![...current.keys()].some(candidate=>normalizeText(candidate)===normalizeText(name)));
      const signature=set=>[...set].sort().join('|'),renames=[];
      removed.forEach(from=>{
        const match=added.find(to=>signature(current.get(from))&&signature(current.get(from))===signature(next.get(to))&&!renames.some(item=>item.to===to));
        if(match)renames.push({from,to:match});
      });
      return renames;
    }
    function workspaceDocumentScope(item){
      if(!item)return 'Sin clasificar';
      if(normalizeText(item.scope)==='transversal'||item.documentScopeMode==='transversal')return [item.product,'Transversal'].filter(Boolean).join(' › ');
      const raw=[item.product,item.funnel,item.minifunnel,item.component].map(value=>String(value||'').trim()).filter(Boolean),parts=[];
      raw.forEach(value=>{if(!parts.length||normalizeText(parts[parts.length-1])!==normalizeText(value))parts.push(value);});
      return parts.join(' › ')||'Sin clasificar';
    }
    function workspaceClassificationChanges(documentDiff){
      const keys=['initiative','initiatives','product','funnel','minifunnel','screen','component','element','scope','documentScopeMode'];
      return documentDiff.modified.map(id=>{
        const current=documentDiff.currentMap.get(id),next=documentDiff.nextMap.get(id);
        const changed=keys.some(key=>workspaceComparable(current?.[key])!==workspaceComparable(next?.[key]));
        if(!changed)return null;
        const currentInitiative=String(current?.initiative||(current?.initiatives||[]).join(' · ')||'Sin iniciativa');
        const nextInitiative=String(next?.initiative||(next?.initiatives||[]).join(' · ')||'Sin iniciativa');
        const initiativeChanged=normalizeText(currentInitiative)!==normalizeText(nextInitiative);
        const currentScope=workspaceDocumentScope(current),nextScope=workspaceDocumentScope(next);
        const scopeChanged=normalizeText(currentScope)!==normalizeText(nextScope);
        return {id,name:next?.name||current?.name||id,currentInitiative,nextInitiative,initiativeChanged,currentScope,nextScope,scopeChanged};
      }).filter(Boolean);
    }
    function workspaceImportDiff(currentState,nextState){
      const documents=workspaceEntityDiff(currentState.documents,nextState.documents,workspaceDocumentComparable);
      const records=workspaceRecordDiff(currentState.documents,nextState.documents);
      const learnings=workspaceEntityDiff(currentState.learningState,nextState.learningState);
      const renames=inferWorkspaceInitiativeRenames(currentState,nextState);
      const classifications=workspaceClassificationChanges(documents);
      return {documents,records,learnings,renames,classifications};
    }
    function workspaceDiffCountMarkup(label,diff){
      return `<div class="workspace-import-count"><strong>${escapeHtml(label)}</strong><span>+${diff.added.length} añadidos · −${diff.removed.length} eliminados · ${diff.modified.length} modificados</span></div>`;
    }
    function renderWorkspaceImportDiff(currentState,nextState){
      const diff=workspaceImportDiff(currentState,nextState),container=document.getElementById('import-workspace-diff');
      if(!container)return diff;
      const totalChanges=['documents','records','learnings'].reduce((total,key)=>total+diff[key].added.length+diff[key].removed.length+diff[key].modified.length,0);
      const highlights=[];
      diff.renames.forEach(item=>highlights.push(`<p class="workspace-import-change"><strong>Iniciativa:</strong> ${escapeHtml(item.from)} → ${escapeHtml(item.to)}</p>`));
      diff.classifications.slice(0,8).forEach(item=>{
        const changes=[];
        if(item.initiativeChanged)changes.push(`iniciativa ${escapeHtml(item.currentInitiative)} → ${escapeHtml(item.nextInitiative)}`);
        if(item.scopeChanged)changes.push(`dónde aplica ${escapeHtml(item.currentScope)} → ${escapeHtml(item.nextScope)}`);
        if(changes.length)highlights.push(`<p class="workspace-import-change"><strong>${escapeHtml(item.name)}:</strong> ${changes.join(' · ')}</p>`);
      });
      if(diff.classifications.length>8)highlights.push(`<p class="workspace-import-change">Y ${diff.classifications.length-8} cambios de clasificación adicionales.</p>`);
      container.innerHTML=`<div class="workspace-import-counts">${workspaceDiffCountMarkup('Documentos',diff.documents)}${workspaceDiffCountMarkup('Registros',diff.records)}${workspaceDiffCountMarkup('Learnings',diff.learnings)}</div>${totalChanges===0?'<p class="workspace-import-equivalent">El backup es equivalente al estado actual: no se han detectado cambios de contenido.</p>':`<div class="workspace-import-section"><h3>Cambios destacados al importar</h3>${highlights.length?highlights.join(''):'<p class="workspace-import-equivalent">Hay cambios de contenido, pero no afectan a iniciativa o clasificación de documento.</p>'}</div>`}`;
      return diff;
    }
    function openWorkspaceImportConfirmation(state,fileName){
      const currentState=buildWorkspaceState();
      const currentDocs=currentState.documents.length,currentRecords=workspaceStructuredRecordCount(currentState.documents),currentLearnings=currentState.learningState.length;
      const nextDocs=state.documents.length,nextRecords=workspaceStructuredRecordCount(state.documents),nextLearnings=state.learningState.length;
      pendingWorkspaceImport={state,fileName,currentState};
      importWorkspaceReturnFocus=document.activeElement;
      document.getElementById('import-workspace-copy').textContent=`Estado actual: ${currentDocs} documentos · ${currentRecords} registros · ${currentLearnings} learnings. Backup: ${nextDocs} documentos · ${nextRecords} registros · ${nextLearnings} learnings. Revisa debajo qué cambiaría realmente. Antes de importar se descargará automáticamente una copia del estado actual. Los PDF y Excel guardados en este navegador no se borrarán.`;
      renderWorkspaceImportDiff(currentState,state);
      importWorkspaceModal.hidden=false;
      document.getElementById('import-workspace-cancel').focus();
    }
    async function confirmWorkspaceImport(){
      if(!pendingWorkspaceImport)return;
      const {state,currentState}=pendingWorkspaceImport;
      const beforeImportBackup={...currentState,exportedAt:new Date().toISOString()};
      const exported=downloadWorkspaceBackup(beforeImportBackup,workspaceBackupFilename(new Date(),'UX_Intelligence_backup_before_import'));
      if(!exported){showToast('Importación cancelada','No se ha podido crear la copia automática previa. No se ha sustituido el workspace.');return;}
      replaceWorkspaceState(state);
      if(!safeStorageWrite({project:false})){
        replaceWorkspaceState(currentState);
        safeStorageWrite({project:false});
        closeImportWorkspaceModal();
        refreshWorkspaceAfterImport();
        showToast('Importación cancelada','El backup no se ha podido guardar. Se ha mantenido el estado anterior.');
        return;
      }
      if(typeof queueProjectHistory==='function'){
        try{
          const historySaved=await queueProjectHistory();
          if(!historySaved)throw new Error('El histórico del proyecto no ha confirmado el guardado.');
        }catch(error){
          console.error('No se pudo actualizar el histórico tras importar el workspace',error);
          replaceWorkspaceState(currentState);
          safeStorageWrite({project:false});
          closeImportWorkspaceModal();
          closeDrawer();
          refreshWorkspaceAfterImport();
          showToast('Importación cancelada','El histórico del proyecto no se ha podido actualizar. Se ha restaurado el estado anterior y conservas la copia automática descargada.');
          return;
        }
      }
      closeImportWorkspaceModal();
      closeDrawer();
      refreshWorkspaceAfterImport();
      showToast('Backup importado','El workspace se ha restaurado. Los archivos físicos existentes se han conservado y seguirán vinculados cuando coincida su identificador.');
    }
    async function readWorkspaceImportFile(file){
      try{
        const state=validateWorkspaceBackup(JSON.parse(await file.text()));
        openWorkspaceImportConfirmation(state,file.name||'backup.json');
      }catch(error){
        console.error('No se pudo leer el backup del workspace',error);
        showToast('Backup no válido',String(error?.message||'No se puede importar este archivo.'));
      }
    }
    document.getElementById('export-workspace')?.addEventListener('click',exportWorkspaceBackup);
    const importWorkspaceInput=document.getElementById('import-workspace-input');
    document.getElementById('import-workspace')?.addEventListener('click',()=>importWorkspaceInput.click());
    importWorkspaceInput?.addEventListener('change',()=>{const file=importWorkspaceInput.files?.[0];importWorkspaceInput.value='';if(file)readWorkspaceImportFile(file);});

    document.getElementById('import-workspace-close')?.addEventListener('click',closeImportWorkspaceModal);
    document.getElementById('import-workspace-confirm')?.addEventListener('click',confirmWorkspaceImport);

    const insightRegisterInput=document.getElementById('insight-register-upload');

    async function storeNewDocumentSource(file){
      const fileName=String(file?.name||'documento'),item={id:`UP-${Date.now()}`,name:canonicalDocumentTitle(fileName.replace(/\.[^.]+$/,'')),product:'',screen:'',component:'',element:'',initiative:'Sin clasificar',type:'Sin clasificar',exactDate:'No especificada',documentDate:null,ingestedAt:new Date().toISOString(),status:'Pendiente de Insight Register',simpleKnowledgeActive:false,originalName:fileName,fileMeta:{name:fileName,size:file?.size||0,type:file?.type||'',lastModified:file?.lastModified||0}};
      try{await saveDocumentBlob(item.id,file);sourceItems.unshift(item);if(!safeStorageWrite({project:false}))throw new Error('No se ha podido guardar el documento.');renderSources();renderOverview();showPanel('sources');openSource(item.id);showToast('Documento guardado','La fuente original está preservada. Añade su Excel revisado para incorporar conocimiento a la base activa.');}
      catch(error){const index=sourceItems.findIndex(value=>value.id===item.id);if(index>=0)sourceItems.splice(index,1);await deleteDocumentBlob(item.id).catch(()=>{});console.error('[UX Intel source ingest]',error);showToast('No se ha guardado el documento',String(error?.message||error));}
    }
    async function storeSupportingDocument(file){
      const fileName=String(file?.name||'documentacion.pdf');
      if(!fileName.toLowerCase().endsWith('.pdf')){showToast('Formato no compatible','La documentación adicional debe ser un PDF.');return;}
      const item={id:`PDF-${Date.now()}`,name:canonicalDocumentTitle(fileName.replace(/\.[^.]+$/,'')),product:'',screen:'',component:'',element:'',initiative:'Documentación',initiatives:[],type:'Documentación adicional',exactDate:'No especificada',documentDate:null,ingestedAt:new Date().toISOString(),status:'Disponible',supportingDocument:true,simpleKnowledgeActive:false,originalName:fileName,fileMeta:{name:fileName,size:file?.size||0,type:file?.type||'application/pdf',lastModified:file?.lastModified||0}};
      try{
        await saveDocumentBlob(item.id,file);sourceItems.unshift(item);
        if(!safeStorageWrite({project:false}))throw new Error('No se ha podido guardar el PDF.');
        if(projectHistory.ready&&!projectHistory.error&&documentHydrationComplete)await queueProjectHistory();
        renderSources();renderOverview();showPanel(pdfReturnPanel||'sources');openSource(item.id);
        showToast('PDF añadido','Se conserva como documentación de apoyo. No genera conocimiento automáticamente.');
      }catch(error){const index=sourceItems.findIndex(value=>value.id===item.id);if(index>=0)sourceItems.splice(index,1);await deleteDocumentBlob(item.id).catch(()=>{});safeStorageWrite({project:false});console.error('[UX Intel supporting PDF]',error);showToast('No se ha guardado el PDF',String(error?.message||error));}
    }
    const LEARNING_SUGGESTION_VERSION='2026-09-21.2';
    function learningSuggestionHash(value){
      let hash=2166136261;
      const text=String(value||'');
      for(let index=0;index<text.length;index++){hash^=text.charCodeAt(index);hash=Math.imul(hash,16777619);}
      return (hash>>>0).toString(36);
    }
    function learningSuggestionRowText(row){
      return [row?.insight,row?.evidence,row?.uxImplication,row?.recommendation,row?.openQuestion,row?.uxLine,row?.component,row?.element,row?.screen,row?.minifunnel].filter(Boolean).join(' · ');
    }
    function learningSuggestionRowSignature(row,index){
      return [row?.sourceRow||index+1,row?.insight,row?.evidence,row?.uxImplication,row?.recommendation,row?.recordType,row?.product,row?.funnel,row?.minifunnel,row?.screen,row?.component,row?.element,row?.initiative,row?.impactUx].map(value=>String(value||'').trim()).join('|');
    }
    function learningSuggestionSourceRows(entries){return entries.map(entry=>entry.row?.sourceRow||entry.index+1).sort((a,b)=>Number(a)-Number(b));}
    function learningSuggestionEvidence(entries,item){
      const origin=String(entries[0]?.row?.origin||'').trim()||documentAnalysisType(item)||sourceDisplayType(item);
      return entries.slice(0,5).map(entry=>`${origin}: ${String(entry.row?.insight||entry.row?.evidence||entry.row?.recommendation||'Hallazgo revisado').trim()}`);
    }
    function learningSuggestionImpact(entries){
      const values=entries.map(entry=>String(entry.row?.impactUx||'').trim());
      return values.includes('Alto')?'Alto':values.includes('Medio')?'Medio':values.includes('Bajo')?'Bajo':'Medio';
    }
    function learningSuggestionLearningText(learning){
      return [learning?.title,learning?.know,learning?.text,...(learning?.applications||[]),...(learning?.components||[]),learning?.component,learning?.screen,learning?.element].filter(Boolean).join(' ');
    }
    function learningSuggestionInitiativeMatches(learning,item,row){
      const rowInitiative=String(row?.initiative||item?.initiative||'').trim();
      if(!rowInitiative)return false;
      return [learning?.initiative,...(learning?.initiatives||[])].some(value=>initiativeNameEquals(value,rowInitiative));
    }
    function learningSuggestionSpecificAnchorScore(row,learning){
      const anchor=String(learning?.element||'').trim();
      if(!anchor)return 1;
      return semanticSimilarity(
        [row?.insight,row?.component,row?.element,row?.uxLine].filter(Boolean).join(' '),
        [learning?.title,anchor].filter(Boolean).join(' ')
      );
    }
    function learningSuggestionTitleScore(row,learning){
      return semanticSimilarity(String(row?.insight||''),String(learning?.title||''));
    }
    function learningSuggestionScore(entry,learning,item){
      const row=entry.row,text=learningSuggestionRowText(row),learningText=learningSuggestionLearningText(learning);
      const titleScore=learningSuggestionTitleScore(row,learning);
      let score=semanticSimilarity(text,learningText)+Math.min(.18,titleScore*.28);
      if(learningSuggestionInitiativeMatches(learning,item,row))score+=.04;
      if(row?.product&&learning?.product&&normalizeText(row.product)===normalizeText(learning.product))score+=.02;
      if(row?.screen&&learning?.screen&&normalizeText(row.screen)===normalizeText(learning.screen))score+=.01;
      const rowComponent=normalizeText(row?.component||''),learningComponent=normalizeText(learning?.component||'');
      if(rowComponent&&learningComponent&&rowComponent===learningComponent)score+=.04;
      const rowContext=[row?.minifunnel,row?.component,row?.element,row?.uxLine].filter(Boolean).join(' ');
      score+=Math.min(.04,semanticSimilarity(rowContext,learningText)*.16);

      // Los learnings ligados a un elemento concreto (calendario, CTA, última búsqueda...)
      // necesitan evidencia temática real; compartir Home/Buscador no basta.
      const anchorScore=learningSuggestionSpecificAnchorScore(row,learning);
      if(learning?.element&&anchorScore<.16)score=Math.min(score,.29);

      // Lo mismo para componentes específicos: "Accesos rápidos" no debe absorber
      // registros genéricos de Buscador o Above the fold.
      const genericComponents=new Set(['','buscador','home','multipantalla','transversal']);
      if(learningComponent&&!genericComponents.has(learningComponent)){
        const componentScore=semanticSimilarity(String(row?.component||''),String(learning?.component||''));
        if(componentScore<.35&&titleScore<.35)score=Math.min(score,.29);
      }
      return Math.max(0,Math.min(1,score));
    }
    function learningSuggestionIsContradiction(row){
      return explicitContradictionSignal([learningSuggestionRowText(row),row?.observations,row?.recordType].filter(Boolean).join(' · '));
    }
    function learningSuggestionServiceTheme(entry){
      const text=normalizeText(learningSuggestionRowText(entry.row));
      const context=normalizeText([entry.row?.minifunnel,entry.row?.screen,entry.row?.component].filter(Boolean).join(' '));
      if(/asiento|equipaje|priority|fast track|sala vip/.test(context))return false;
      return /reduccion de esfuerzo|reducir esfuerzo|prerrellen|autocomplet|evita volver a introducir|evitar repetir|tareas repet|conveniencia|reconocimiento|personalizacion|capa de servicio|informacion ya conocida/.test(text);
    }
    function learningSuggestionGovernanceTheme(entry){
      const row=entry.row||{};
      const text=normalizeText([row.insight,row.uxImplication,row.uxLine,row.component].filter(Boolean).join(' '));
      const recordType=normalizeText(row.recordType||'');
      if(recordType==='restriccion')return true;
      return /fuente de verdad|gobernanza|dependencia tecnica|restriccion tecnica|amadeus|pss|contenido desactualizado|reglas.*sistema|responsable.*regla/.test(text);
    }
    function learningSuggestionCandidateId(targetKey,entries){return `LS-${learningSuggestionHash(`${targetKey}|${learningSuggestionSourceRows(entries).join(',')}`)}`;}
