    function learningSuggestionExistingAction(entries,learning,scores){
      if(entries.some(entry=>learningSuggestionIsContradiction(entry.row)))return 'Contradice learning';
      const contextText=learningSuggestionLearningText(learning);
      const expanded=entries.filter(entry=>semanticSimilarity([entry.row?.component,entry.row?.screen,entry.row?.uxLine].filter(Boolean).join(' '),contextText)<.10).length;
      const average=scores.reduce((sum,value)=>sum+value,0)/Math.max(1,scores.length);
      return expanded/Math.max(1,entries.length)>.45||average<.36?'Ampliar learning':'Reforzar learning existente';
    }
    function learningSuggestionNewTitle(item,theme,entries){
      if(theme==='service-effort'){
        const initiative=String(item?.initiative||entries[0]?.row?.initiative||'').trim();
        if(/suma|frequent flyer/i.test(initiative))return 'Si ya sabemos quién es el usuario, no deberíamos pedirle lo mismo otra vez';
        return 'Si ya tenemos información del usuario, debemos usarla para evitar pasos repetidos';
      }
      const representative=String(entries[0]?.row?.insight||entries[0]?.row?.uxImplication||'Nuevo aprendizaje a consolidar').trim();
      return representative.length>120?`${representative.slice(0,117).trim()}…`:representative;
    }
    function learningSuggestionNewSummary(theme,entries){
      if(theme==='service-effort')return 'Cuando el sistema ya dispone de datos del usuario, debemos aprovecharlos para evitar repeticiones y aplicar las ventajas disponibles.';
      return entries.slice(0,3).map(entry=>String(entry.row?.uxImplication||entry.row?.insight||'').trim()).filter(Boolean).join(' ');
    }
    function learningSuggestionGenericClusters(entries){
      const clusters=[];
      entries.forEach(entry=>{
        let best=null,bestScore=0;
        clusters.forEach(cluster=>{
          const score=Math.max(...cluster.entries.map(other=>semanticSimilarity(learningSuggestionRowText(entry.row),learningSuggestionRowText(other.row))));
          if(score>bestScore){bestScore=score;best=cluster;}
        });
        if(best&&bestScore>=.24)best.entries.push(entry);else clusters.push({entries:[entry]});
      });
      return clusters;
    }
    function sameLearningSuggestionRows(left,right){
      const a=[...(left||[])].map(String).sort(),b=[...(right||[])].map(String).sort();
      return a.length===b.length&&a.every((value,index)=>value===b[index]);
    }
    function learningSuggestionResolvedRowRefs(item,state=null){
      const resolved=new Set(),register=item?.insightRegister||{},current=state||register.learningSuggestions||{};
      const addRefs=entry=>{
        if(!entry||!entry.status||entry.status==='pending')return;
        (entry.rowRefs||[]).forEach(value=>resolved.add(String(value)));
      };
      (current.history||[]).forEach(addRefs);
      (current.candidates||[]).forEach(addRefs);
      learningDecisionLogRead().forEach(entry=>{
        if(String(entry?.documentId||'')===String(item?.id||'')&&entry?.status&&entry.status!=='pending'){
          (entry.rowRefs||[]).forEach(value=>resolved.add(String(value)));
        }
      });
      try{
        learnings.forEach(learning=>{
          (learning.publicationHistory||[]).forEach(entry=>{
            if(String(entry?.documentId||'')===String(item?.id||'')){
              (entry.rowRefs||[]).forEach(value=>resolved.add(String(value)));
            }
          });
        });
      }catch(_){}
      return resolved;
    }

    function restoreLearningSuggestionCandidate(candidate,previousCandidates,item){
      const previous=(previousCandidates||[]).find(entry=>entry.fingerprint===candidate.fingerprint)
        ||(previousCandidates||[]).find(entry=>sameLearningSuggestionRows(entry.rowRefs,candidate.rowRefs)&&(entry.edited||(entry.status&&entry.status!=='pending')));
      if(previous){
        const restored={...candidate,...previous,fingerprint:candidate.fingerprint,rowRefs:candidate.rowRefs};
        if(previous.status&&previous.status!=='pending')return restored;
        if(previous.edited)return restored;
      }
      const rowsKey=learningDecisionRowsKey(candidate.rowRefs);
      const remembered=[...learningDecisionLogRead()].reverse().find(entry=>
        String(entry.documentId||'')===String(item?.id||'')&&
        ((rowsKey&&entry.rowsKey===rowsKey)||(candidate.fingerprint&&entry.fingerprint===candidate.fingerprint))
      );
      if(remembered&&remembered.status&&remembered.status!=='pending'){
        return {
          ...candidate,
          status:remembered.status,
          action:remembered.action||candidate.action,
          targetLearningId:remembered.learningId||candidate.targetLearningId,
          appliedLearningId:remembered.learningId||candidate.appliedLearningId,
          title:remembered.title||candidate.title,
          know:remembered.know||candidate.know,
          text:remembered.text||candidate.text,
          edited:Boolean(remembered.edited||candidate.edited),
          resolvedAt:remembered.savedAt||candidate.resolvedAt||''
        };
      }
      try{
        for(const learning of learnings){
          const publication=(learning.publicationHistory||[]).find(entry=>
            String(entry?.documentId||'')===String(item?.id||'')&&
            sameLearningSuggestionRows(entry?.rowRefs,candidate.rowRefs)
          );
          if(publication){
            return {
              ...candidate,
              status:'confirmed',
              action:publication.decision||candidate.action,
              targetLearningId:learning.id,
              appliedLearningId:learning.id,
              resolvedAt:publication.publishedAt||candidate.resolvedAt||''
            };
          }
        }
      }catch(_){}
      return candidate;
    }
    function buildLearningSuggestions(item,previousState=null){
      const register=item?.insightRegister;if(!register?.rows?.length)return null;
      const allEntries=register.rows.map((row,index)=>({row,index}));
      const rowFingerprint=learningSuggestionHash(allEntries.map(entry=>learningSuggestionRowSignature(entry.row,entry.index)).join('\n'));
      const previousCandidates=previousState?.candidates||register.learningSuggestions?.candidates||[];
      const resolvedRows=learningSuggestionResolvedRowRefs(item,previousState||register.learningSuggestions);
      const entries=allEntries.filter(entry=>!resolvedRows.has(String(entry.row?.sourceRow||entry.index+1)));
      const resolvedCandidates=(previousCandidates||[]).filter(candidate=>candidate?.status&&candidate.status!=='pending');
      const historicalResolved=[];
      (previousState?.history||register.learningSuggestions?.history||[]).forEach(historyEntry=>{
        if(!historyEntry?.status||historyEntry.status==='pending')return;
        const alreadyPresent=resolvedCandidates.some(candidate=>
          sameLearningSuggestionRows(candidate?.rowRefs,historyEntry?.rowRefs)&&
          String(candidate?.status||'')===String(historyEntry.status||'')&&
          String(candidate?.targetLearningId||candidate?.appliedLearningId||'')===String(historyEntry?.learningId||'')
        );
        if(alreadyPresent)return;
        const refs=new Set((historyEntry.rowRefs||[]).map(String));
        const historicalEntries=allEntries.filter(entry=>refs.has(String(entry.row?.sourceRow||entry.index+1)));
        const action=historyEntry.decision||(historyEntry.status==='evidence-only'?'Mantener solo como evidencia':'Reforzar learning existente');
        historicalResolved.push({
          action,
          targetLearningId:historyEntry.learningId||'',
          appliedLearningId:historyEntry.learningId||'',
          newLearningTitle:'',
          summary:historyEntry.correctionReason||'Decisión ya resuelta y conservada en el historial.',
          impactUx:historicalEntries.length?learningSuggestionImpact(historicalEntries):'Medio',
          rationale:historyEntry.correctionReason||'Decisión histórica ya revisada.',
          evidence:historicalEntries.length?learningSuggestionEvidence(historicalEntries,item):[],
          theme:historyEntry.status==='evidence-only'?'evidence-only':'',
          id:historyEntry.candidateId||learningSuggestionCandidateId(historyEntry.learningId||action,historicalEntries),
          fingerprint:historyEntry.fingerprint||learningSuggestionHash(`${historyEntry.learningId||action}|${(historyEntry.rowRefs||[]).join(',')}`),
          rowRefs:[...(historyEntry.rowRefs||[])],
          status:historyEntry.status,
          resolvedAt:historyEntry.at||'',
          createdAt:historyEntry.at||new Date().toISOString()
        });
      });
      const reserved=new Set(),candidates=[...resolvedCandidates,...historicalResolved];
      const addCandidate=(candidate,entriesForCandidate)=>{
        const rowRefs=learningSuggestionSourceRows(entriesForCandidate),targetKey=candidate.targetLearningId||candidate.theme||candidate.action;
        const fingerprint=learningSuggestionHash(`${targetKey}|${rowRefs.join(',')}`);
        candidates.push(restoreLearningSuggestionCandidate({...candidate,id:learningSuggestionCandidateId(targetKey,entriesForCandidate),fingerprint,rowRefs,status:'pending',createdAt:new Date().toISOString()},previousCandidates,item));
        entriesForCandidate.forEach(entry=>reserved.add(entry.index));
      };

      const serviceEntries=entries.filter(learningSuggestionServiceTheme);
      if(serviceEntries.length>=3)addCandidate({action:'Crear nuevo learning',theme:'service-effort',targetLearningId:'',newLearningTitle:learningSuggestionNewTitle(item,'service-effort',serviceEntries),summary:learningSuggestionNewSummary('service-effort',serviceEntries),impactUx:learningSuggestionImpact(serviceEntries),rationale:`${serviceEntries.length} registros repiten la misma idea: usar información ya conocida para reducir pasos y esfuerzo.`,evidence:learningSuggestionEvidence(serviceEntries,item)},serviceEntries);

      const governanceEntries=entries.filter(entry=>!reserved.has(entry.index)&&learningSuggestionGovernanceTheme(entry));
      const governanceIndexes=new Set(governanceEntries.map(entry=>entry.index));

      const groups=new Map(),unmatched=[];
      entries.filter(entry=>!reserved.has(entry.index)&&!governanceIndexes.has(entry.index)).forEach(entry=>{
        const ranked=learnings.map(learning=>({learning,score:learningSuggestionScore(entry,learning,item)})).sort((a,b)=>b.score-a.score);
        const best=ranked[0];
        const titleScore=best?learningSuggestionTitleScore(entry.row,best.learning):0;
        const anchorScore=best?learningSuggestionSpecificAnchorScore(entry.row,best.learning):0;
        const qualifies=best&&(best.score>=.34||titleScore>=.22||(best.learning?.element&&anchorScore>=.35));
        if(qualifies){
          if(!groups.has(best.learning.id))groups.set(best.learning.id,{learning:best.learning,entries:[],scores:[]});
          groups.get(best.learning.id).entries.push(entry);groups.get(best.learning.id).scores.push(best.score);
        }else unmatched.push(entry);
      });
      groups.forEach(group=>{
        const action=learningSuggestionExistingAction(group.entries,group.learning,group.scores);
        addCandidate({action,targetLearningId:group.learning.id,newLearningTitle:'',summary:`${group.entries.length} ${group.entries.length===1?'registro aporta':'registros aportan'} evidencia nueva al learning existente y ${action==='Reforzar learning existente'?'refuerza su dirección actual':'amplía su contexto o aplicaciones'}.`,impactUx:learningSuggestionImpact(group.entries),rationale:`Coincidencia con ${group.learning.id} · ${Math.round((group.scores.reduce((sum,value)=>sum+value,0)/group.scores.length)*100)}% de afinidad media.`,evidence:learningSuggestionEvidence(group.entries,item)},group.entries);
      });

      const genericClusters=learningSuggestionGenericClusters(unmatched).filter(cluster=>cluster.entries.length>=3);
      genericClusters.slice(0,2).forEach((cluster,index)=>addCandidate({action:'Crear nuevo learning',theme:`cluster-${index+1}`,targetLearningId:'',newLearningTitle:learningSuggestionNewTitle(item,`cluster-${index+1}`,cluster.entries),summary:learningSuggestionNewSummary(`cluster-${index+1}`,cluster.entries),impactUx:learningSuggestionImpact(cluster.entries),rationale:`${cluster.entries.length} registros no encajan con suficiente claridad en un learning existente y comparten una misma línea temática.`,evidence:learningSuggestionEvidence(cluster.entries,item)},cluster.entries));

      const evidenceOnly=entries.filter(entry=>!reserved.has(entry.index));
      if(evidenceOnly.length)addCandidate({action:'Mantener solo como evidencia',theme:'evidence-only',targetLearningId:'',newLearningTitle:'Registros que todavía no justifican un learning independiente',summary:'Estos registros aportan contexto, reglas u oportunidades útiles, pero todavía no hay suficiente evidencia común para convertirlos en un learning propio.',impactUx:learningSuggestionImpact(evidenceOnly),rationale:`${evidenceOnly.length} registros quedan como evidencia estructurada sin forzar una conclusión consolidada.`,evidence:learningSuggestionEvidence(evidenceOnly,item)},evidenceOnly);

      const resolvedOutput=candidates.filter(candidate=>candidate?.status&&candidate.status!=='pending');
      const pendingOutput=candidates.filter(candidate=>!candidate?.status||candidate.status==='pending').slice(0,Math.max(0,6-resolvedOutput.length));
      const state={version:LEARNING_SUGGESTION_VERSION,generatedAt:new Date().toISOString(),rowsFingerprint:rowFingerprint,candidates:[...resolvedOutput,...pendingOutput],history:[...(previousState?.history||register.learningSuggestions?.history||[])]};
      register.learningSuggestions=state;return state;
    }
    function ensureLearningSuggestions(item,{force=false,previousState=null}={}){
      const register=item?.insightRegister;if(!register?.rows?.length)return null;
      const fingerprint=learningSuggestionHash(register.rows.map((row,index)=>learningSuggestionRowSignature(row,index)).join('\n'));
      const current=register.learningSuggestions;
      if(!force&&current?.version===LEARNING_SUGGESTION_VERSION&&current?.rowsFingerprint===fingerprint)return current;
      return buildLearningSuggestions(item,previousState||current);
    }
    function learningSuggestionTarget(candidate){return candidate?.targetLearningId?learnings.find(learning=>learning.id===candidate.targetLearningId):null;}
    function learningSuggestionStatusLabel(candidate){return candidate.status==='confirmed'?'Confirmado':candidate.status==='evidence-only'?'Solo evidencia':'Pendiente';}
    function learningSuggestionStatusClass(candidate){return candidate.status==='confirmed'?'system-success':candidate.status==='evidence-only'?'neutral':'system-pending';}
    function learningSuggestionsMarkup(item){
      const state=ensureLearningSuggestions(item);
      if(!state?.candidates?.length)return `<section class="drawer-section learning-suggestion-section"><div class="learning-suggestion-head"><div><h3>Sugerencias de learning</h3><p>No se han detectado propuestas de consolidación para este Excel.</p></div></div></section>`;
      const pending=state.candidates.filter(candidate=>candidate.status==='pending').length;
      return `<section class="drawer-section learning-suggestion-section"><div class="learning-suggestion-head"><div><h3>Sugerencias de learning</h3><p>UX Intel ha comparado los registros con los learnings existentes. Nada se incorpora al conocimiento consolidado sin tu confirmación.</p></div><span class="learning-suggestion-count">${pending} ${pending===1?'pendiente':'pendientes'}</span></div><div class="learning-suggestion-list">${state.candidates.map(candidate=>{
        const target=learningSuggestionTarget(candidate),title=target?.title||candidate.newLearningTitle||'Propuesta de learning',resolved=candidate.status!=='pending';
        return `<article class="learning-suggestion-card"><div class="learning-suggestion-card-head"><div class="learning-suggestion-copy"><span class="learning-suggestion-action">${escapeHtml(candidate.action)}</span><h4>${escapeHtml(title)}</h4><p>${escapeHtml(candidate.summary||candidate.rationale||'')}</p><div class="learning-suggestion-meta"><span>${candidate.rowRefs.length} ${candidate.rowRefs.length===1?'registro relacionado':'registros relacionados'}</span>${target?`<span>Objetivo: ${escapeHtml(target.id)}</span>`:''}<span>Impacto UX: ${escapeHtml(candidate.impactUx||'No informado')}</span></div></div><span class="status-badge learning-suggestion-status ${learningSuggestionStatusClass(candidate)}">${escapeHtml(learningSuggestionStatusLabel(candidate))}</span></div><details class="learning-suggestion-details"><summary>Ver registros que la sustentan</summary><ul>${(candidate.evidence||[]).map(value=>`<li>${escapeHtml(value)}</li>`).join('')}</ul></details>${resolved?'':`<div class="learning-suggestion-actions"><button class="action-button" type="button" data-learning-suggestion-confirm="${escapeHtml(candidate.id)}" data-source-id="${escapeHtml(item.id)}">Confirmar</button><button class="secondary-button" type="button" data-learning-suggestion-edit="${escapeHtml(candidate.id)}" data-source-id="${escapeHtml(item.id)}">Editar</button><button class="text-button" type="button" data-learning-suggestion-evidence="${escapeHtml(candidate.id)}" data-source-id="${escapeHtml(item.id)}">Mantener solo como evidencia</button></div>`}</article>`;
      }).join('')}</div></section>`;
    }
    function learningSuggestionRows(item,candidate){
      const refs=new Set((candidate?.rowRefs||[]).map(value=>String(value)));
      return (item?.insightRegister?.rows||[]).filter((row,index)=>refs.has(String(row?.sourceRow||index+1)));
    }
    function learningSuggestionEntryRows(item,candidate){
      const refs=new Set((candidate?.rowRefs||[]).map(value=>String(value)));
      return (item?.insightRegister?.rows||[]).map((row,index)=>({row,index})).filter(entry=>refs.has(String(entry.row?.sourceRow||entry.index+1)));
    }
    function learningSuggestionCountText(value,count){
      const text=String(value||'').trim();
      if(!text)return text;
      const label=count===1?'1 registro':`${count} registros`;
      return text.replace(/^\d+\s+registros?\b/i,label);
    }
    function learningSuggestionSplitCandidate(item,entries){
      if(!entries?.length)return null;
      const rowRefs=learningSuggestionSourceRows(entries);
      const fingerprint=learningSuggestionHash(`manual-split|${rowRefs.join(',')}`);
      return {
        action:'Mantener solo como evidencia',
        theme:'manual-split',
        targetLearningId:'',
        newLearningTitle:'Registros separados de la propuesta',
        summary:'Estos registros se han separado manualmente de otra propuesta. Puedes mantenerlos como evidencia o editarlos para asociarlos a otro learning.',
        impactUx:learningSuggestionImpact(entries),
        rationale:'Separados manualmente durante la revisión UX de la sugerencia.',
        evidence:learningSuggestionEvidence(entries,item),
        id:`LS-${fingerprint}`,
        fingerprint,
        rowRefs,
        status:'pending',
        createdAt:new Date().toISOString(),
        manualSplit:true
      };
    }
    function appendLearningSuggestionSplit(item,entries,currentCandidate){
      if(!entries?.length)return null;
      const state=item?.insightRegister?.learningSuggestions;if(!state)return null;
      const split=learningSuggestionSplitCandidate(item,entries);if(!split)return null;
      const already=state.candidates.some(candidate=>candidate!==currentCandidate&&sameLearningSuggestionRows(candidate?.rowRefs,split.rowRefs));
      if(!already)state.candidates.push(split);
      return split;
    }

    function nextLearningIdForInitiative(item){
      const initiative=String(item?.initiative||'UX').trim();
      const same=learnings.filter(learning=>[learning.initiative,...(learning.initiatives||[])].some(value=>initiativeNameEquals(value,initiative)));
      const prefixes=same.map(learning=>String(learning.id||'').match(/^(L-[A-Z0-9]+)-\d+$/)?.[1]).filter(Boolean);
      const prefix=prefixes[0]||`L-${normalizeText(initiative).replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g,'-').replace(/^-|-$/g,'').slice(0,8).toUpperCase()||'UX'}`;
      const numbers=learnings.map(learning=>{const match=String(learning.id||'').match(/^(L-[A-Z0-9]+)-(\d+)$/);return match&&match[1]===prefix?Number(match[2]):null;}).filter(value=>Number.isFinite(value));
      return `${prefix}-${String((numbers.length?Math.max(...numbers):0)+1).padStart(2,'0')}`;
    }
    function learningHistoryRowRefs(refs){
      return [...new Set([...(refs||[])].map(value=>String(value)))];
    }
    function learningHistoryRowsNested(left,right){
      const a=learningHistoryRowRefs(left),b=learningHistoryRowRefs(right);
      if(!a.length||!b.length)return false;
      const aInB=a.every(value=>b.includes(value));
      const bInA=b.every(value=>a.includes(value));
      return aInB||bInA;
    }
    function learningHistoryWithinWindow(left,right){
      const a=Date.parse(left||''),b=Date.parse(right||'');
      return Number.isFinite(a)&&Number.isFinite(b)&&Math.abs(a-b)<=10*60*1000;
    }
    function dedupeLearningPublicationHistory(entries){
      const output=[];
      [...(entries||[])].forEach(entry=>{
        const refs=learningHistoryRowRefs(entry?.rowRefs);
        if(!refs.length||!entry?.documentId){output.push(entry);return;}
        let duplicateIndex=-1;
        for(let index=output.length-1;index>=0;index--){
          const previous=output[index];
          if(String(previous?.documentId||'')!==String(entry.documentId||''))continue;
          if(String(previous?.decision||'')!==String(entry?.decision||''))continue;
          const exactRows=sameLearningSuggestionRows(previous?.rowRefs,entry?.rowRefs);
          const nestedRecent=learningHistoryRowsNested(previous?.rowRefs,entry.rowRefs)&&learningHistoryWithinWindow(previous?.publishedAt,entry?.publishedAt);
          if(!exactRows&&!nestedRecent)continue;
          duplicateIndex=index;
          break;
        }
        if(duplicateIndex<0){output.push(entry);return;}
        const previous=output[duplicateIndex];
        const previousRefs=learningHistoryRowRefs(previous?.rowRefs);
        const previousAt=Date.parse(previous?.publishedAt||'');
        const currentAt=Date.parse(entry?.publishedAt||'');
        const preferCurrent=refs.length>previousRefs.length||(refs.length===previousRefs.length&&currentAt>=previousAt);
        if(preferCurrent){
          output.splice(duplicateIndex,1);
          output.push(entry);
        }
      });
      return output;
    }
    function dedupeLearningSuggestionHistory(entries){
      const output=[];
      [...(entries||[])].forEach(entry=>{
        const refs=learningHistoryRowRefs(entry?.rowRefs);
        if(!refs.length){output.push(entry);return;}
        let duplicateIndex=-1;
        for(let index=output.length-1;index>=0;index--){
          const previous=output[index];
          if(String(previous?.learningId||'')!==String(entry?.learningId||''))continue;
          if(String(previous?.decision||'')!==String(entry?.decision||''))continue;
          const exactRows=sameLearningSuggestionRows(previous?.rowRefs,entry?.rowRefs);
          const nestedRecent=learningHistoryRowsNested(previous?.rowRefs,entry.rowRefs)&&learningHistoryWithinWindow(previous?.at,entry?.at);
          if(!exactRows&&!nestedRecent)continue;
          duplicateIndex=index;
          break;
        }
        if(duplicateIndex<0){output.push(entry);return;}
        const previous=output[duplicateIndex];
        const previousRefs=learningHistoryRowRefs(previous?.rowRefs);
        const previousAt=Date.parse(previous?.at||'');
        const currentAt=Date.parse(entry?.at||'');
        const preferCurrent=refs.length>previousRefs.length||(refs.length===previousRefs.length&&currentAt>=previousAt);
        if(preferCurrent){
          output.splice(duplicateIndex,1);
          output.push(entry);
        }
      });
      return output;
    }
    function normalizeLearningHistoryState(){
      let changed=false;
      learnings.forEach(learning=>{
        const current=[...(learning?.publicationHistory||[])];
        const cleaned=dedupeLearningPublicationHistory(current);
        if(JSON.stringify(current)!==JSON.stringify(cleaned)){
          learning.publicationHistory=cleaned;
          changed=true;
        }
      });
      sourceItems.forEach(item=>{
        const state=item?.insightRegister?.learningSuggestions;
        if(!state)return;
        const current=[...(state.history||[])];
        const cleaned=dedupeLearningSuggestionHistory(current);
        if(JSON.stringify(current)!==JSON.stringify(cleaned)){
          state.history=cleaned;
          changed=true;
        }
      });
      return changed;
    }


    // Mantiene las preguntas abiertas sincronizadas con nueva evidencia ya revisada.
    // Solo actúa cuando una fila declara explícitamente su estado (Resuelta / Parcial / Abierta)
    // y la relación semántica con una pregunta existente es suficientemente fuerte.
    function questionResolutionStatus(row){
      const value=normalizeText(row?.status||'');
      if(value==='resuelta')return 'resolved';
      if(value==='parcial')return 'partial';
      if(value==='abierta')return 'open';
      return '';
    }
    function questionResolutionRowText(row){
      return [row?.insight,row?.evidence,row?.implication,row?.recommendation,row?.openQuestion,row?.product,row?.funnel,row?.minifunnel,row?.screen,row?.component,row?.element]
        .filter(Boolean).join(' · ');
    }
    function questionOwnerCompatible(owner,row){
      const ownerProduct=normalizeText(owner?.product||''),rowProduct=normalizeText(row?.product||'');
      return !(ownerProduct&&rowProduct&&ownerProduct!==rowProduct);
    }
    function questionLooksLikeDesignQuestion(question){
      const value=normalizeText(question||'');
      const asksHow=/^[¿?¡!\s]*(como|de que forma|que patron|que tratamiento|como deberia|como deben)/.test(value);
      const designTopic=/(comunic|mostrar|presentar|explicar|diseñ|patron|interaccion|mensaje|jerarquia|visual)/.test(value);
      return asksHow&&designTopic;
    }
    function questionTopicBonus(question,row){
      const q=normalizeText(question||''),text=normalizeText(questionResolutionRowText(row));
      let bonus=0;
      ['upsell','fare families','fare family','priority boarding','asientos','equipaje','login','multipasajero'].forEach(term=>{
        if(q.includes(term)&&text.includes(term))bonus+=0.10;
      });
      ['millas','acumulacion','beneficios','tarifa','suma','pasajero','elegibilidad','descuento'].forEach(term=>{
        if(q.includes(term)&&text.includes(term))bonus+=0.025;
      });
      return Math.min(0.18,bonus);
    }
    function questionResolutionMatch(question,owner,rows){
      // Las preguntas de diseño/representación siguen abiertas aunque una dependencia técnica se aclare.
      if(questionLooksLikeDesignQuestion(question))return null;
      let best=null;
      (rows||[]).forEach(row=>{
        const status=questionResolutionStatus(row);
        if(!status||!questionOwnerCompatible(owner,row))return;
        let score=semanticSimilarity(question,questionResolutionRowText(row))+questionTopicBonus(question,row);
        if(normalizeText(owner?.product||'')&&normalizeText(owner?.product||'')===normalizeText(row?.product||''))score+=0.04;
        const ownerComponent=normalizeText(owner?.component||''),rowComponent=normalizeText(row?.component||'');
        if(ownerComponent&&rowComponent&&ownerComponent===rowComponent)score+=0.04;
        const ownerScreen=normalizeText(owner?.screen||''),rowScreen=normalizeText(row?.screen||'');
        if(ownerScreen&&rowScreen&&(ownerScreen===rowScreen||rowScreen.includes(ownerScreen)||ownerScreen.includes(rowScreen)))score+=0.03;
        if(score>=0.30&&(!best||score>best.score))best={row,status,score};
      });
      return best;
    }
    function recordQuestionTransition(owner,from,to,status,item,row){
      owner.questionHistory=Array.isArray(owner.questionHistory)?owner.questionHistory:[];
      const entry={from:String(from||''),to:String(to||''),status,sourceName:item?.name||'',sourceFile:item?.originalName||'',at:new Date().toISOString(),rowRef:row?.sourceRow||''};
      const duplicate=owner.questionHistory.some(value=>normalizeText(value?.from||'')===normalizeText(entry.from)&&normalizeText(value?.to||'')===normalizeText(entry.to)&&String(value?.sourceName||'')===entry.sourceName);
      if(!duplicate)owner.questionHistory.push(entry);
    }
    function reconcileLearningQuestions(learning,rows,item){
      const current=Array.isArray(learning?.open)?learning.open:[];
      if(!current.length)return false;
      let changed=false;
      const next=[];
      current.forEach(question=>{
        const match=questionResolutionMatch(question,learning,rows);
        if(!match){next.push(question);return;}
        const replacement=String(match.row?.openQuestion||'').trim();
        if(match.status==='resolved'){
          changed=true;
          if(replacement&&normalizeText(replacement)!==normalizeText(question))next.push(replacement);
          recordQuestionTransition(learning,question,replacement,'resolved',item,match.row);
          return;
        }
        if(replacement&&normalizeText(replacement)!==normalizeText(question)){
          next.push(replacement);changed=true;
          recordQuestionTransition(learning,question,replacement,match.status,item,match.row);
        }else next.push(question);
      });
      const seen=new Set();
      learning.open=next.filter(value=>{const key=normalizeText(value);if(!key||seen.has(key))return false;seen.add(key);return true;});
      if(changed)learning.updatedAt=new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10);
      return changed;
    }
    function reconcileStandaloneQuestions(rows,item){
      let changed=false;
      openQuestionItems.forEach(questionItem=>{
        if(questionItem?.resolvedAt||!questionItem?.text)return;
        const match=questionResolutionMatch(questionItem.text,questionItem,rows);
        if(!match)return;
        const replacement=String(match.row?.openQuestion||'').trim();
        recordQuestionTransition(questionItem,questionItem.text,replacement,match.status,item,match.row);
        if(match.status==='resolved'&&!replacement){
          questionItem.resolvedAt=new Date().toISOString();
          questionItem.resolvedBy=item?.name||'';
          changed=true;
          return;
        }
        if(replacement&&normalizeText(replacement)!==normalizeText(questionItem.text)){
          questionItem.text=replacement;
          questionItem.updatedAt=new Date().toISOString();
          changed=true;
        }
      });
      return changed;
    }
    function reconcileOpenQuestionsFromRows(rows,item){
      const useful=(rows||[]).filter(row=>questionResolutionStatus(row));
      if(!useful.length)return false;
      let changed=false;
      learnings.forEach(learning=>{if(reconcileLearningQuestions(learning,useful,item))changed=true;});
      if(reconcileStandaloneQuestions(useful,item))changed=true;
      return changed;
    }
    function reconcileResolvedQuestionKnowledge(){
      // Conservada solo por compatibilidad con estados antiguos.
      // UX Intel ya no modifica preguntas mediante matching global.
      return false;
    }

    function applyLearningSuggestion(item,candidate){
      const rows=learningSuggestionRows(item,candidate),publishedAt=new Date().toISOString(),entries=rows.map((row,index)=>({row,index})),evidence=learningSuggestionEvidence(entries,item);
      const components=[...new Set(rows.map(row=>canonicalComponentName(row.component)).filter(Boolean))];
      const applications=[...new Set(rows.flatMap(row=>[row.screen,row.component,row.element,...String(row.uxLine||'').split(/[;|·]/)]).map(value=>String(value||'').trim()).filter(Boolean))];
      if(candidate.action==='Mantener solo como evidencia'){candidate.status='evidence-only';candidate.resolvedAt=publishedAt;return null;}
      let learning=learningSuggestionTarget(candidate);
      if(candidate.action==='Crear nuevo learning'){
        const products=[...new Set(rows.map(row=>String(row.product||'').trim()).filter(Boolean))],screens=[...new Set(rows.map(row=>String(row.screen||'').trim()).filter(Boolean))],devices=[...new Set(rows.map(row=>String(row.channelEnvironment||row.device||'').trim()).filter(Boolean))],markets=[...new Set(rows.map(row=>String(row.market||'').trim()).filter(Boolean))];
        const rawComponents=[...new Set(rows.map(row=>String(row.component||'').trim()).filter(Boolean))];
        const inferredComponent=components.length===1?components[0]:(rawComponents.length===1?rawComponents[0]:'');
        const enteredComponent=String(candidate.component||inferredComponent||'').trim();
        const selectedComponent=canonicalComponentName(enteredComponent)||enteredComponent;
        if(!selectedComponent)throw new Error('Edita la propuesta y escribe un único componente antes de crear el learning.');
        learning={id:nextLearningIdForInitiative(item),initiative:item.initiative||rows[0]?.initiative||'Sin clasificar',initiatives:[item.initiative||rows[0]?.initiative||'Sin clasificar'].filter(Boolean),area:rows[0]?.uxLine||rows[0]?.component||'UX',impactUx:candidate.impactUx||'Medio',confidence:'Media',product:products.length===1?products[0]:(item.product||products[0]||'No especificado'),screen:screens.length===1?screens[0]:'Multipantalla',component:selectedComponent,element:'',device:devices.length===1?devices[0]:(devices.length?'Desktop + mobile':'No especificado'),market:markets.length===1?markets[0]:'No especificado',title:candidate.newLearningTitle||'Nuevo learning',text:candidate.summary||'',know:candidate.summary||'',forEvidence:evidence,contrary:[],noProof:['Este learning se consolida a partir de los registros revisados de esta fuente; no demuestra por sí solo impacto causal ni permite generalizar fuera de su contexto.'],open:[...new Set(rows.map(row=>String(row.openQuestion||'').trim()).filter(Boolean))],applications,sourceNames:[item.name],status:'Learning consolidado',createdAt:publishedAt.slice(0,10),updatedAt:publishedAt.slice(0,10),publicationHistory:[]};
        learnings.push(learning);candidate.targetLearningId=learning.id;
      }else{
        if(!learning)throw new Error('Selecciona un learning objetivo antes de confirmar esta propuesta.');
        if(candidate.action==='Contradice learning')mergeUnique(learning,'contrary',evidence);else{mergeUnique(learning,'forEvidence',evidence);if(candidate.action==='Ampliar learning'&&candidate.summary&&!normalizeText(learning.know||'').includes(normalizeText(candidate.summary).slice(0,60)))learning.know=`${String(learning.know||'').trim()} ${candidate.summary}`.trim();}
        mergeUnique(learning,'applications',applications);mergeUnique(learning,'sourceNames',[item.name]);mergeUnique(learning,'initiatives',[item.initiative].filter(Boolean));learning.updatedAt=publishedAt.slice(0,10);
      }
      learning.publicationHistory=dedupeLearningPublicationHistory([
        ...(learning.publicationHistory||[]),
        {documentId:item.id,sourceName:item.name,decision:candidate.action,publishedAt,rowRefs:[...(candidate.rowRefs||[])]}
      ]);
      candidate.status='confirmed';candidate.resolvedAt=publishedAt;candidate.appliedLearningId=learning.id;return learning;
    }
    function resolveLearningSuggestion(item,candidate,status){
      if(!item?.insightRegister?.learningSuggestions||!candidate)return;
      const state=item.insightRegister.learningSuggestions;
      candidate.status=status;
      candidate.resolvedAt=new Date().toISOString();
      state.history=dedupeLearningSuggestionHistory([...(state.history||[]),{
        candidateId:candidate.id,
        fingerprint:candidate.fingerprint,
        rowRefs:[...(candidate.rowRefs||[])],
        status,
        decision:candidate.action,
        learningId:candidate.appliedLearningId||candidate.targetLearningId||'',
        at:candidate.resolvedAt
      }]);
      rememberLearningSuggestionDecision(item,candidate,status,candidate.appliedLearningId||candidate.targetLearningId||'');
      if(status==='evidence-only')reconcileOpenQuestionsFromRows(learningSuggestionRows(item,candidate),item);
      safeStorageWrite({project:true});renderOverview();renderLearnings();renderSources();renderExplore();openInsightRegister(item);
    }
    function confirmLearningSuggestion(item,candidate){
      try{
        const learning=applyLearningSuggestion(item,candidate),state=item.insightRegister.learningSuggestions;
        reconcileOpenQuestionsFromRows(learningSuggestionRows(item,candidate),item);
        state.history=dedupeLearningSuggestionHistory([...(state.history||[]),{
          candidateId:candidate.id,
          fingerprint:candidate.fingerprint,
          rowRefs:[...(candidate.rowRefs||[])],
          status:candidate.status,
          decision:candidate.action,
          learningId:learning?.id||candidate.appliedLearningId||candidate.targetLearningId||'',
          at:candidate.resolvedAt
        }]);
        rememberLearningSuggestionDecision(item,candidate,candidate.status,learning?.id||candidate.appliedLearningId||candidate.targetLearningId||'');
        if(learning&&['Reforzar learning existente','Ampliar learning'].includes(candidate.action)){
          reconcileLearningQuestions(learning,learningSuggestionRows(item,candidate),item);
        }
        if(!safeStorageWrite({project:true}))throw new Error('No se ha podido guardar la decisión.');
        refreshCatalogMetadata();refreshAllTaxonomyFilters?.();renderOverview();renderLearnings();renderSources();renderExplore();openInsightRegister(item);showToast(learning?'Learning actualizado':'Evidencia mantenida',learning?`${learning.id} se ha actualizado con esta fuente.`:'Los registros siguen disponibles en Explorar sin crear ni modificar un learning.');
      }catch(error){console.error('[UX Intel learning suggestion]',error);showToast('No se ha aplicado la sugerencia',String(error?.message||error));}
    }
    function openLearningSuggestionEditor(item,candidate){
      const sameInitiative=learnings.filter(learning=>!item.initiative||[learning.initiative,...(learning.initiatives||[])].some(value=>initiativeNameEquals(value,item.initiative)));
      const entries=learningSuggestionEntryRows(item,candidate),rows=entries.map(entry=>entry.row);
      const inferredCanonical=[...new Set(rows.map(row=>canonicalComponentName(row.component)).filter(Boolean))];
      const inferredRaw=[...new Set(rows.map(row=>String(row.component||'').trim()).filter(Boolean))];
      const contextItem={product:rows[0]?.product||item.product||'',screen:rows[0]?.screen||item.screen||''};
      const componentOptions=learningComponentOptions(contextItem);
      const selectedComponent=String(candidate.component||(inferredCanonical.length===1?inferredCanonical[0]:(inferredRaw.length===1?inferredRaw[0]:''))||'').trim();
      const componentListId=`learning-component-options-${String(candidate.id||'candidate').replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g,'-')}`;
      const componentHelp=selectedComponent?(canonicalComponentName(selectedComponent)?`Componente existente: ${canonicalComponentName(selectedComponent)}.`:'Nuevo componente. Se guardará con este nombre.'):'Escribe un componente o elige uno existente. Un learning tiene un único componente principal.';
      const rowOptions=entries.map(({row,index})=>{
        const ref=row?.sourceRow||index+1;
        const meta=[row.component?`Componente: ${row.component}`:'',row.channelEnvironment?`Dispositivo: ${row.channelEnvironment}`:row.device?`Dispositivo: ${row.device}`:'',row.recordType?`Tipo: ${row.recordType}`:''].filter(Boolean).join(' · ');
        return `<label class="learning-suggestion-row-option"><input type="checkbox" name="rowRef" value="${escapeHtml(String(ref))}" checked><span><strong>Fila ${escapeHtml(String(ref))} · ${escapeHtml(row.insight||row.evidence||row.recommendation||'Registro revisado')}</strong>${meta?`<span>${escapeHtml(meta)}</span>`:''}</span></label>`;
      }).join('');
      const header=`<div class="drawer-kicker">Sugerencia de learning</div><h2>Editar propuesta</h2><p class="drawer-lead">Ajusta la decisión y elige exactamente qué registros forman parte de ella. El Excel original no se modifica.</p>`;
      const body=`<form id="learning-suggestion-edit-form" data-source-id="${escapeHtml(item.id)}" data-candidate-id="${escapeHtml(candidate.id)}"><div class="form-grid"><div class="learning-suggestion-row-editor"><div class="learning-suggestion-row-editor-head"><div><strong>Registros incluidos en esta propuesta</strong><span>Desmarca cualquier registro que no pertenezca a este learning. Los que quites quedarán como una propuesta pendiente separada para poder reasignarlos o mantenerlos solo como evidencia.</span></div><span>${entries.length} ${entries.length===1?'registro':'registros'}</span></div><div class="learning-suggestion-row-list">${rowOptions}</div></div><label class="form-field full">Decisión<select name="action"><option ${candidate.action==='Crear nuevo learning'?'selected':''}>Crear nuevo learning</option><option ${candidate.action==='Reforzar learning existente'?'selected':''}>Reforzar learning existente</option><option ${candidate.action==='Ampliar learning'?'selected':''}>Ampliar learning</option><option ${candidate.action==='Contradice learning'?'selected':''}>Contradice learning</option><option ${candidate.action==='Mantener solo como evidencia'?'selected':''}>Mantener solo como evidencia</option></select></label><label class="form-field full">Learning objetivo<select name="targetLearningId"><option value="">Sin learning objetivo</option>${sameInitiative.map(learning=>`<option value="${escapeHtml(learning.id)}" ${candidate.targetLearningId===learning.id?'selected':''}>${escapeHtml(learning.id)} · ${escapeHtml(learning.title)}</option>`).join('')}</select></label><label class="form-field full">Título si se crea un nuevo learning<input name="newLearningTitle" value="${escapeHtml(candidate.newLearningTitle||'')}"></label><label class="form-field full">Componente si se crea un nuevo learning<input name="component" type="text" list="${escapeHtml(componentListId)}" value="${escapeHtml(selectedComponent)}" placeholder="Escribe o selecciona un componente" data-component-free-input><datalist id="${escapeHtml(componentListId)}">${componentOptions.map(value=>`<option value="${escapeHtml(value)}"></option>`).join('')}</datalist><span class="form-help" data-component-free-help>${escapeHtml(componentHelp)}</span></label><label class="form-field full">Síntesis<textarea name="summary">${escapeHtml(candidate.summary||'')}</textarea></label><label class="form-field">Impacto UX<select name="impactUx"><option ${candidate.impactUx==='Alto'?'selected':''}>Alto</option><option ${candidate.impactUx==='Medio'?'selected':''}>Medio</option><option ${candidate.impactUx==='Bajo'?'selected':''}>Bajo</option></select></label></div><div class="form-actions"><button class="secondary-button" type="button" data-learning-suggestion-cancel data-source-id="${escapeHtml(item.id)}">Cancelar</button><button class="action-button" type="submit">Guardar propuesta</button></div></form>`;
      openDrawer(header,body);
    }
    function ensureRecentLearningSuggestions(){
      let changed=0;const now=Date.now(),windowMs=3*24*60*60*1000;
      sourceItems.forEach(item=>{if(!item?.simpleKnowledgeActive||!item?.insightRegister?.rows?.length||item.insightRegister.learningSuggestions)return;const ingested=Date.parse(item.ingestedAt||'');if(!Number.isFinite(ingested)||now-ingested>windowMs)return;if(ensureLearningSuggestions(item,{force:true}))changed++;});
      if(changed)safeStorageWrite({project:false});return changed;
    }

    function openInsightRegister(item){
      const register=item?.insightRegister;if(!register)return;
      const fieldLabels={product:'Producto',funnel:'Funnel',minifunnel:'Minifunnel',component:'Componente',recordType:'Tipo',insight:'Insight / hallazgo',evidence:'Evidencia',uxImplication:'Lectura UX',recommendation:'Recomendación / oportunidad',openQuestion:'Pregunta abierta',origin:'Origen',device:'Dispositivo',channelEnvironment:'Dispositivo',market:'Mercado',region:'Región',impactUx:'Impacto UX',uxLine:'Dimensión UX',observations:'Observaciones'};
      const recognized=(register.recognizedFields||[]).map(field=>fieldLabels[field]||field);
      const visible=register.rows.slice(0,50),filename=register.file?.name||item.originalName||'Excel revisado.xlsx';
      const deviceValues=[...new Set(register.rows.map(row=>row.channelEnvironment||row.device).filter(Boolean))];
      const deviceScope=deviceValues.length===1?deviceValues[0]:deviceValues.length>1?'Varios dispositivos':'No especificado';
      const header=`<div class="drawer-kicker">Excel revisado por UX</div><h2>${escapeHtml(item.name||filename.replace(/\.[^.]+$/,''))}</h2><p class="drawer-file-name">${escapeHtml(filename)}</p><div class="drawer-tags"><span class="status-badge ${register.reviewedByUx?'system-success':'system-pending'}">${register.reviewedByUx?'Revisado por UX':'Revisión UX no confirmada'}</span></div>${documentDrawerMeta(item,deviceScope)}`;
      const relations=documentLearningRelations(item);
      const relatedLearnings=[...relations.used,...relations.proposed].filter((learning,index,array)=>array.findIndex(candidate=>candidate.id===learning.id)===index);
      const relatedLearningMarkup=relatedLearnings.length
        ? `<div class="relation-list document-learning-list">${relatedLearnings.map(learning=>`<div class="relation-row"><div class="relation-copy"><strong>${escapeHtml(learning.title)}</strong><span>${escapeHtml([pathOf(learning),learning.impactUx?`Impacto UX: ${learning.impactUx}`:''].filter(Boolean).join(' · '))}</span></div><button class="relation-action" type="button" data-learning="${escapeHtml(learning.id)}">Abrir</button></div>`).join('')}</div>`
        : `<p class="document-knowledge-empty">Todavía no hay un learning consolidado vinculado directamente a este Excel.</p>`;
      const body=`<section class="drawer-section drawer-section-primary document-knowledge-contribution"><h3>Qué aporta a UX Intelligence</h3><p>Este Excel aporta <strong>${register.rows.length}</strong> ${register.rows.length===1?'registro estructurado':'registros estructurados'}${relatedLearnings.length?` y está relacionado con <strong>${relatedLearnings.length}</strong> ${relatedLearnings.length===1?'learning consolidado':'learnings consolidados'}`:''}.</p>${relatedLearningMarkup}</section>${learningSuggestionsMarkup(item)}<section class="drawer-section"><h3>Conocimiento estructurado</h3><div class="import-summary"><strong>${register.rows.length}</strong><span>${register.rows.length===1?'registro disponible':'registros disponibles'} en Explorar</span></div><div class="relation-list excel-register-list">${visible.map(row=>`<div class="relation-row"><div class="relation-copy"><strong>${escapeHtml(row.insight||row.evidence||row.recommendation||row.openQuestion||`Fila ${row.sourceRow}`)}</strong><span>${escapeHtml([row.recordType?`Tipo: ${row.recordType}`:'',compactJourneyValues(row).length?`Dónde aplica: ${compactJourneyValues(row).join(' › ')}`:''].filter(Boolean).join(' · '))}</span><span>${escapeHtml([row.channelEnvironment?`Dispositivo: ${row.channelEnvironment}`:row.device?`Dispositivo: ${row.device}`:'',row.market?`Mercado: ${row.market}`:'',row.region?`Región: ${row.region}`:'',row.impactUx?`Impacto UX: ${row.impactUx}`:'',row.uxLine?`Dimensión UX: ${String(row.uxLine).split(';').map(value=>value.trim()).filter(Boolean).join(' · ')}`:''].filter(Boolean).join(' · '))}</span></div></div>`).join('')}</div>${register.rows.length>visible.length?`<p>Se muestran 50 de ${register.rows.length} registros.</p>`:''}</section>${drawerFileFooter(item,'excel')}`;
      openDrawer(header,body);
    }
    async function relinkInsightRegisterFile(file,documentId){
      const item=sourceItems.find(document=>document.id===documentId);
      if(!item?.insightRegister){showToast('No se puede vincular','Este documento no tiene un Excel revisado registrado.');return;}
      try{
        const parsed=await UXInsightRegister.parseFile(file),validation=UXInsightRegister.validate(parsed);
        if(!validation.valid)throw new Error(validation.errors.join(' '));
        const existing=item.insightRegister;
        const normalize=value=>String(value??'').trim().replace(/\s+/g,' ').toLowerCase();
        const identity=rows=>(rows||[]).map(row=>normalize(row?.insight||row?.evidence||row?.recommendation||row?.openQuestion||''));
        if((existing.rows||[]).length!==(parsed.rows||[]).length||JSON.stringify(identity(existing.rows))!==JSON.stringify(identity(parsed.rows))){
          throw new Error('El Excel seleccionado no parece corresponder a este documento. Comprueba que estás seleccionando el archivo original asociado.');
        }
        await saveInsightRegisterBlob(item.id,file);
        await persistSourceFile(item.id,'insight-register',file);
        showToast('Archivo Excel vinculado','El fichero ha quedado guardado de forma permanente. Si contiene las mismas filas con nuevos campos de gobierno, UX Intelligence los sincronizará al recargar.');
renderSources();
        openInsightRegister(item);
      }catch(error){
        console.error('[UX Intel relink Insight Register]',error);
        showToast('No se ha vinculado el Excel',String(error?.message||error));
      }
    }

    async function syncInsightRegistersFromStoredFiles(){
      let changed=0;
      const normalize=value=>String(value??'').trim().replace(/\s+/g,' ').toLowerCase();
      const identity=rows=>(rows||[]).map(row=>normalize(row?.insight||row?.evidence||row?.recommendation||row?.openQuestion||''));
      for(const item of sourceItems){
        if(!item?.insightRegister)continue;
        try{
          let file=await getInsightRegisterBlob(item.id).catch(()=>null);
          if(!file)file=await getPersistentSourceBlob(item.id,'insight-register').catch(()=>null);
          if(!file)continue;

          const parsed=await UXInsightRegister.parseFile(file);
          const validation=UXInsightRegister.validate(parsed);
          if(!validation.valid)continue;

          const existing=item.insightRegister;
          const sameIdentity=(existing.rows||[]).length===(parsed.rows||[]).length
            && JSON.stringify(identity(existing.rows))===JSON.stringify(identity(parsed.rows));
          if(!sameIdentity)continue;

          const before=JSON.stringify((existing.rows||[]).map(row=>({
            impactUx:row?.impactUx||'',
            initiative:row?.initiative||'',
            funnel:row?.funnel||'',
            minifunnel:row?.minifunnel||''
          })));
          const after=JSON.stringify((parsed.rows||[]).map(row=>({
            impactUx:row?.impactUx||'',
            initiative:row?.initiative||'',
            funnel:row?.funnel||'',
            minifunnel:row?.minifunnel||''
          })));
          const inferredDocumentDate=insightRegisterDocumentDateFromFileName(parsed.file?.name||item.originalName||item.fileMeta?.name||'');
          const metadataNeedsRepair=existing.reviewedByUx!==true
            || item.documentProfile?.reviewedByUx!==true
            || shouldApplyInsightRegisterFileDate(item,inferredDocumentDate);
          if(before===after&&!metadataNeedsRepair)continue;

          parsed.history=existing.history||[];
          parsed.reviewedByUx=true;
          if(existing.status)parsed.status=existing.status;
          if(existing.analysisVersion)parsed.analysisVersion=existing.analysisVersion;
          if(shouldApplyInsightRegisterFileDate(item,inferredDocumentDate)){
            item.documentDate=inferredDocumentDate;
            item.exactDate=formatDateEs(inferredDocumentDate);
          }

          parsed.learningSuggestions=existing.learningSuggestions||null;
          item.insightRegister=parsed;
          ensureLearningSuggestions(item,{force:true,previousState:existing.learningSuggestions||null});
          item.documentProfile=UXInsightRegister.documentProfile(item,{
            ...(item.documentProfile||{}),
            date:item.exactDate,
            reviewedByUx:true,
            associatedFiles:{insightRegister:parsed.file}
          });
          item.exportDimensions=UXInsightRegister.exportModel().filters;
          changed++;
        }catch(error){
          console.warn('[UX Intel Excel metadata sync]',item?.name,error);
        }
      }
      if(changed){
        safeStorageWrite({project:false});
        renderSources();
        renderOverview();
        renderExplore();
        console.info(`[UX Intel] ${changed} Excel revisado${changed===1?'':'s'} sincronizado${changed===1?'':'s'} con el archivo vinculado.`);
      }
      return changed;
    }

    const UX_INTEL_ALLOWED_RECORD_TYPES=new Set([
      'hecho','evidencia interna','evidencia externa','hallazgo','insight del autor',
      'hipotesis','inferencia','oportunidad','decision','experimento','resultado',
      'learning consolidado','patron','restriccion','pregunta abierta','insight ux'
    ]);
    const UX_INTEL_ALLOWED_ORIGINS=new Set([
      'ux research','test de usuarios','quantum metric','a/b test','cro','auditoria ux',
      'heuristica','benchmark','desk research','data','negocio','csat'
    ]);
    const UX_INTEL_ALLOWED_UX_DIMENSIONS=new Set(["accesibilidad","arquitectura de informacion","claridad y comprension","consistencia","contenido y copy","control y autonomia","encontrabilidad","feedback y estado","flujo y secuenciacion","friccion y eficiencia","jerarquia visual","navegacion","orientacion y ayuda","personalizacion"]);
    function uxRegisterNorm(value){
      return String(value??'')
        .normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,'')
        .replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim().toLocaleLowerCase('es');
    }
    function uxRegisterSplit(value){
      return String(value||'').split(/[;|·\n]/).map(part=>uxRegisterNorm(part)).filter(Boolean);
    }
    function validateInsightRegisterControlledVocabulary(parsed){
      const invalid={recordType:new Set(),origin:new Set(),uxLine:new Set()};
      let tooManyDimensions=0;
      (parsed?.rows||[]).forEach(row=>{
        const normalizedRecordType=(typeof normalizeRecordType==='function')?normalizeRecordType(row?.recordType):row?.recordType;
        const recordType=uxRegisterNorm(normalizedRecordType);
        if(recordType&&!UX_INTEL_ALLOWED_RECORD_TYPES.has(recordType))invalid.recordType.add(String(row.recordType));
        uxRegisterSplit(row?.origin).forEach(value=>{
          if(!UX_INTEL_ALLOWED_ORIGINS.has(value))invalid.origin.add(value);
        });
        const dimensions=uxRegisterSplit(row?.uxLine);
        if(dimensions.length>2)tooManyDimensions++;
        dimensions.forEach(value=>{
          if(!UX_INTEL_ALLOWED_UX_DIMENSIONS.has(value))invalid.uxLine.add(value);
        });
      });
      const messages=[];
      if(invalid.recordType.size)messages.push(`Tipo de registro fuera de vocabulario: ${[...invalid.recordType].join(', ')}`);
      if(invalid.origin.size)messages.push(`Origen del hallazgo fuera de vocabulario: ${[...invalid.origin].join(', ')}`);
      if(invalid.uxLine.size)messages.push(`Dimensión UX fuera de vocabulario: ${[...invalid.uxLine].join(', ')}`);
      if(tooManyDimensions)messages.push(`Dimensión UX admite un máximo de 2 valores (${tooManyDimensions} ${tooManyDimensions===1?'fila':'filas'})`);
      if(messages.length)throw new Error(messages.join('. '));
      return true;
    }
    function insightRegisterGovernedStructure(rows){
      const levels=['product','funnel','minifunnel','component'];
      const result={};
      levels.forEach(level=>result[level]=new Set());
      (rows||[]).forEach(row=>{
        levels.forEach(level=>{
          const values=level==='component'
            ? [...(Array.isArray(row?.components)?row.components:[]),row?.component]
            : [row?.[level]];
          values.map(value=>String(value||'').trim()).filter(Boolean)
            .forEach(value=>result[level].add(uxRegisterNorm(value)));
        });
      });
      return result;
    }
    function insightRegisterIntroducesGovernedStructure(parsed,previous){
      if(!previous)return true;
      const before=insightRegisterGovernedStructure(previous?.rows||[]);
      const after=insightRegisterGovernedStructure(parsed?.rows||[]);
      return Object.keys(after).some(level=>[...after[level]].some(value=>!before[level].has(value)));
    }
    // Excel import governance: historical Pantalla/Elemento columns are compatibility input only.
    // The governed structure is Producto > Funnel > Minifunnel > Componente, so legacy levels
    // are excluded from taxonomy review and cannot block a valid reviewed Excel.
    function insightRegisterTaxonomyReviewPayload(parsed){
      return {
        ...parsed,
        rows:(parsed?.rows||[]).map(row=>({...row,screen:'',element:''}))
      };
    }
    function isLegacyTaxonomyImportError(error){
      return /Nivel o estado taxon[oó]mico no v[aá]lido/i.test(String(error?.message||error||''));
    }
    async function applyInsightRegisterTaxonomySafely(parsed,fileName){
      validateInsightRegisterControlledVocabulary(parsed);
      const payload=insightRegisterTaxonomyReviewPayload(parsed);
      try{
        const governance=await taxonomyReviewRegisterImport(payload,fileName);
        if(governance===null)return null;
        try{
          await taxonomyApplyRegisterGovernance(governance,fileName);
          return {skipped:false,deferred:false};
        }catch(error){
          if(!isLegacyTaxonomyImportError(error))throw error;
          console.warn('[UX Intel taxonomy] La validación heredada de taxonomía no debe bloquear el Excel.',error,governance);
          return {skipped:true,deferred:true,reason:'legacy-taxonomy-validator'};
        }
      }catch(error){
        if(!isLegacyTaxonomyImportError(error))throw error;
        console.warn('[UX Intel taxonomy] La revisión heredada de taxonomía no debe bloquear el Excel.',error);
        return {skipped:true,deferred:true,reason:'legacy-taxonomy-validator'};
      }
    }
    async function applyInsightRegisterTaxonomyForUpdate(parsed,previous,fileName){
      validateInsightRegisterControlledVocabulary(parsed);
      // En una sustitución editorial del mismo Excel, si la estructura gobernada
      // no incorpora ningún valor nuevo, no se vuelve a crear/retirar taxonomía.
      // Esto evita que un validador taxonómico legacy bloquee el guardado.
      if(previous&&!insightRegisterIntroducesGovernedStructure(parsed,previous)){
        return {skipped:true,reason:'same-governed-structure'};
      }
      const taxonomyResult=await applyInsightRegisterTaxonomySafely(parsed,fileName);
      if(taxonomyResult===null)return null;
      return taxonomyResult;
    }

    async function attachInsightRegister(file,documentId){
      const item=sourceItems.find(document=>document.id===documentId);if(!item)return;const previous=item.insightRegister?cloneAnalysisValue(item.insightRegister):null,previousBlob=await getInsightRegisterBlob(item.id).catch(()=>null),previousProfile=item.documentProfile?cloneAnalysisValue(item.documentProfile):null;
      try{
        const parsed=await UXInsightRegister.parseFile(file),validation=UXInsightRegister.validate(parsed);if(!validation.valid)throw new Error(validation.errors.join(' '));
        const fileName=String(file?.name||'Excel revisado.xlsx'),inferredDocumentDate=insightRegisterDocumentDateFromFileName(fileName);
        const taxonomyResult=await applyInsightRegisterTaxonomyForUpdate(parsed,previous,fileName);
        if(taxonomyResult===null){showToast('Importación cancelada','No se ha modificado la taxonomía ni el documento.');return;}
        if(previous){parsed.history=[...(previous.history||[]),{replacedAt:new Date().toISOString(),file:previous.file,rowCount:previous.rows?.length||0,rows:previous.rows||[],recognizedFields:previous.recognizedFields||[]}];}
        parsed.reviewedByUx=true;
        if(inferredDocumentDate){item.documentDate=inferredDocumentDate;item.exactDate=formatDateEs(inferredDocumentDate);}
        item.originalName=fileName;item.fileMeta={name:fileName,size:file?.size||0,type:file?.type||'',lastModified:file?.lastModified||0};
        item.insightRegister=parsed;ensureLearningSuggestions(item,{force:true,previousState:previous?.learningSuggestions||null});item.documentProfile=UXInsightRegister.documentProfile(item,{...(item.documentProfile||{}),date:item.exactDate,reviewedByUx:true,associatedFiles:{insightRegister:parsed.file}});item.exportDimensions=UXInsightRegister.exportModel().filters;
        if(item.status==='Pendiente de Insight Register'&&!hasDocumentAnalysis(item)){item.simpleKnowledgeActive=true;item.status='Procesado';}
        await saveInsightRegisterBlob(item.id,file);await persistSourceFile(item.id,'insight-register',file);if(!safeStorageWrite({project:false}))throw new Error('No se ha podido guardar la asociación en el navegador.');
        if(hasDocumentAnalysis(item))await persistDocumentAnalysis(item,item.analysis);else await queueProjectHistory();
        renderSources();renderOverview();renderExplore();openSource(item.id);showToast(previous?'Excel revisado actualizado':'Excel revisado añadido',item.simpleKnowledgeActive?'Los registros curados ya forman parte de la base activa.':'La síntesis UX pertenece al mismo documento y no se ha publicado conocimiento.');
      }catch(error){item.insightRegister=previous;item.documentProfile=previousProfile;if(previousBlob)await saveInsightRegisterBlob(item.id,previousBlob).catch(()=>{});else await deleteInsightRegisterBlob(item.id).catch(()=>{});safeStorageWrite({project:false});console.error('[UX Intel Insight Register]',error);showToast('No se ha guardado el Excel revisado',String(error?.message||error));}
    }

    async function importInsightRegister(file){
      let item=null;
      try{
        const parsed=await UXInsightRegister.parseFile(file),validation=UXInsightRegister.validate(parsed);
        if(!validation.valid)throw new Error(validation.errors.join(' '));
        const fileName=String(file?.name||'Excel revisado.xlsx'),inferredDocumentDate=insightRegisterDocumentDateFromFileName(fileName);
        const taxonomyResult=await applyInsightRegisterTaxonomySafely(parsed,fileName);
        if(taxonomyResult===null){showToast('Importación cancelada','No se ha modificado la taxonomía ni la base de conocimiento.');return;}
        const firstRow=parsed.rows[0]||{};
        item={
          id:`IR-${Date.now()}`,
          name:canonicalDocumentTitle(fileName.replace(/\.[^.]+$/,'')),
          originalName:fileName,
          product:firstRow.product||'',funnel:firstRow.funnel||'',minifunnel:firstRow.minifunnel||'',screen:firstRow.screen||'',component:firstRow.component||'',
          initiative:firstRow.initiative||firstRow.component||firstRow.product||'Sin clasificar',
          initiatives:firstRow.initiative?[firstRow.initiative]:[],
          type:'Insight Register',
          exactDate:inferredDocumentDate?formatDateEs(inferredDocumentDate):'No especificada',documentDate:inferredDocumentDate,ingestedAt:new Date().toISOString(),status:'Procesado',
          simpleKnowledgeActive:true,fileMeta:{name:fileName,size:file?.size||0,type:file?.type||'',lastModified:file?.lastModified||0},
          insightRegister:parsed,exportDimensions:UXInsightRegister.exportModel().filters,
          taxonomySyncPending:taxonomyResult?.deferred===true
        };
        ensureLearningSuggestions(item,{force:true});
        item.documentProfile=UXInsightRegister.documentProfile(item,{reviewedByUx:true,associatedFiles:{insightRegister:parsed.file}});
        item.insightRegister.reviewedByUx=true;
        await saveInsightRegisterBlob(item.id,file);
        await persistSourceFile(item.id,'insight-register',file);
        sourceItems.unshift(item);
        if(!safeStorageWrite({project:false}))throw new Error('No se ha podido guardar el Excel en el navegador.');
        if(projectHistory.ready&&!projectHistory.error&&documentHydrationComplete)await queueProjectHistory();
        renderSources();renderOverview();renderExplore();
        showPanel(excelReturnPanel||'sources');openInsightRegister(item);
        showToast('Excel incorporado',taxonomyResult?.deferred
          ? `${parsed.rows.length} registros añadidos. La validación taxonómica heredada queda pendiente y ya no bloquea la importación.`
          : `${parsed.rows.length} registros añadidos. Puedes consultarlos cuando quieras en Explorar.`);
      }catch(error){
        if(item){const index=sourceItems.findIndex(value=>value.id===item.id);if(index>=0)sourceItems.splice(index,1);await deleteInsightRegisterBlob(item.id).catch(()=>{});safeStorageWrite({project:false});}
        console.error('[UX Intel Excel ingest]',error);showToast('No se ha importado el Excel',String(error?.message||error));
      }
    }
    async function analyzeWithInsightRegister(item){
      if(!item?.insightRegister||!hasDocumentAnalysis(item))return;
      if(!['active-new-ingest','insight-register-primary'].includes(item.analysis?.documentFirstV2?.mode)){showToast('Migración controlada necesaria','El documento histórico conserva document-first-1. Añadir el Excel no lo migra automáticamente.');return;}
      const previous=serializableAnalysisClone(item.analysis).value,previousRegister=cloneAnalysisValue(item.insightRegister);
      try{
        const prepared=UXDocumentAnalysisV2.prepareWithInsightRegister({analysis:previous,document:item,register:item.insightRegister,canonicalContext:{product:item.product,screen:item.screen,component:item.component}}),tree=UXDocumentAnalysis.inspectDataTree(prepared);if(tree.total)throw new Error('El análisis con el Excel revisado contiene valores no persistibles.');
        item.analysis=serializableAnalysisClone(prepared).value;item.insightRegister={...item.insightRegister,status:'analyzed-pending-review',analysisVersion:item.analysis.documentFirstV2.createdAt};item.status='Pendiente de revisión';item.analysis.review={...(item.analysis.review||{}),status:'Pendiente de revisión',updatedAt:new Date().toISOString()};
        await saveDocumentAnalysis(item.id,item.analysis);if(!safeStorageWrite({project:false}))throw new Error('No se ha podido guardar el análisis en el navegador.');await persistDocumentAnalysis(item,previous);activeReviewDocumentId=item.id;renderSources();showPanel('review');showToast('Análisis preparado','La síntesis UX es la capa editorial principal. Nada se ha publicado.');
      }catch(error){item.analysis=previous;item.insightRegister=previousRegister;await saveDocumentAnalysis(item.id,previous).catch(()=>{});safeStorageWrite({project:false});console.error('[UX Intel Insight Register analysis]',error);showToast('No se ha preparado el análisis',String(error?.message||error));}
    }
    function sourceUpdateCounts(item){const related=documentKnowledgeRelations(item);return [['learnings',related.learnings.length],['evidencias',related.evidence.length],['tests A/B',related.tests.length],['patrones',related.patterns.length]].filter(([,count])=>count).map(([label,count])=>`${count} ${label}`).join(' · ')||'Sin conocimiento publicado relacionado';}
    function openSourceUpdateConfirmation(file,item){
      pendingSourceUpdate={file,documentId:item.id};
      const header=`<div class="drawer-kicker">Actualizar fuente documental</div><h2>${escapeHtml(item.name)}</h2><p class="drawer-lead">Vincula el archivo al registro existente sin borrar su histórico.</p>`;
      const body=`<section class="drawer-section"><h3>Archivo seleccionado</h3><p>${escapeHtml(file.name)} · ${Math.max(1,Math.round(file.size/1024))} KB</p></section><section class="drawer-section"><h3>Se conserva</h3><p>ID ${escapeHtml(item.id)} · ${escapeHtml(sourceUpdateCounts(item))} · clasificación y estados anteriores.</p></section><section class="drawer-section"><h3>Qué ocurrirá</h3><p>El archivo se analizará como una nueva versión de esta fuente. Las coincidencias, límites, contradicciones y propuestas nuevas quedarán pendientes de revisión. No se publicará ni se fusionará conocimiento automáticamente.</p></section><div class="form-actions"><button class="secondary-button" type="button" data-cancel-source-update>Cancelar</button><button class="action-button" type="button" data-confirm-source-update>Vincular archivo y analizar</button></div>`;
      openDrawer(header,body);
    }
    function openSourceCandidateChoice(file,matches){
      pendingSourceUpdate={file,documentId:null};
      const header=`<div class="drawer-kicker">Posible documento existente</div><h2>¿A qué registro pertenece?</h2><p class="drawer-lead">No se creará un duplicado hasta que confirmes el registro correcto.</p>`;
      const body=`<section class="drawer-section"><h3>${escapeHtml(file.name)}</h3>${matches.map(match=>`<button class="relation-action" type="button" data-source-update-choice="${escapeHtml(match.item.id)}">${escapeHtml(match.item.name)}</button>`).join('')}</section><div class="form-actions"><button class="secondary-button" type="button" data-cancel-source-update>Cancelar</button></div>`;
      openDrawer(header,body);
    }
    async function processDocumentFile(file,targetId,{linkExisting=false}={}){
      let documentItem=sourceItems.find(item=>item.id===targetId);
      const isNewIngest=!documentItem;
      const resolvedFileName=String(file?.name||documentItem?.originalName||documentItem?.fileMeta?.name||`${documentItem?.name||'documento'}.pdf`);
      if(!documentItem){documentItem={id:`UP-${Date.now()}`,name:canonicalDocumentTitle(resolvedFileName.replace(/\.[^.]+$/,'')),product:'',screen:'',component:'',element:'',initiative:'Sin clasificar',type:'Sin clasificar',exactDate:'No especificada',documentDate:null,ingestedAt:new Date().toISOString(),status:'Disponible'};sourceItems.unshift(documentItem);}
      const previousDocument=linkExisting?JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(documentItem)):null;
      const previousBlob=linkExisting?await getDocumentBlob(documentItem.id).catch(()=>null):null;
      const sourceUpdateBaseline=linkExisting?documentSourceUpdateBaseline(documentItem):null;
      const previousAnalysis=documentItem.analysis?serializableAnalysisClone(documentItem.analysis).value:null;
      let savingProject=false;
      if(!documentItem.ingestedAt)documentItem.ingestedAt=new Date().toISOString();
      documentItem.originalName=resolvedFileName;
      documentItem.fileMeta={name:resolvedFileName,size:file?.size||documentItem.fileMeta?.size||0,type:file?.type||documentItem.fileMeta?.type||'',lastModified:file?.lastModified||documentItem.fileMeta?.lastModified||0};
      documentItem.status='Analizando';documentItem.processing=null;documentItem.analysisFailure=null;documentItem.analysisError='';documentItem.analysisStack='';
      pipelineCheckpoint(documentItem,'file_received',{sizeBytes:documentItem.fileMeta.size,mimeType:documentItem.fileMeta.type||'No indicado'});
      renderSources();showPanel('sources');showToast('Analizando documento','Leyendo todas las páginas y preparando unidades de conocimiento.');
      try{
        await saveDocumentBlob(documentItem.id,file);
        pipelineCheckpoint(documentItem,'file_stored',{storage:'IndexedDB/files'});
        const extraction=await extractDocumentPages(file,resolvedFileName,(phase,details)=>pipelineCheckpoint(documentItem,phase,details));
        documentItem.analysis=buildAnalysis(documentItem,extraction.pages,{engine:isNewIngest?'document-first-2':'document-first-1'});
        if(sourceUpdateBaseline){annotateSourceUpdateAnalysis(documentItem.analysis,documentItem,sourceUpdateBaseline);documentItem.sourceHistory=[...(documentItem.sourceHistory||[]),sourceUpdateBaseline.revision];}
        pipelineCheckpoint(documentItem,'findings_generated',{findings:documentItem.analysis.findings.length,relations:documentItem.analysis.relations.length});
        documentItem.analysis.extraction=extraction.diagnostics;
        documentItem.analysis.extractedPages=extraction.pages;
        documentItem.analysis.extractedText=extraction.pages.map(page=>`[Página ${page.page}]\n${page.text}`).join('\n\n');
        documentItem.analysis.review={status:'Pendiente de revisión',updatedAt:new Date().toISOString()};
        documentItem.documentDate=temporalValue(documentItem.analysis.metadata.date,'documento')||documentItem.documentDate||temporalValue(documentItem.exactDate,'manual')||temporalValue(documentItem.catalogDate,'catálogo');
        documentItem.analysis.metadata.date=documentDate(documentItem);
        if(!documentItem.analysis.findings.length)throw new Error('El texto se ha leído, pero no se han podido generar hallazgos reales para revisión. El documento no se marcará como analizado.');
        pipelineCheckpoint(documentItem,'analysis_created',{pages:documentItem.analysis.pages,findings:documentItem.analysis.findings.length,relations:documentItem.analysis.relations.length});
        const treeCheck=UXDocumentAnalysis.inspectDataTree(documentItem.analysis);
        pipelineCheckpoint(documentItem,'analysis_tree_checked',{circularReferences:treeCheck.circularReferences.length,sharedReferences:treeCheck.sharedReferences.length,unsupportedValues:treeCheck.unsupportedValues.length,undefinedValues:treeCheck.undefinedValues.length,total:treeCheck.total});
        if(treeCheck.total)throw new Error(`El análisis no es un árbol persistible: ${JSON.stringify(treeCheck)}`);
        const serializable=serializableAnalysisClone(documentItem.analysis);
        documentItem.analysis=serializable.value;
        pipelineCheckpoint(documentItem,'analysis_serialized',{jsonLength:serializable.jsonLength});
        await saveDocumentAnalysis(documentItem.id,documentItem.analysis);
        pipelineCheckpoint(documentItem,'analysis_persisted',{storage:'IndexedDB/analyses'});
        const persistedAnalysis=await getDocumentAnalysis(documentItem.id);
        if(!persistedAnalysis)throw new Error('IndexedDB no devolvió el análisis después de guardarlo.');
        pipelineCheckpoint(documentItem,'analysis_restored',{pages:persistedAnalysis.pages||0,findings:persistedAnalysis.findings?.length||0,relations:persistedAnalysis.relations?.length||0});
        const wrongSourceIds=(persistedAnalysis.findings||[]).filter(finding=>finding.source?.documentId!==documentItem.id).length;
        if(wrongSourceIds)throw new Error(`${wrongSourceIds} hallazgos no están relacionados con el documento ${documentItem.id}.`);
        documentItem.analysis=persistedAnalysis;
        pipelineCheckpoint(documentItem,'document_linked',{documentId:documentItem.id,sourceIdsVerified:persistedAnalysis.findings.length});
        documentItem.product=documentItem.analysis.metadata.products[0]||'No especificado';documentItem.screen=documentItem.analysis.metadata.screens[0]||'No especificado';documentItem.component=documentItem.analysis.metadata.components[0]||'';documentItem.initiative=documentItem.analysis.metadata.initiative;documentItem.type=documentItem.analysis.metadata.detectedType;documentItem.analysisType=normalizeDocumentAnalysisType(documentItem.analysis.metadata.detectedType)||documentAnalysisType(documentItem);
        documentItem.exactDate=documentItem.analysis.metadata.date;
        documentItem.documentProfile=UXInsightRegister.documentProfile(documentItem,{analysisType:documentItem.analysisType||documentAnalysisType(documentItem),primaryProduct:documentItem.product,devices:documentItem.analysis.metadata.devices||[],markets:documentItem.analysis.metadata.markets||[],segments:documentItem.analysis.metadata.segments||[],documentLimits:documentItem.analysis.documentFirstV2?.documentLimits||[]});
        documentItem.exportDimensions=UXInsightRegister.exportModel().filters;
        documentItem.status='Pendiente de revisión';
        documentItem.analysisError='';documentItem.analysisStack='';documentItem.analysisFailure=null;documentItem.sourceUpdateFailure=null;
        activeReviewDocumentId=documentItem.id;
        if(!safeStorageWrite({project:false}))throw new Error('El análisis se ha completado, pero no se ha podido guardar en el navegador. El documento no se marcará como pendiente de revisión.');
        const persistedDocument=safeStorageRead().documents?.find(item=>item.id===documentItem.id);
        if(persistedDocument?.status!=='Pendiente de revisión'||persistedDocument?.analysisSummary?.findings!==documentItem.analysis.findings.length||persistedAnalysis?.findings?.length!==documentItem.analysis.findings.length||persistedAnalysis?.extractedPages?.length!==extraction.pages.length)throw new Error('El análisis no ha superado la comprobación de persistencia. Vuelve a seleccionar el archivo para intentarlo de nuevo.');
        pipelineCheckpoint(documentItem,'status_updated',{status:documentItem.status});
        documentItem.processing.completedAt=new Date().toISOString();
        savingProject=true;
        pipelineCheckpoint(documentItem,'project_persisting',{documentId:documentItem.id});
        await persistDocumentAnalysis(documentItem,previousAnalysis,{allowInterruptedSourceUpdate:linkExisting});
        savingProject=false;
        safeStorageWrite({project:false});
        setDocumentAnalysisTypeFilter(document.getElementById('source-type')?.value||'Todos');refreshDocumentDateFilter();renderSources();renderOverview();showPanel('review');
        showToast('Análisis listo',`${documentItem.analysis.findings.length} hallazgos propuestos para revisión humana.`);
      }catch(error){
        const failure={message:String(error?.message||error),phase:error?.phase||documentItem.processing?.phase||'unknown',timestamp:new Date().toISOString(),stack:error?.stack||'Stack no disponible',runId:documentItem.processing?.runId||'',checkpoints:documentItem.processing?.checkpoints||[]};
        console.error('[UX Intel PDF] Error exacto del análisis',{fileName:resolvedFileName,fileSize:file?.size||documentItem.fileMeta?.size||0,...failure,error});
        if(linkExisting&&previousDocument){
          Object.keys(documentItem).forEach(key=>delete documentItem[key]);Object.assign(documentItem,previousDocument,{sourceUpdateFailure:failure});
          try{if(previousAnalysis)await saveDocumentAnalysis(documentItem.id,previousAnalysis);else await deleteDocumentAnalysis(documentItem.id);if(previousBlob)await saveDocumentBlob(documentItem.id,previousBlob);else await deleteDocumentBlob(documentItem.id);}
          catch(cacheError){console.error('No se pudo restaurar íntegramente la fuente anterior',cacheError);}
        }else if(savingProject){
          documentItem.analysis=previousAnalysis;
          try{if(previousAnalysis)await saveDocumentAnalysis(documentItem.id,previousAnalysis);else await deleteDocumentAnalysis(documentItem.id);}
          catch(cacheError){console.error('No se pudo restaurar la copia del navegador tras el fallo del proyecto',cacheError);}
        }
        if(!linkExisting){documentItem.status='Error';documentItem.analysisError=failure.message;documentItem.analysisStack=failure.stack;documentItem.analysisFailure=failure;}
        safeStorageWrite({project:false});renderSources();showToast(linkExisting?'No se ha podido actualizar la fuente':savingProject?'El análisis se ha generado pero no se ha podido guardar.':'No se pudo analizar',linkExisting?`Fase: ${failure.phase} · Error: ${failure.message}`:`${failure.phase}: ${failure.message}`);
      }
    }
    // Las migraciones editoriales históricas no se ejecutan en runtime.
