    // Los estados ya migrados permanecen persistidos en localStorage/IndexedDB.

    uploadInput.addEventListener('change',async()=>{const file=uploadInput.files?.[0],targetId=uploadTargetId,intent=uploadIntent;uploadInput.value='';uploadTargetId=null;uploadIntent='new';if(!file)return;if(intent==='supporting'){await storeSupportingDocument(file);return;}if(targetId){const item=sourceItems.find(record=>record.id===targetId);if(intent==='link'&&item){openSourceUpdateConfirmation(file,item);return;}await processDocumentFile(file,targetId);return;}const matches=documentUploadCandidates(file);if(matches.length){const top=matches[0],ambiguous=matches[1]&&top.score-matches[1].score<.12;if(ambiguous)openSourceCandidateChoice(file,matches.slice(0,5));else openSourceUpdateConfirmation(file,top.item);return;}await storeNewDocumentSource(file);});

    async function downloadDocument(item){const blob=await getDocumentBlob(item.id);if(!blob){showToast('Archivo no disponible','Este registro no conserva todavía el archivo original.');return;}const url=URL.createObjectURL(blob),link=document.createElement('a');link.href=url;link.download=item.originalName||item.name;link.click();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),1000);}
    async function downloadInsightRegister(item){let blob=await getInsightRegisterBlob(item.id).catch(()=>null);if(!blob)blob=await getPersistentSourceBlob(item.id,'insight-register').catch(()=>null);if(!blob){showToast('Excel no disponible','Este Excel histórico todavía no está guardado en el proyecto. En Documentos, abre el menú ⋯ y elige “Volver a vincular archivo”. Solo tendrás que hacerlo una vez.');return;}const url=URL.createObjectURL(blob),link=document.createElement('a');link.href=url;link.download=item.insightRegister?.file?.name||'Excel revisado.xlsx';link.click();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),1000);}
    function openSourceMetadataEditor(item){const inferredScope=sourceHasTransversalScopeMarker(item)?'transversal':'specific';const header=`<div class="drawer-kicker">Documento</div><h2>Editar datos</h2><p class="drawer-lead">Corrige la clasificación global del documento. El detalle de cada registro del Excel se conserva intacto.</p>`;const body=`<form id="source-edit-form" data-source-id="${item.id}"><div class="form-grid"><label class="form-field full">Nombre<input name="name" value="${escapeHtml(item.name)}"></label><label class="form-field">Tipo de análisis<select name="analysisType">${documentAnalysisTypeOptions(item)}</select></label><label class="form-field">Iniciativa<input name="initiative" value="${escapeHtml(item.initiative)}"></label><label class="form-field">Alcance<select name="documentScopeMode"><option value="specific" ${inferredScope==='specific'?'selected':''}>Específico</option><option value="transversal" ${inferredScope==='transversal'?'selected':''}>Transversal</option></select></label><label class="form-field">Producto<input name="product" value="${escapeHtml(item.product)}"></label><label class="form-field">Funnel<input name="funnel" value="${escapeHtml(item.funnel||'')}"></label><label class="form-field">Minifunnel<input name="minifunnel" value="${escapeHtml(item.minifunnel||'')}"></label><label class="form-field">Componente<input name="component" value="${escapeHtml(item.component)}"></label><label class="form-field">Fecha del documento<input name="exactDate" value="${escapeHtml(item.documentDate?.source==='manual'&&isSpecifiedMetadata(item.exactDate)?item.exactDate:documentDate(item))}" placeholder="Ej. sep 26, sept 2026 o 09/2026"></label></div><div class="form-actions"><button class="secondary-button" type="button" data-close-editor>Cancelar</button><button class="action-button" type="submit">Guardar cambios</button></div></form>`;openDrawer(header,body);}

    let pendingDelete=null,deleteReturnFocus=null;
    const deleteModal=document.getElementById('delete-modal');
    function openDelete(id){const item=sourceItems.find(value=>value.id===id);if(!item)return;deleteReturnFocus=document.activeElement;pendingDelete=id;const impact=learnings.filter(learning=>learning.sourceNames.includes(item.name)).length;document.getElementById('delete-copy').textContent=`“${item.name}” está relacionado con ${impact} ${impact===1?'learning':'learnings'}. Si lo eliminas, dejará de aparecer en esta vista.`;deleteModal.hidden=false;deleteModal.querySelector('[data-delete-cancel]').focus();}
    function closeDelete(){if(deleteModal.hidden)return;deleteModal.hidden=true;pendingDelete=null;if(deleteReturnFocus?.isConnected)deleteReturnFocus.focus();deleteReturnFocus=null;}
    document.querySelector('[data-delete-cancel]').addEventListener('click',closeDelete);
    document.querySelector('.modal-close').addEventListener('click',closeDelete);
    document.querySelector('[data-delete-confirm]').addEventListener('click',()=>{if(!pendingDelete)return;const id=pendingDelete,index=sourceItems.findIndex(value=>value.id===id);const name=sourceItems[index]?.name;if(index>=0)sourceItems.splice(index,1);deleteDocumentBlob(id).catch(()=>{});deleteInsightRegisterBlob(id).catch(()=>{});deleteDocumentAnalysis(id).catch(()=>{});closeDelete();safeStorageWrite();renderSources();showToast('Documento eliminado',name||'El documento ya no aparece en la lista.');});
    deleteModal.addEventListener('click',event=>{if(event.target===deleteModal)closeDelete();});
    document.getElementById('source-table').addEventListener('click',event=>{
      const action=event.target.closest('[data-doc-action]');
      if(!action){const row=event.target.closest('[data-doc-open]');if(row&&!event.target.closest('[data-action-menu],.action-menu'))openSource(row.dataset.docOpen);return;}
      event.stopPropagation();document.querySelectorAll('.action-menu').forEach(value=>value.classList.remove('open'));
      const item=sourceItems.find(value=>value.id===action.dataset.id);if(!item)return;
      if(action.dataset.docAction==='open')openSource(item.id);
      else if(action.dataset.docAction==='view')consultDocument(item.id);
      else if(action.dataset.docAction==='knowledge')openDocumentKnowledge(item.id);
      else if(action.dataset.docAction==='process'){
        if(!documentHydrationComplete||isDocumentPublished(item)||item.status==='Analizando')return;
        uploadTargetId=item.id;uploadIntent='analyze';uploadInput.click();
      }
      else if(action.dataset.docAction==='link-source'){
        if(!documentCanUpdateSource(item))return;
        uploadTargetId=item.id;uploadIntent='link';uploadInput.click();
      }
      else if(action.dataset.docAction==='add-register'){insightRegisterTargetId=item.id;insightRegisterUploadIntent='attach';insightRegisterInput.click();}
      else if(action.dataset.docAction==='relink-register'){insightRegisterTargetId=item.id;insightRegisterUploadIntent='relink';insightRegisterInput.click();}
      else if(action.dataset.docAction==='view-register')openInsightRegister(item);
      else if(action.dataset.docAction==='download')downloadDocument(item);
      else if(action.dataset.docAction==='edit')openSourceMetadataEditor(item);
      else if(action.dataset.docAction==='delete')openDelete(item.id);
    });
    document.getElementById('source-table').addEventListener('keydown',event=>{const row=event.target.closest('[data-doc-open]');if(row&&(event.key==='Enter'||event.key===' ')){event.preventDefault();openSource(row.dataset.docOpen);}});

    insightRegisterInput.addEventListener('change',async()=>{const file=insightRegisterInput.files?.[0],documentId=insightRegisterTargetId,intent=insightRegisterUploadIntent;insightRegisterInput.value='';insightRegisterTargetId=null;insightRegisterUploadIntent='attach';if(!file||!documentId)return;if(intent==='new'||documentId==='__new_excel__')await importInsightRegister(file);else if(intent==='relink')await relinkInsightRegisterFile(file,documentId);else await attachInsightRegister(file,documentId);});

    document.addEventListener('click',event=>{
      const componentEdit=event.target.closest('[data-learning-component-edit]');

    document.addEventListener('input',event=>{

    document.addEventListener('change',event=>{
      const taxonomyField=event.target.closest('[data-taxonomy-level]');if(taxonomyField){refreshTaxonomyEditor(taxonomyField.form,taxonomyField.dataset.taxonomyLevel);return;}
      if(event.target.closest('[data-cluster-choice],[data-relation-choice]')&&!reviewCanEdit(activeReviewDocument()))return;
      const clusterChoice=event.target.closest('[data-cluster-choice]');if(clusterChoice){const documentItem=activeReviewDocument();setClusterReviewDecision(documentItem,clusterChoice.dataset.clusterChoice,clusterChoice.value,'pending').then(()=>renderReview());return;}
      const choice=event.target.closest('[data-relation-choice]');if(!choice)return;
      const documentItem=activeReviewDocument(),relation=documentItem?.analysis?.relations?.find(item=>item.id===choice.dataset.relationChoice);
      if(relation){relation.decision=choice.value;relation.status='pending';touchReview(documentItem);safeStorageWrite();}
    });
    document.addEventListener('submit',event=>{
      if(event.target.id==='taxonomy-resolver-form'){event.preventDefault();showTaxonomyResolution(event.target);return;}
      if(event.target.id==='taxonomy-classification-form'){
        event.preventDefault();const form=event.target,error=form.querySelector('.taxonomy-validation');submitTaxonomyClassification(form).then(()=>{const type=form.dataset.objectType,id=form.dataset.objectId;closeDrawer();refreshAllTaxonomyFilters();renderLearnings();renderEvidence();renderExperiments();renderPatterns();renderTaxonomy();showToast('Clasificación actualizada','La corrección y su trazabilidad se han guardado sin modificar la fuente original.');if(type==='evidence')openEvidence(id);else if(type==='learning')openLearning(id);else if(type==='experiment')openExperiment(id);else openPattern(id);taxonomyAddDrawerAction(type,id);}).catch(reason=>{error.textContent=reason.message;error.hidden=false;});return;
      }
      if(event.target.id==='learning-component-edit-form'){
        event.preventDefault();
        const item=learnings.find(value=>value.id===event.target.dataset.learningId);if(!item)return;
        const data=new FormData(event.target);
        const selectedComponent=String(data.get('component')||'').trim();
        const previousComponent=String(item.component||'').trim();
        const nextComponent=selectedComponent?(canonicalComponentName(selectedComponent)||selectedComponent):previousComponent;
        const allowedDevices=new Set(['Desktop','Mobile','Desktop + mobile','No especificado']);
        const nextDevice=String(data.get('device')||'').trim();
        if(!allowedDevices.has(nextDevice)){showToast('Dispositivo no válido','Selecciona Desktop, Mobile, Desktop + mobile o No especificado.');return;}
        const previousDevice=String(item.device||'').trim();
        const changedAt=new Date().toISOString();
        if(nextComponent)item.component=nextComponent;
        item.device=nextDevice;
        if(Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(item,'components'))delete item.components;
        item.updatedAt=changedAt.slice(0,10);
        item.classificationHistory=Array.isArray(item.classificationHistory)?item.classificationHistory:[];
        if(normalizeText(previousComponent)!==normalizeText(nextComponent)){
          item.classificationHistory.push({field:'component',from:previousComponent,to:nextComponent,at:changedAt,origin:'Edición manual en learning'});
        }
        if(previousDevice!==nextDevice){
          item.classificationHistory.push({field:'device',from:previousDevice,to:nextDevice,at:changedAt,origin:'Edición manual en learning'});
        }
        if(!safeStorageWrite({project:true})){showToast('No se ha guardado','No se ha podido guardar la clasificación.');return;}
        refreshAllTaxonomyFilters?.();renderOverview();renderLearnings();renderExplore();openLearning(item.id);
        showToast('Clasificación actualizada',`${item.id}: ${nextComponent} · ${nextDevice}.`);return;
      }
      if(event.target.id==='learning-suggestion-edit-form'){
        event.preventDefault();
        const item=sourceItems.find(value=>value.id===event.target.dataset.sourceId),candidate=item?.insightRegister?.learningSuggestions?.candidates?.find(value=>value.id===event.target.dataset.candidateId);
        if(!item||!candidate)return;
        const data=new FormData(event.target),selectedRefs=[...new Set(data.getAll('rowRef').map(value=>String(value)))];
        if(!selectedRefs.length){showToast('Selecciona al menos un registro','Una propuesta necesita al menos un registro. Si no quieres asociarlos, usa “Mantener solo como evidencia”.');return;}
        const allEntries=learningSuggestionEntryRows(item,candidate),selectedSet=new Set(selectedRefs);
        const selectedEntries=allEntries.filter(entry=>selectedSet.has(String(entry.row?.sourceRow||entry.index+1)));
        const removedEntries=allEntries.filter(entry=>!selectedSet.has(String(entry.row?.sourceRow||entry.index+1)));
        candidate.rowRefs=learningSuggestionSourceRows(selectedEntries);
        candidate.evidence=learningSuggestionEvidence(selectedEntries,item);
        candidate.action=String(data.get('action')||candidate.action).trim();

        // Guardado robusto del learning objetivo.
        // El select visual del UI Kit puede mostrar una opción seleccionada aunque,
        // en algunos drawers dinámicos, el select nativo no llegue a reflejarla en FormData.
        const targetSelect=event.target.querySelector('select[name="targetLearningId"]');
        let targetLearningId=String(data.get('targetLearningId')||targetSelect?.value||'').trim();
        if(!targetLearningId&&targetSelect){
          const visibleLabel=String(targetSelect.nextElementSibling?.querySelector('.ds-select-value')?.textContent||'').trim();
          const visibleTarget=learnings.find(learning=>{
            const id=String(learning?.id||'').trim();
            const title=String(learning?.title||'').trim();
            return visibleLabel===title||visibleLabel===`${id} · ${title}`||(title&&visibleLabel.endsWith(`· ${title}`));
          });
          if(visibleTarget)targetLearningId=String(visibleTarget.id||'').trim();
        }
        if(!targetLearningId&&['Reforzar learning existente','Ampliar learning','Contradice learning'].includes(candidate.action)){
          const eligibleTargets=learnings.filter(learning=>!item.initiative||[learning.initiative,...(learning.initiatives||[])].some(value=>initiativeNameEquals(value,item.initiative)));
          if(eligibleTargets.length===1)targetLearningId=String(eligibleTargets[0].id||'').trim();
        }
        candidate.targetLearningId=targetLearningId;
        candidate.fingerprint=learningSuggestionHash(`${candidate.targetLearningId||candidate.action}|${candidate.rowRefs.join(',')}`);
        candidate.newLearningTitle=String(data.get('newLearningTitle')||'').trim();
        candidate.component=String(data.get('component')||'').trim();
        candidate.summary=learningSuggestionCountText(String(data.get('summary')||'').trim(),candidate.rowRefs.length);
        candidate.rationale=learningSuggestionCountText(candidate.rationale,candidate.rowRefs.length);
        candidate.impactUx=String(data.get('impactUx')||'Medio').trim();
        candidate.edited=true;
        if(removedEntries.length)appendLearningSuggestionSplit(item,removedEntries,candidate);
        if(!safeStorageWrite({project:true})){showToast('No se ha guardado','No se ha podido guardar la propuesta.');return;}
        openInsightRegister(item);
        showToast('Propuesta actualizada',removedEntries.length?`${candidate.rowRefs.length} registro(s) permanecen en la propuesta y ${removedEntries.length} quedan separados para revisión.`:'La sugerencia se ha editado. El learning todavía no se ha modificado.');return;
      }

      if(event.target.id==='insight-edit-form'){
        event.preventDefault();const documentItem=activeReviewDocument();if(!reviewCanEdit(documentItem))return;
        const data=new FormData(event.target),patch=Object.fromEntries(insightEditorialFields.filter(key=>data.has(key)).map(key=>[key,String(data.get(key)||'').trim()]));
        if(isDocumentPublished(documentItem))patch.publishEditorialChanges=data.has('publishEditorialChanges');
        saveAnalysisEdit(documentItem,event.target.dataset.insightId,patch).then(saved=>{
          if(!saved)return;closeDrawer();renderReview();showToast('Análisis guardado',pendingAnalysisChanges(documentItem).length?'Cambios pendientes de publicar. La base de conocimiento no ha cambiado.':'La edición se ha guardado. No se ha publicado nada.');
        });return;
      }
      if(event.target.id==='source-edit-form'){
        event.preventDefault();
        const item=sourceItems.find(value=>value.id===event.target.dataset.sourceId);if(!item)return;
        const data=new FormData(event.target),enteredDate=String(data.get('exactDate')||'').trim(),normalizedEnteredDate=normalizeText(enteredDate),clearDate=!enteredDate||['no especificada','no especificado','no indicada','no indicado'].includes(normalizedEnteredDate),manualDate=clearDate?null:temporalValue(enteredDate,'manual');
        if(!clearDate&&!manualDate){showToast('Fecha no válida','Usa un formato como “sep 26”, “sept 2026”, “09/26”, “09/2026” o “19/09/2026”.');return;}
        const previousInitiative=String(item.initiative||'').trim();
        const nextInitiative=String(data.get('initiative')||'').trim();
        const initiativeChanged=previousInitiative&&nextInitiative&&!initiativeNameEquals(previousInitiative,nextInitiative);
        const oldUsage=sourceItems.filter(source=>initiativeNameEquals(source.initiative,previousInitiative)||(source.initiatives||[]).some(value=>initiativeNameEquals(value,previousInitiative))).length;
        const nextAlreadyExists=sourceItems.some(source=>source.id!==item.id&&(initiativeNameEquals(source.initiative,nextInitiative)||(source.initiatives||[]).some(value=>initiativeNameEquals(value,nextInitiative))));
        const globalRename=Boolean(initiativeChanged&&oldUsage>1&&!nextAlreadyExists);
        ['name','product','funnel','minifunnel','screen','component'].forEach(key=>item[key]=String(data.get(key)||'').trim());
        item.analysisType=normalizeDocumentAnalysisType(data.get('analysisType'));
        item.documentScopeMode=String(data.get('documentScopeMode')||'specific')==='transversal'?'transversal':'specific';
        if(item.documentScopeMode==='transversal'){item.funnel='';item.minifunnel='';item.screen='';item.component='';item.components=[];}
        if(globalRename){
          renameInitiativeEverywhere(previousInitiative,nextInitiative);
        }else{
          item.initiative=nextInitiative;
          item.initiatives=nextInitiative?[nextInitiative]:[];
          syncInsightRegisterInitiative(item);
        }
        item.initiativeManual=true;
        const previousDocumentDate=temporalValue(item.documentDate||item.exactDate);
        const dateActuallyChanged=clearDate
          ? Boolean(previousDocumentDate)
          : !previousDocumentDate||previousDocumentDate.value!==manualDate.value||previousDocumentDate.precision!==manualDate.precision;
        if(dateActuallyChanged){
          item.exactDate=clearDate?'No especificada':enteredDate;
          item.documentDate=manualDate;
        }
        item.documentProfile=UXInsightRegister.documentProfile(item,{...(item.documentProfile||{}),title:item.name,analysisType:item.analysisType||documentAnalysisType(item),date:item.exactDate,initiative:item.initiative,primaryProduct:item.product,scope:item.documentScopeMode==='transversal'?'Transversal':''});
        refreshDocumentDateFilter();
        refreshCatalogMetadata();
        refreshInitiativeFilters(previousInitiative,nextInitiative);
        refreshAllTaxonomyFilters?.();
        const saved=safeStorageWrite();
        closeDrawer();
        renderSources();renderOverview();renderExplore();renderLearnings();renderEvidence();renderExperiments();renderPatterns();renderReview();renderTaxonomy();
        if(saved===false)showToast('No se ha podido guardar','La edición no ha quedado persistida.');
        else if(globalRename)showToast('Iniciativa renombrada',`“${previousInitiative}” ahora es “${nextInitiative}” en todo UX Intelligence.`);
        else showToast('Documento actualizado',`La clasificación y la fecha ${clearDate?'sin especificar':item.exactDate} se han guardado correctamente.`);
      }
    });

    const searchInput=document.getElementById('global-search');
    const overviewInput=document.getElementById('overview-search-input');
    const searchPopover=document.getElementById('search-popover');
    const overviewResults=document.getElementById('overview-search-results');
    function currentStructuredRecords(){
      try{
        const rows=globalThis.UXSimpleKnowledge?.allRecords?.(sourceItems)||[];
        return rows.map(row=>globalThis.UXGovernedTaxonomy?.normalizeRecord?.(row)||row);
      }catch(e){return [];}
    }
    const searchStopWords=new Set([
      'que','qué','sabemos','saber','del','de','la','las','el','los','un','una','unos','unas',
      'sobre','acerca','por','para','en','y','o','con','sin','me','dime','quiero','ver','hay',
      'tenemos','tengo','como','cómo','cual','cuál','cuales','cuáles'
    ]);

    const searchSynonymGroups=[
      ['mobile','movil','móvil','responsive'],
      ['desktop','escritorio'],
      ['autoservicio','autogestion','autogestión','self service','self-service'],
      ['reserva','reservas','gestionar reserva','gestion de reserva','gestión de reserva','mi reserva','mis reservas','mmb'],
      ['check-in','checkin','facturacion','facturación'],
      ['buscador','buscar','busqueda','búsqueda','search'],
      ['header','cabecera','navegacion','navegación'],
      ['calendario','fechas','fecha'],
      ['pasajeros','pasajero','pax'],
      ['ultima busqueda','última búsqueda','busqueda anterior','búsqueda anterior','retomar busqueda','retomar búsqueda'],
      ['cta','boton','botón','accion principal','acción principal'],
      ['banner','promocion','promoción'],
      ['ayuda','soporte'],
      ['impacto ux','impacto'],
      ['mercado','pais','país'],
      ['evidencia','registro','registros','hallazgo','insight'],
      ['learning','learnings','aprendizaje','aprendizajes','conocimiento']
    ];

    function searchTokens(query){
      const normalized=normalizeText(query)
        .replace(/[^\p{L}\p{N}+\-/]+/gu,' ')
        .replace(/\s+/g,' ')
        .trim();
      if(!normalized)return [];
      const raw=normalized.split(' ').filter(Boolean);
      return [...new Set(raw.filter(token=>token.length>1&&!searchStopWords.has(token)))];
    }

    function expandSearchTokens(tokens){
      const expanded=new Set(tokens);
      const normalizedGroups=searchSynonymGroups.map(group=>group.map(normalizeText));
      tokens.forEach(token=>{
        normalizedGroups.forEach(group=>{
          if(group.some(term=>term===token||term.split(' ').includes(token))){
            group.forEach(term=>term.split(' ').forEach(piece=>{if(piece.length>1&&!searchStopWords.has(piece))expanded.add(piece);}));
          }
        });
      });
      return [...expanded];
    }

    function searchableArray(value){
      if(Array.isArray(value))return value.flatMap(searchableArray);
      if(value&&typeof value==='object')return Object.values(value).flatMap(searchableArray);
      return [String(value??'')];
    }

    function searchBlob(parts){
      return normalizeText(searchableArray(parts).join(' ')).replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim();
    }

    function tokenCoverage(blob,tokens){
      if(!tokens.length)return 0;
      const matched=tokens.filter(token=>blob.includes(token)).length;
      return matched/tokens.length;
    }

    function weightedScore(query,tokens,sections){
      const nq=normalizeText(query).trim();
      let score=0;
      let covered=new Set();
      sections.forEach(({value,weight=1})=>{
        const blob=searchBlob(value);
        if(!blob)return;
        if(nq&&blob.includes(nq))score+=weight*4;
        tokens.forEach(token=>{
          if(blob.includes(token)){score+=weight;covered.add(token);}
        });
      });
      if(tokens.length){
        const coverage=covered.size/tokens.length;
        if(coverage===1)score+=18;
        else if(coverage>=.67)score+=7;
        else if(coverage<.5)score-=4;
      }
      return score;
    }

    function searchGroups(query){
      const q=normalizeText(query).trim();
      const baseTokens=searchTokens(query);
      if(q.length<2||!baseTokens.length)return [];
      const tokens=expandSearchTokens(baseTokens);
      const structured=currentStructuredRecords();

      const learningMatches=learnings.map(item=>{
        const score=weightedScore(query,tokens,[
          {value:[item.title],weight:8},
          {value:[item.know,item.text],weight:6},
          {value:[item.applications,item.forEvidence,item.contrary,item.noProof,item.open],weight:3},
          {value:[item.product,item.funnel,item.minifunnel,item.screen,item.component,item.components,item.element,item.device,item.market,item.impactUx,item.initiative,item.initiatives,item.area],weight:4},
          {value:[item.sourceNames],weight:2}
        ]);
        return {item,score};
      }).filter(entry=>entry.score>0)
        .sort((a,b)=>b.score-a.score||String(a.item.title).localeCompare(String(b.item.title),'es'))
        .slice(0,5)
        .map(entry=>entry.item);

      const structuredMatches=structured.map(item=>{
        const score=weightedScore(query,tokens,[
          {value:[item.insight],weight:7},
          {value:[item.uxImplication||item.implication,item.recommendation,item.openQuestion],weight:5},
          {value:[item.evidence],weight:4},
          {value:[item.product,item.funnel,item.minifunnel,item.screen,item.component,item.element,item.device,item.market,item.region,item.origin,item.recordType,item.uxLine,item.initiative],weight:3}
        ]);
        return {item,score};
      }).filter(entry=>entry.score>0)
        .sort((a,b)=>b.score-a.score||String(a.item.insight||'').localeCompare(String(b.item.insight||''),'es'))
        .slice(0,6)
        .map(entry=>entry.item);

      const documentMatches=sourceItems.map(item=>{
        const score=weightedScore(query,tokens,[
          {value:[item.name,item.originalName],weight:7},
          {value:[item.initiative,item.initiatives,item.product,item.funnel,item.minifunnel,item.screen,item.component,item.element,item.type],weight:4},
          {value:[searchContext(item)],weight:3}
        ]);
        return {item,score};
      }).filter(entry=>entry.score>0)
        .sort((a,b)=>b.score-a.score||String(a.item.name).localeCompare(String(b.item.name),'es'))
        .slice(0,5)
        .map(entry=>entry.item);

      const contexts=taxonomyContexts().map(item=>{
        const score=weightedScore(query,tokens,[
          {value:[pathOf(item)],weight:5},
          {value:[item.product,item.screen,item.component,item.element],weight:4}
        ]);
        return {item,score};
      }).filter(entry=>entry.score>0)
        .sort((a,b)=>b.score-a.score||pathOf(a.item).localeCompare(pathOf(b.item),'es'))
        .slice(0,5)
        .map(entry=>({...entry.item,id:JSON.stringify(entry.item)}));

      const contextCount=item=>structured.filter(row=>{
        const current=globalThis.UXGovernedTaxonomy?.normalizeRecord?.(row)||row;
        return ['product','funnel','minifunnel','component'].every(level=>
          !item[level]||normalizeText(current[level])===normalizeText(item[level])
        );
      }).length;

      return [
        ...(learningMatches.length?[{
          label:'Learnings consolidados',
          kind:'learning',
          items:learningMatches,
          title:item=>item.title,
          meta:item=>`${pathOf(item)||'Sin contexto'} · Impacto UX ${String(item.impactUx||'No informado').toLowerCase()}`
        }]:[]),
        ...(structuredMatches.length?[{
          label:'Registros relacionados',
          kind:'simple-record',
          items:structuredMatches,
          title:item=>item.insight||item.evidence||'Registro UX',
          meta:item=>`${compactJourneyValues(item).join(' › ')||'Sin contexto'} · ${item.recordType||'Registro UX'}`
        }]:[]),
        ...(documentMatches.length?[{
          label:'Fuentes',
          kind:'source',
          items:documentMatches,
          title:item=>item.name,
          meta:item=>`${documentApplicationPath(item)||item.initiative||'Sin clasificar'} · ${item.supportingDocument?'PDF de apoyo':item.simpleKnowledgeActive&&item.insightRegister?'Excel revisado':item.type}`
        }]:[]),
        ...(contexts.length?[{
          label:'Contextos de producto',
          kind:'context',
          items:contexts,
          title:item=>pathOf(item),
          meta:item=>{const count=contextCount(item);return count?`${count} ${count===1?'registro disponible':'registros disponibles'} en Explorar`:'Sin registros cargados para este contexto';}
        }]:[])
      ];
    }

    function searchMarkup(query){
      const groups=searchGroups(query);
      return groups.length
        ? groups.map(group=>`<div class="search-group" role="presentation">${escapeHtml(group.label)}</div>${group.items.map(item=>`<button type="button" role="option" tabindex="-1" class="search-result" data-search-kind="${group.kind}" data-search-id="${escapeHtml(item.id)}"><strong>${escapeHtml(group.title(item))}</strong><span>${escapeHtml(group.meta(item))}</span></button>`).join('')}`).join('')
        : '<div class="empty-state">No hay conocimiento relacionado con esta consulta. Prueba con otro término o contexto.</div>';
    }

    function searchOptions(popover){return [...popover.querySelectorAll('[data-search-kind]')];}
    function focusSearchOption(popover,index){
      const options=searchOptions(popover);
      if(!options.length)return;
      const safeIndex=(index+options.length)%options.length;
      options.forEach((option,i)=>option.tabIndex=i===safeIndex?0:-1);
      options[safeIndex].focus();
    }
    function searchKeyboard(event,input,popover){
      if(event.key==='Escape'){closeSearch();input.focus();return;}
      const options=searchOptions(popover);
      if(!options.length)return;
      const current=options.indexOf(document.activeElement);
      if(event.key==='ArrowDown'){
        event.preventDefault();
        focusSearchOption(popover,current<0?0:current+1);
      }else if(event.key==='ArrowUp'){
        event.preventDefault();
        focusSearchOption(popover,current<0?options.length-1:current-1);
      }else if(event.key==='Enter'&&document.activeElement===input&&popover.classList.contains('open')){
        event.preventDefault();
        options[0]?.click();
      }
    }

    function renderSearch(input,popover){
      const query=input.value;
      input.parentElement.querySelector('.search-clear')?.classList.toggle('visible',Boolean(query));
      if(query.trim().length<2){
        popover.classList.remove('open');
        popover.innerHTML='';
        input.setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');
        return;
      }
      popover.innerHTML=searchMarkup(query);
      popover.classList.add('open');
      input.setAttribute('aria-expanded','true');
    }
    function closeSearch(){searchPopover.classList.remove('open');overviewResults?.classList.remove('open');searchInput.setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');overviewInput?.setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');}
    searchInput.addEventListener('input',()=>renderSearch(searchInput,searchPopover));
    overviewInput?.addEventListener('input',()=>renderSearch(overviewInput,overviewResults));
    searchInput.addEventListener('keydown',event=>searchKeyboard(event,searchInput,searchPopover));
    overviewInput?.addEventListener('keydown',event=>searchKeyboard(event,overviewInput,overviewResults));
    document.querySelectorAll('.search-clear').forEach(button=>button.addEventListener('click',()=>{const input=button.parentElement.querySelector('input');input.value='';button.classList.remove('visible');closeSearch();input.focus();}));
    function chooseSearch(event){
      const result=event.target.closest('[data-search-kind]');if(!result)return;closeSearch();
      if(result.dataset.searchKind==='simple-record'){showPanel('explore');(globalThis.__uxOpenExploreRecord||globalThis.UXSimpleKnowledge?.openById)?.(result.dataset.searchId);}
      else if(result.dataset.searchKind==='context')goToKnowledgeContext(JSON.parse(result.dataset.searchId));
      else if(result.dataset.searchKind==='learning'){showPanel('learnings');openLearning(result.dataset.searchId);taxonomyAddDrawerAction('learning',result.dataset.searchId);}
      else if(result.dataset.searchKind==='evidence'){showPanel('evidence');openEvidence(result.dataset.searchId);taxonomyAddDrawerAction('evidence',result.dataset.searchId);}
      else if(result.dataset.searchKind==='pattern'){showPanel('patterns');openPattern(result.dataset.searchId);taxonomyAddDrawerAction('pattern',result.dataset.searchId);}
      else if(result.dataset.searchKind==='knowledge')openKnowledgeUnit(result.dataset.searchId);
      else {showPanel('sources');openSource(result.dataset.searchId);}
    }
    searchPopover.addEventListener('click',chooseSearch);overviewResults?.addEventListener('click',chooseSearch);
    document.addEventListener('click',event=>{if(!event.target.closest('.global-search')&&!event.target.closest('.overview-search'))closeSearch();});
  </script>

<script>
(function(){
  function countInsightRows(){
    try{
      if (Array.isArray(globalThis.sourceItems)) {
        return globalThis.sourceItems.reduce((sum,item)=>sum + (item?.insightRegister?.rows?.length || 0),0);
      }
      return 0;
    }catch(e){ return 0; }
  }
  function updateBaseCounters(){
    // V6: Resumen ya calcula sus métricas con la semántica actual
    // (registros, Excel revisados, PDF de apoyo e iniciativas). No sobrescribirlas
    // con los contadores heredados de la arquitectura anterior.
    try{ if(typeof renderOverview==='function') renderOverview(); }catch(e){}
  }

  function text(v){return String(v??'').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim();}
  function csvCell(v){return '"' + text(v).replace(/"/g,'""') + '"';}

  function exportExplore(){
    const root=document.getElementById('explore-content');
    if(!root){ alert('No hay resultados disponibles para exportar.'); return; }
    const candidates=[...root.querySelectorAll('article, details, .cluster-card, .compact-item, .knowledge-card, [data-detail], [data-learning]')];
    const rows=[];
    const seen=new Set();
    for(const el of candidates){
      const t=text(el.innerText);
      if(!t || t.length<20 || seen.has(t)) continue;
      seen.add(t);
      rows.push([t]);
    }
    if(!rows.length){
      alert('No hay resultados visibles para exportar con los filtros actuales.');
      return;
    }
    const csv=[['Resultado visible'],...rows].map(r=>r.map(csvCell).join(';')).join('\n');
    const blob=new Blob(["\ufeff"+csv],{type:'text/csv;charset=utf-8;'});
    const url=URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a=document.createElement('a');
    a.href=url;
    a.download='UX_Intelligence_resultados_filtrados.csv';
    a.click();
    setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),1000);
  }

  document.addEventListener('click',e=>{
    const jump=e.target.closest('[data-panel-jump]');
    if(jump && typeof showPanel==='function'){
      e.preventDefault();
      showPanel(jump.dataset.panelJump);
    }
    if(e.target.closest('#export-explore-xlsx')) exportExplore();
  });

  window.addEventListener('load',()=>setTimeout(updateBaseCounters,250));
  setTimeout(updateBaseCounters,700);
})();
</script>

  const solutionInsightKey=value=>norm(value)
    .normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,'')
    .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g,' ')
    .replace(/\s+/g,' ')
    .trim();
  const REVIEWED_SOLUTION_BY_NORMALIZED_INSIGHT=new Map(
    Object.entries(REVIEWED_SOLUTION_BY_INSIGHT).map(([key,value])=>[solutionInsightKey(key),value])
  );
  function reviewedSolutionMeta(insight){
    return REVIEWED_SOLUTION_BY_NORMALIZED_INSIGHT.get(solutionInsightKey(insight))||null;
  }
  function withSolutionMeta(record){
    const reviewed=reviewedSolutionMeta(record?.insight)||{};
    const currentDesignRef=clean(record?.designRef);
    const currentDesignHasUrl=/https?:\/\//i.test(currentDesignRef);
    return {...record,
      solutionId:clean(record?.solutionId)||reviewed.solutionId||'',
      solutionUx:clean(record?.solutionUx)||reviewed.solutionUx||'',
      designRationale:clean(record?.designRationale)||reviewed.designRationale||'',
      // Un texto de interfaz como "Abrir diseño ↗" no es una referencia.
      // Si existe una URL revisada, debe prevalecer para UI y exportaciones.
      designRef:(currentDesignHasUrl?currentDesignRef:'')||reviewed.designRef||currentDesignRef||'',
      solutionStatus:clean(record?.solutionStatus)||reviewed.solutionStatus||'',
      solutionRelation:clean(record?.solutionRelation)||reviewed.solutionRelation||''
    };
  }

  function normalizeOrigins(value){
    const raw=Array.isArray(value)?value:[value];
    const out=[];
    raw.flatMap(item=>String(item??'').split(/[;|\n]+/)).map(clean).filter(Boolean).forEach(item=>{
      const normalized=norm(item);
      let mapped=originAtomicMap.get(normalized);
      if(mapped===undefined){
        // Los nombres de consultoras, estudios o referencias concretas se consideran Desk research.
        if(/\b(mckinsey|bcg|deloitte|iata|collinson|km malta|investigación académica|investigacion academica|desk research)\b/i.test(item)){
          mapped=['Desk research'];
        }else{
          mapped=[];
        }
      }
      mapped.filter(label=>originValues.some(allowed=>norm(allowed)===norm(label))).forEach(label=>{
        if(!out.some(existing=>norm(existing)===norm(label)))out.push(label);
      });
    });
return out;
  }

  function canonicalFromMap(value,map,allowed){
    const raw=clean(value);if(!raw)return '';
    const mapped=map.get(norm(raw));
    if(mapped&&(!allowed||allowed.some(item=>norm(item)===norm(mapped))))return mapped;
    if(allowed){const canonical=allowed.find(item=>norm(item)===norm(raw));if(canonical)return canonical;}
    return raw;
  }
  function normalizeRecordType(value){return canonicalFromMap(value,recordTypeAtomicMap,recordTypeValues);}
  function normalizeDevice(value){return canonicalFromMap(value,deviceAtomicMap,deviceValues);}
  function normalizeMarket(value){return canonicalFromMap(value,marketAtomicMap,marketValues);}
  function normalizeRegion(value){return canonicalFromMap(value,regionAtomicMap,regionValues);}
  function normalizeUserState(value){return canonicalFromMap(value,userStateAtomicMap);}
  function normalizeResult(value){return canonicalFromMap(value,resultAtomicMap);}
  function normalizeImplementation(value){return canonicalFromMap(value,implementationAtomicMap);}
  function normalizePlatform(value){return canonicalFromMap(value,platformAtomicMap);}

  function normalizeUxLines(value){
    const raw=Array.isArray(value)?value:[value];const out=[];
    raw.flatMap(item=>String(item??'').split(/[;|·\n]+/)).map(clean).filter(Boolean).forEach(item=>{
      const mapped=uxLineAtomicMap.get(norm(item))||[];
      mapped.filter(label=>uxDimensionValues.some(allowed=>norm(allowed)===norm(label))).forEach(label=>{
        if(!out.some(existing=>norm(existing)===norm(label)))out.push(label);
      });
    });
    return out.slice(0,2);
  }
  function deriveRegion(market){const value=normalizeMarket(market);if(!value)return '';return regionByMarket.get(norm(value))||(/multimercado|no especificado/i.test(value)?'No especificado':'');}
