applyBorder="1" applyAlignment="1"><alignment vertical="top" horizontal="center" wrapText="1"/></xf><xf numFmtId="0" fontId="6" fillId="5" borderId="1" xfId="0" applyFont="1" applyFill="1" applyBorder="1" applyAlignment="1"><alignment vertical="top" horizontal="center" wrapText="1"/></xf></cellXfs><cellStyles count="1"><cellStyle name="Normal" xfId="0" builtinId="0"/></cellStyles></styleSheet>`;
    const workbook=`<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?><workbook xmlns="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/spreadsheetml/2006/main" xmlns:r="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships"><bookViews><workbookView xWindow="0" yWindow="0" windowWidth="24000" windowHeight="12000"/></bookViews><sheets><sheet name="CRO" sheetId="1" r:id="rId1"/></sheets><calcPr calcId="191029"/></workbook>`;
    const workbookRels=`<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?><Relationships xmlns="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/relationships"><Relationship Id="rId1" Type="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships/worksheet" Target="worksheets/sheet1.xml"/><Relationship Id="rId2" Type="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships/styles" Target="styles.xml"/></Relationships>`;
    const rootRels=`<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?><Relationships xmlns="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/relationships"><Relationship Id="rId1" Type="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships/officeDocument" Target="xl/workbook.xml"/><Relationship Id="rId2" Type="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/relationships/metadata/core-properties" Target="docProps/core.xml"/><Relationship Id="rId3" Type="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships/extended-properties" Target="docProps/app.xml"/></Relationships>`;
    const contentTypes=`<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?><Types xmlns="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/content-types"><Default Extension="rels" ContentType="application/vnd.openxmlformats-package.relationships+xml"/><Default Extension="xml" ContentType="application/xml"/><Override PartName="/xl/workbook.xml" ContentType="application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet.main+xml"/><Override PartName="/xl/worksheets/sheet1.xml" ContentType="application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.worksheet+xml"/><Override PartName="/xl/styles.xml" ContentType="application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.styles+xml"/><Override PartName="/docProps/core.xml" ContentType="application/vnd.openxmlformats-package.core-properties+xml"/><Override PartName="/docProps/app.xml" ContentType="application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.extended-properties+xml"/></Types>`;
    const now=new Date().toISOString();
    const core=`<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?><cp:coreProperties xmlns:cp="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/metadata/core-properties" xmlns:dc="http://purl.org/dc/elements/1.1/" xmlns:dcterms="http://purl.org/dc/terms/" xmlns:dcmitype="http://purl.org/dc/dcmitype/" xmlns:xsi="http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema-instance"><dc:creator>Air Europa · UX Intelligence</dc:creator><cp:lastModifiedBy>Air Europa · UX Intelligence</cp:lastModifiedBy><dcterms:created xsi:type="dcterms:W3CDTF">${now}</dcterms:created><dcterms:modified xsi:type="dcterms:W3CDTF">${now}</dcterms:modified><dc:title>UX Intelligence · Export CRO</dc:title></cp:coreProperties>`;
    const app=`<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?><Properties xmlns="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/extended-properties" xmlns:vt="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/docPropsVTypes"><Application>Air Europa UX Intelligence</Application><AppVersion>1.0</AppVersion></Properties>`;
    return croXlsxZipStore([
      ['[Content_Types].xml',contentTypes],['_rels/.rels',rootRels],['docProps/app.xml',app],['docProps/core.xml',core],['xl/workbook.xml',workbook],['xl/_rels/workbook.xml.rels',workbookRels],['xl/styles.xml',styles],['xl/worksheets/sheet1.xml',sheet],['xl/worksheets/_rels/sheet1.xml.rels',sheetRels]
    ]);
  }
  function exportFilteredCro(){
    if(!filtered.length){alert('No hay resultados para exportar.');return;}
    const cols=[
      ['Iniciativa','initiative'],['Producto','product'],['Funnel','funnel'],['Minifunnel','minifunnel'],['Componente','component'],
      ['Fecha de incorporación','incorporationDateDisplay'],
      ['Dispositivo','device'],['Tipo de registro','recordType'],['Insight / hallazgo','insight'],['Evidencia','evidence'],
      ['Posible respuesta UX','recommendation'],['Impacto UX','impactUx'],['Tipo de análisis','origin'],['Dimensión','uxLine'],
      ['ID solución UX','solutionId'],['Solución UX propuesta','solutionUx'],['Por qué se plantea','designRationale'],
      ['Estado de la solución','solutionStatus'],['Diseño / referencia','designRef']
    ].map(([label,key])=>({label,key}));
    const bytes=croXlsxBuild(filtered,cols);
    const blob=new Blob([bytes],{type:'application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet'});
    const url=URL.createObjectURL(blob),link=document.createElement('a');
    link.href=url;link.download='UX_Intelligence_CRO_resultados_filtrados.xlsx';link.click();
    setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),1000);
  }

  let initialized=false;

  function renderUxLineMultiselect(){
    const root=document.getElementById('uxs-ux-line'),menu=document.getElementById('uxs-ux-line-menu'),trigger=document.getElementById('uxs-ux-line-trigger');
    if(!root||!menu||!trigger)return;
    const options=filterOptions('uxLine'),selected=Array.isArray(filters.uxLine)?filters.uxLine:[];
    menu.innerHTML=options.length?options.map(value=>{const checked=selected.some(item=>norm(item)===norm(value));return `<label class="ux-line-option" role="option" aria-selected="${checked}"><input type="checkbox" value="${escapeHtml(value)}" ${checked?'checked':''}><span>${escapeHtml(value)}</span></label>`;}).join(''):'<p class="ux-line-empty">Sin dimensiones UX disponibles</p>';
    const value=trigger.querySelector('.ux-line-trigger-value');
    if(value)value.textContent=!selected.length?'Todos':selected.length===1?selected[0]:`${selected[0]} +${selected.length-1}`;
  }
  function setUxLineOpen(open){const root=document.getElementById('uxs-ux-line'),menu=document.getElementById('uxs-ux-line-menu'),trigger=document.getElementById('uxs-ux-line-trigger');if(!root||!menu||!trigger)return;root.classList.toggle('is-open',open);menu.hidden=!open;trigger.setAttribute('aria-expanded',String(open));}

  function publishSharedRecords(){
    globalThis.UXSimpleKnowledge=globalThis.UXSimpleKnowledge||{};
    globalThis.UXSimpleKnowledge.currentNormalizedRecords=()=>records.map(record=>({...record}));
  }

  function refresh(){
    records=normalizedRecords().map(record=>withSolutionMeta(globalThis.UXGovernedTaxonomy?.normalizeRecord?.(record)||record));
    publishSharedRecords();
    try{if(typeof renderOverview==='function')renderOverview();}catch(_){}
    ids.forEach(([field,id])=>fillSelect(id,field,field));
    try{globalThis.__dsSyncSelects?.();}catch(_){}
    renderUxLineMultiselect();
    apply();
  }

  globalThis.__uxSimpleExploreRefresh=refresh;

  function init(){
    if(initialized){ refresh(); return; }
    initialized=true;
    records=normalizedRecords().map(record=>withSolutionMeta(globalThis.UXGovernedTaxonomy?.normalizeRecord?.(record)||record));
    publishSharedRecords();
    try{if(typeof renderOverview==='function')renderOverview();}catch(_){}
    ids.forEach(([field,id])=>fillSelect(id,field,field));
    renderUxLineMultiselect();
    ids.forEach(([field,id])=>{
      const el=document.getElementById(id); if(!el)return;
      el.addEventListener('change',()=>{
        filters[field]=el.value;
        if(field==='initiative'){
          filters.product='';
          filters.funnel='';
          filters.minifunnel='';
          filters.component='';
          fillSelect('uxs-product','product','product');
          fillSelect('uxs-funnel','funnel','funnel');
          fillSelect('uxs-minifunnel','minifunnel','minifunnel');
          fillSelect('uxs-component','component','component');
          try{globalThis.__dsSyncSelects?.();}catch(_){}
        }else if(field==='product'){
          filters.funnel='';
          filters.minifunnel='';
          filters.component='';
          fillSelect('uxs-funnel','funnel','funnel');
          fillSelect('uxs-minifunnel','minifunnel','minifunnel');
          fillSelect('uxs-component','component','component');
          try{globalThis.__dsSyncSelects?.();}catch(_){}
        }else if(field==='funnel'){
          filters.minifunnel='';
          filters.component='';
          fillSelect('uxs-minifunnel','minifunnel','minifunnel');
          fillSelect('uxs-component','component','component');
          try{globalThis.__dsSyncSelects?.();}catch(_){}
        }else if(field==='minifunnel'){
          filters.component='';
          fillSelect('uxs-component','component','component');
          try{globalThis.__dsSyncSelects?.();}catch(_){}
        }
        apply();
      });
    });
    document.getElementById('uxs-ux-line-trigger')?.addEventListener('click',event=>{event.preventDefault();const root=document.getElementById('uxs-ux-line');setUxLineOpen(!root?.classList.contains('is-open'));});
    document.getElementById('uxs-ux-line-menu')?.addEventListener('change',event=>{const input=event.target.closest('input[type="checkbox"]');if(!input)return;const selected=new Set((filters.uxLine||[]).map(norm));if(input.checked)selected.add(norm(input.value));else selected.delete(norm(input.value));filters.uxLine=filterOptions('uxLine').filter(value=>selected.has(norm(value)));renderUxLineMultiselect();setUxLineOpen(true);apply();});
    document.addEventListener('click',event=>{if(!event.target.closest('#uxs-ux-line'))setUxLineOpen(false);});
    document.getElementById('uxs-ux-line')?.addEventListener('keydown',event=>{if(event.key==='Escape')setUxLineOpen(false);});
    document.getElementById('uxs-design')?.addEventListener('change',event=>{filters.design=event.target.value||'';apply();});
    document.getElementById('uxs-sort')?.addEventListener('change',apply);
    document.getElementById('uxs-included-from')?.addEventListener('change',apply);
    document.getElementById('uxs-included-to')?.addEventListener('change',apply);
    document.getElementById('uxs-more-filters')?.addEventListener('click',()=>{
      const sec=document.getElementById('uxs-secondary');
      const btn=document.getElementById('uxs-more-filters');
      sec.hidden=!sec.hidden;
      btn.setAttribute('aria-expanded',String(!sec.hidden));
      btn.textContent=sec.hidden?'Más filtros':'Menos filtros';
    });
    document.getElementById('uxs-export')?.addEventListener('click',exportFiltered);
    document.getElementById('uxs-export-cro')?.addEventListener('click',exportFilteredCro);
    document.getElementById('uxs-active-filters')?.addEventListener('click',e=>{
      const remove=e.target.closest('[data-uxs-remove]');
      if(remove){
        const field=remove.dataset.uxsRemove;
        filters[field]='';
        if(field==='product'){filters.funnel='';filters.minifunnel='';filters.component='';}
        if(field==='funnel'){filters.minifunnel='';filters.component='';}
        if(field==='minifunnel')filters.component='';
        ids.forEach(([name,id])=>fillSelect(id,name,name));
        try{globalThis.__dsSyncSelects?.();}catch(_){}
        apply();
        return;
      }
      const removeDesign=e.target.closest('[data-uxs-remove-design]');
      if(removeDesign){filters.design='';const select=document.getElementById('uxs-design');if(select)select.value='';apply();return;}
      const removeLine=e.target.closest('[data-uxs-remove-line]');
      if(removeLine){filters.uxLine=(filters.uxLine||[]).filter(value=>norm(value)!==norm(removeLine.dataset.uxsRemoveLine));renderUxLineMultiselect();apply();return;}
      const removeDate=e.target.closest('[data-uxs-remove-date]');
      if(removeDate){const input=document.getElementById(removeDate.dataset.uxsRemoveDate);if(input){input.value='';input.dispatchEvent(new Event('change',{bubbles:true}));}apply();return;}
      if(e.target.closest('[data-uxs-clear]')){
        ids.forEach(([field,id])=>{
          filters[field]='';
          const select=document.getElementById(id);
          if(select)select.value='';
        });
        filters.uxLine=[];renderUxLineMultiselect();
        filters.design='';const designSelect=document.getElementById('uxs-design');if(designSelect)designSelect.value='';
        ids.forEach(([name,id])=>fillSelect(id,name,name));
        try{globalThis.__dsSyncSelects?.();}catch(_){}
        const from=document.getElementById('uxs-included-from'),to=document.getElementById('uxs-included-to');
        if(from){from.value='';from.dispatchEvent(new Event('change',{bubbles:true}));}
        if(to){to.value='';to.dispatchEvent(new Event('change',{bubbles:true}));}
        apply();
      }
    });
    document.getElementById('uxs-results-list')?.addEventListener('click',e=>{
      const row=e.target.closest('[data-uxs-id]'); if(row)openDetail(row.dataset.uxsId);
    });
    document.getElementById('uxs-results-list')?.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{
      if((e.key==='Enter'||e.key===' ')&&e.target.closest('[data-uxs-id]')){
        e.preventDefault();openDetail(e.target.closest('[data-uxs-id]').dataset.uxsId);
      }
    });
    document.getElementById('uxs-pagination')?.addEventListener('click',e=>{
      const button=e.target.closest('[data-page-target="explore"]');
      if(!button||button.disabled)return;
      explorePage=Number(button.dataset.page)||1;
      render();
      const first=document.getElementById('uxs-results-list')?.querySelector('[data-uxs-id]');
      first?.focus({preventScroll:true});
      first?.scrollIntoView({block:'nearest'});
    });
    apply();
  }

  window.addEventListener('load',()=>{
    setTimeout(init,350);
    setTimeout(refresh,1200);
  });
})();
</script>


<script id="v6-design-system-selects-script">
(function(){
  let uid=0;
  const instances=new Set();
  function closeAll(except){
    instances.forEach(api=>{if(api!==except)api.close(false);});
  }
  function enhance(select){
    if(!select||select.dataset.dsSelect==='1'||select.multiple)return;
    select.dataset.dsSelect='1';
    select.classList.add('ds-native-select');
    const wrap=document.createElement('div');wrap.className='ds-select';
    const trigger=document.createElement('div');trigger.className='ds-select-trigger';trigger.tabIndex=select.disabled?-1:0;trigger.setAttribute('role','combobox');trigger.setAttribute('aria-haspopup','listbox');trigger.setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');trigger.setAttribute('aria-disabled',String(Boolean(select.disabled)));
    const value=document.createElement('span');value.className='ds-select-value';
    const chevron=document.createElement('span');chevron.className='ds-select-chevron';chevron.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
    const menu=document.createElement('div');menu.className='ds-select-menu';menu.setAttribute('role','listbox');
    const listId=`ds-select-list-${++uid}`;menu.id=listId;trigger.setAttribute('aria-controls',listId);
    trigger.append(value,chevron);wrap.append(trigger,menu);select.insertAdjacentElement('afterend',wrap);
    let optionNodes=[];
    function selectedIndex(){return Math.max(0,select.selectedIndex);}
    function renderOptions(){
      menu.innerHTML='';optionNodes=[];
      [...select.options].forEach((opt,index)=>{
        const row=document.createElement('div');row.className='ds-select-option';row.setAttribute('role','option');row.dataset.index=String(index);row.tabIndex=-1;row.textContent=opt.textContent||opt.label||opt.value;row.classList.toggle('is-selected',index===selectedIndex());row.classList.toggle('is-disabled',Boolean(opt.disabled));row.setAttribute('aria-selected',String(index===selectedIndex()));if(opt.disabled)row.setAttribute('aria-disabled','true');menu.appendChild(row);optionNodes.push(row);
      });
    }
    function sync(){
      const opt=select.options[selectedIndex()]||select.options[0];value.textContent=opt?.textContent||'Seleccionar';trigger.tabIndex=select.disabled?-1:0;trigger.setAttribute('aria-disabled',String(Boolean(select.disabled)));renderOptions();
    }
    function focusOption(index){
      const valid=optionNodes.filter(node=>!node.classList.contains('is-disabled'));if(!valid.length)return;
      const requested=optionNodes[index];const target=requested&&!requested.classList.contains('is-disabled')?requested:valid[0];target.focus({preventScroll:true});target.scrollIntoView({block:'nearest'});
    }
    function open(focusSelected=false){if(select.disabled)return;closeAll(api);wrap.classList.add('is-open');trigger.setAttribute('aria-expanded','true');renderOptions();if(focusSelected)setTimeout(()=>focusOption(selectedIndex()),0);}
    function close(returnFocus=false){wrap.classList.remove('is-open');trigger.setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');if(returnFocus)trigger.focus({preventScroll:true});}
    function choose(index){const opt=select.options[index];if(!opt||opt.disabled)return;select.selectedIndex=index;select.dispatchEvent(new Event('change',{bubbles:true}));sync();close(true);}
    trigger.addEventListener('click',event=>{event.preventDefault();event.stopPropagation();wrap.classList.contains('is-open')?close(false):open(false);});
    trigger.addEventListener('keydown',event=>{
      if(['Enter',' ','ArrowDown','ArrowUp'].includes(event.key)){event.preventDefault();open(true);}else if(event.key==='Escape'){close(false);}
    });
    menu.addEventListener('click',event=>{const row=event.target.closest('.ds-select-option');if(!row)return;event.preventDefault();event.stopPropagation();choose(Number(row.dataset.index));});
    menu.addEventListener('keydown',event=>{
      const row=event.target.closest('.ds-select-option');if(!row)return;const index=Number(row.dataset.index);
      if(event.key==='Enter'||event.key===' '){event.preventDefault();choose(index);return;}
      if(event.key==='Escape'){event.preventDefault();close(true);return;}
      if(event.key==='ArrowDown'||event.key==='ArrowUp'){event.preventDefault();const direction=event.key==='ArrowDown'?1:-1;let next=index;for(let i=0;i<optionNodes.length;i++){next=(next+direction+optionNodes.length)%optionNodes.length;if(!optionNodes[next].classList.contains('is-disabled'))break;}focusOption(next);}
    });
    select.addEventListener('change',sync);
    const observer=new MutationObserver(()=>queueMicrotask(sync));observer.observe(select,{childList:true,subtree:true,attributes:true,attributeFilter:['disabled']});
    const api={select,wrap,trigger,sync,open,close};instances.add(api);sync();
  }
  function scan(root=document){
    if(root.matches?.('select'))enhance(root);
    root.querySelectorAll?.('select').forEach(enhance);
  }
  document.addEventListener('click',event=>{if(!event.target.closest('.ds-select'))closeAll();});
  document.addEventListener('keydown',event=>{if(event.key==='Escape')closeAll();});
  scan(document);
  new MutationObserver(mutations=>mutations.forEach(m=>m.addedNodes.forEach(node=>{if(node.nodeType===1)scan(node);}))).observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
  globalThis.__dsSyncSelects=()=>instances.forEach(api=>api.sync());
  document.querySelectorAll('select option[value="Todos"],select option[value="Todas"]').forEach(option=>{option.textContent='Todos';});
  globalThis.__dsSyncSelects();
  document.addEventListener('click',event=>{if(event.target.closest('[data-uxs-clear],[data-reset-filters],[data-panel-jump]'))setTimeout(()=>globalThis.__dsSyncSelects?.(),0);});
})();
</script>


<script id="ux-taxonomy-source-of-truth-script">
(function(){
  const LEVELS=['product','funnel','minifunnel','component'];
  const LEVEL_LABELS={product:'Producto',funnel:'Funnel',minifunnel:'Minifunnel',component:'Componente'};
  let activeView='product';
  let activeTermId='';

  const clean=value=>String(value??'').trim();
  const norm=value=>clean(value).normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,'').toLocaleLowerCase('es');
  const esc=value=>String(value??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,char=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#039;'}[char]));

  function terms(){
    try{
      const all=Array.isArray(taxonomyProject?.state?.terms)?taxonomyProject.state.terms:[];
      return all.filter(term=>{
        const origin=String(term?.origin||'').trim();
        return term?.governed===true||!/^excel revisado\s*·/i.test(origin);
      });
    }catch(_){return [];}
  }

  function termId(term){return clean(term?.id||term?.termId||term?.key);}
  function parentId(term){return clean(term?.parentId||term?.parentTermId||term?.parent?.id||term?.parent);}
  function termName(term){return clean(term?.canonicalName||term?.name||term?.label||term?.value);}
  function termStatus(term){return clean(term?.status||'canonical')||'canonical';}
  function termStatusKind(term){
    const value=norm(termStatus(term));
    if(['canonical','canonico'].includes(value))return 'canonical';
    if(['alias','synonym','sinonimo'].includes(value))return 'alias';
    if(['obsolete','obsoleto','deprecated','retired','retirado'].includes(value))return 'obsolete';
    if(['pending','pendiente','proposed','proposal','propuesta','draft','borrador'].includes(value))return 'pending';
    return 'pending';
  }
  function termStatusLabel(term){
    return {canonical:'Canónico',alias:'Alias',obsolete:'Obsoleto',pending:'Pendiente'}[termStatusKind(term)]||termStatus(term);
  }
  function termLevel(term){return clean(term?.level);}
  function termAliases(term){
    const raw=term?.aliases||term?.alias||term?.historicalNames||[];
    return Array.isArray(raw)?raw.map(clean).filter(Boolean):clean(raw)?[clean(raw)]:[];
  }

  function findTerm(id){return terms().find(term=>termId(term)===clean(id));}

  function ancestry(term){
    if(!term)return [];
    if(Array.isArray(term.path)&&term.path.length)return term.path.map(clean).filter(Boolean);
    if(typeof term.path==='string'&&term.path.trim())return term.path.split(/\s*[›/>]\s*/).map(clean).filter(Boolean);
    const byId=new Map(terms().map(item=>[termId(item),item]).filter(([id])=>id));
    const chain=[];
    const seen=new Set();
    let current=term;
    while(current&&!seen.has(termId(current))){
      const id=termId(current);if(id)seen.add(id);
      chain.unshift(current);
      const pid=parentId(current);
      current=pid?byId.get(pid):null;
    }
    return chain.map(termName).filter(Boolean);
  }

  function contextForTerm(term){
    const context={};
    if(!term)return context;
    const byId=new Map(terms().map(item=>[termId(item),item]).filter(([id])=>id));
    const chain=[];
    const seen=new Set();
    let current=term;
    while(current&&!seen.has(termId(current))){
      const id=termId(current);if(id)seen.add(id);
      chain.unshift(current);
      const pid=parentId(current);
      current=pid?byId.get(pid):null;
    }
    chain.forEach(item=>{
      const level=termLevel(item);
      const name=termName(item);
      if(LEVELS.includes(level)&&name)context[level]=name;
    });
    if(!Object.keys(context).length&&LEVELS.includes(termLevel(term)))context[termLevel(term)]=termName(term);
    return context;
  }

  function structuredRecords(){
    try{
      if(typeof currentStructuredRecords==='function')return currentStructuredRecords();
    }catch(_){}
    try{
      return (sourceItems||[]).flatMap(item=>(item.insightRegister?.rows||[]).map(row=>({...row,__documentId:item.id})));
    }catch(_){return [];}
  }

  function matchesContext(item,context){
    return LEVELS.every(level=>{
      if(!context[level])return true;
      if(level==='component'){
        const values=[item?.component,...(Array.isArray(item?.components)?item.components:[])].map(norm).filter(Boolean);
        return values.includes(norm(context[level]));
      }
      return norm(item?.[level])===norm(context[level]);
    });
  }

  function knowledgeMetrics(term){
    const context=contextForTerm(term);
    const rows=structuredRecords().filter(row=>matchesContext(row,context));
    let learningItems=[];
    try{
      learningItems=(learnings||[]).filter(item=>{
        const candidates=[item,{...item,component:item.component||item.components?.[0]}];
        return candidates.some(candidate=>matchesContext(candidate,context));
      });
    }catch(_){}
    let docs=[];
    try{
      docs=(sourceItems||[]).filter(doc=>{
        const docRows=doc.insightRegister?.rows||[];
        return docRows.some(row=>matchesContext(row,context))||matchesContext(doc,context);
      });
    }catch(_){}
    return {context,records:rows.length,learnings:learningItems.length,sources:docs.length};
  }

  function renderHealth(){
    // La validación de gobierno queda fuera de la interfaz principal hasta que exista una acción clara para el usuario.
  }

  function renderKnowledgeBridge(){
    const detail=document.getElementById('taxonomy-detail');
    if(!detail)return;
    detail.querySelector('.ux-taxonomy-knowledge-bridge')?.remove();
    detail.querySelector('.ux-taxonomy-term-meta')?.remove();

    const activeButton=detail.ownerDocument.querySelector('[data-taxonomy-term].active,[data-taxonomy-term][aria-current="true"],[data-taxonomy-term][aria-selected="true"]');
    const id=activeTermId||activeButton?.dataset?.taxonomyTerm||'';
    const term=findTerm(id);
    if(!term)return;

    const status=termStatus(term);
    const aliases=termAliases(term);
    const path=ancestry(term);
    const metrics=knowledgeMetrics(term);
    const levelLabel=LEVEL_LABELS[termLevel(term)]||termLevel(term)||'Término';

    const statusKind=termStatusKind(term);
    const metaItems=[];
    if(statusKind!=='canonical')metaItems.push(`<span>${esc(termStatusLabel(term))}</span>`);
    if(aliases.length)metaItems.push(`<span>${esc(aliases.length===1?'1 alias':`${aliases.length} aliases`)}</span>`);
    if(metaItems.length){
      const meta=document.createElement('div');
      meta.className='ux-taxonomy-term-meta';
      meta.innerHTML=metaItems.join('');
      const heading=detail.querySelector('h1,h2,h3');
      if(heading)heading.insertAdjacentElement('afterend',meta);else detail.prepend(meta);
    }

    const bridge=document.createElement('section');
    bridge.className='ux-taxonomy-knowledge-bridge';
    bridge.innerHTML=`
      <h3>Conocimiento relacionado</h3>
      <p>${path.length?`Contexto: ${esc(path.join(' › '))}`:'Consulta el conocimiento estructurado clasificado bajo este término.'}</p>
      <div class="ux-taxonomy-knowledge-metrics">
        <div class="ux-taxonomy-knowledge-metric"><strong>${metrics.records}</strong><span>${metrics.records===1?'registro estructurado':'registros estructurados'}</span></div>
        <div class="ux-taxonomy-knowledge-metric"><strong>${metrics.learnings}</strong><span>${metrics.learnings===1?'learning relacionado':'learnings relacionados'}</span></div>
        <div class="ux-taxonomy-knowledge-metric"><strong>${metrics.sources}</strong><span>${metrics.sources===1?'fuente estructurada':'fuentes estructuradas'}</span></div>
      </div>
      <button class="view-link ux-taxonomy-knowledge-action" type="button" data-taxonomy-explore-context='${esc(JSON.stringify(metrics.context))}'>Explorar conocimiento</button>`;
    detail.appendChild(bridge);
  }

  function simplifyTaxonomyDetail(){
    const detail=document.getElementById('taxonomy-detail');
    if(!detail)return;

    const normalized=value=>norm(String(value||'').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim());
    const all=[...detail.querySelectorAll('*')];

    // El estado normal no necesita una etiqueta visible.
    all.filter(node=>normalized(node.textContent)==='canonico').forEach(node=>{
      const candidate=node.closest('.status-badge,.badge,.taxonomy-badge,.taxonomy-status')||node;
      candidate.hidden=true;
      candidate.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
    });

    // "Nivel" duplica el kicker superior (PRODUCTO / PANTALLA / COMPONENTE / ELEMENTO).
    // Dejamos visible la relación jerárquica "Depende de", que sí aporta contexto.
    const levelLabelNode=[...detail.querySelectorAll('span,small,dt,label,strong')].find(node=>normalized(node.textContent)==='nivel');
    if(levelLabelNode){
      let block=levelLabelNode.parentElement;
      while(block&&block!==detail){
        const copy=normalized(block.textContent);
        if(copy.startsWith('nivel')&&copy.length<100){
          block.hidden=true;
          break;
        }
        block=block.parentElement;
      }
    }

    // Los alias vacíos son ruido; si existen, se expresan con lenguaje más claro.
    const aliasHeading=all.find(node=>['h2','h3','h4','strong'].includes(node.tagName.toLowerCase())&&normalized(node.textContent)==='alias');
    if(aliasHeading){
      let block=aliasHeading.parentElement;
      while(block&&block!==detail){
        const copy=normalized(block.textContent);
        if(copy.includes('no tiene alias')){
          block.hidden=true;
          break;
        }
        if(copy.length>120)break;
        block=block.parentElement;
      }
      if(!block?.hidden)aliasHeading.textContent='También puede aparecer como';
    }

    // "Uso actual" no aporta valor en esta vista de gobierno taxonómico.
    // Se elimina del panel de detalle sin alterar el resto de metadatos ni acciones de edición.
    const useHeading=all.find(node=>['h2','h3','h4','strong'].includes(node.tagName.toLowerCase())&&normalized(node.textContent)==='uso actual');
    if(useHeading){
      let block=useHeading.parentElement;
      while(block&&block!==detail){
        const copy=normalized(block.textContent);
        if(copy.includes('uso actual')){
          block.hidden=true;
          break;
        }
        block=block.parentElement;
      }
    }

    // "Comprobar un término" se reserva para gestión técnica y no se muestra en el detalle cotidiano.
    const resolverHeadingToHide=[...detail.querySelectorAll('summary,h2,h3,h4,strong')].find(node=>normalized(node.textContent)==='comprobar un termino');
    if(resolverHeadingToHide){
      const resolverToHide=resolverHeadingToHide.closest('details')||resolverHeadingToHide.parentElement;
      if(resolverToHide&&resolverToHide!==detail)resolverToHide.hidden=true;
    }

    // Origen e ID estable son útiles para gobierno, no para consulta diaria.
    // Se conservan dentro de un disclosure técnico, sin perder funcionalidad.
    let technical=detail.querySelector('.ux-taxonomy-technical');
    if(!technical){
      const techBlocks=[];
      ['origen','id estable'].forEach(label=>{
        const labelNode=[...detail.querySelectorAll('span,small,dt,label,strong')].find(node=>normalized(node.textContent)===label);
        if(!labelNode)return;
        let block=labelNode.parentElement;
        while(block&&block!==detail){
          const copy=normalized(block.textContent);
          if(copy.startsWith(label)&&copy.length<220){
            techBlocks.push(block);
            break;
          }
          block=block.parentElement;
        }
      });

      const unique=[...new Set(techBlocks)].filter(block=>block&&block.isConnected);
      if(unique.length){
        technical=document.createElement('details');
        technical.className='ux-taxonomy-technical';
        technical.innerHTML='<summary>Información técnica</summary><div class="ux-taxonomy-technical-body"></div>';
        const body=technical.querySelector('.ux-taxonomy-technical-body');
        unique.forEach(block=>body.appendChild(block));
        detail.appendChild(technical);
      }
    }
  }

  function normalizeTaxonomyViewHeaders(){
    const root=document.getElementById('taxonomy');
    if(!root)return;
    const headings=[...root.querySelectorAll('h2,h3,strong')];
    const heading=headings.find(node=>norm(node.textContent)==='taxonomia gobernada');
    if(!heading)return;

    let candidate=heading.parentElement;
    const productView=document.getElementById('taxonomy-view-product');
    while(candidate&&candidate!==productView){
      const copy=norm(candidate.textContent);
      const hasExpectedCopy=copy.includes('los nuevos nodos se crean con id estable')||copy.includes('conservan alias');
      if(hasExpectedCopy&&candidate.querySelector('button')){
        candidate.classList.add('ux-taxonomy-view-intro','ux-taxonomy-common-intro');
        break;
      }
      candidate=candidate.parentElement;
    }
  }

  function setView(view){
    activeView=view;
    document.querySelectorAll('#taxonomy [data-taxonomy-panel]').forEach(panel=>{
      panel.hidden=panel.dataset.taxonomyPanel!==view;
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('#taxonomy [data-taxonomy-view]').forEach(button=>{
      const active=button.dataset.taxonomyView===view;
      button.classList.toggle('active',active);
      button.setAttribute('aria-selected',String(active));
    });
    if(view==='product')setTimeout(()=>{renderKnowledgeBridge();normalizeTaxonomyViewHeaders();simplifyTaxonomyDetail();},0);
    renderHealth();
    setTimeout(normalizeTaxonomyViewHeaders,0);
  }


  document.addEventListener('click',event=>{
    const button=event.target.closest('#taxonomy [data-taxonomy-view]');
    if(!button)return;
    event.preventDefault();
    event.stopPropagation();
    setView(button.dataset.taxonomyView);
  },true);

  document.addEventListener('click',event=>{
    const termButton=event.target.closest('#taxonomy [data-taxonomy-term]');
    if(termButton){
      activeTermId=termButton.dataset.taxonomyTerm||'';
      setTimeout(()=>{renderKnowledgeBridge();renderHealth();simplifyTaxonomyDetail();},0);
      return;
    }

    const explore=event.target.closest('[data-taxonomy-explore-context]');
    if(explore){
      let context={};
      try{context=JSON.parse(explore.dataset.taxonomyExploreContext||'{}');}catch(_){}
      if(typeof goToSimpleExploreContext==='function')goToSimpleExploreContext(context);
      return;
    }

    if(event.target.closest('[data-taxonomy-edit],[data-propose-taxonomy]')){
      setTimeout(()=>{renderHealth();renderKnowledgeBridge();simplifyTaxonomyDetail();},250);
    }
  });

  function refreshTaxonomyEnhancements(){
    renderHealth();
    normalizeTaxonomyViewHeaders();
    if(activeView==='product')renderKnowledgeBridge();
    simplifyTaxonomyDetail();
  }

  let taxonomyDetailObserver=null;
  function watchTaxonomyDetail(){
    const detail=document.getElementById('taxonomy-detail');
    if(!detail||taxonomyDetailObserver)return;
    taxonomyDetailObserver=new MutationObserver(()=>{
      requestAnimationFrame(()=>simplifyTaxonomyDetail());
    });
    taxonomyDetailObserver.observe(detail,{childList:true,subtree:true});
    simplifyTaxonomyDetail();
  }

  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>{
    setView('product');
    watchTaxonomyDetail();
    setTimeout(refreshTaxonomyEnhancements,350);
    setTimeout(refreshTaxonomyEnhancements,1200);
  });
  setTimeout(refreshTaxonomyEnhancements,2200);
})();
</script>


<script id="uikit-date-picker-script">
(function(){
  const MONTHS=['enero','febrero','marzo','abril','mayo','junio','julio','agosto','septiembre','octubre','noviembre','diciembre'];
  const WEEKDAYS=['L','M','X','J','V','S','D'];
  const pad=n=>String(n).padStart(2,'0');
  const toIso=date=>`${date.getFullYear()}-${pad(date.getMonth()+1)}-${pad(date.getDate())}`;
  const parseIso=value=>{
    if(!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(value||''))return null;
    const [y,m,d]=value.split('-').map(Number);
    const date=new Date(y,m-1,d,12);
    return Number.isNaN(date.getTime())?null:date;
  };
  const sameDay=(a,b)=>!!a&&!!b&&a.getFullYear()===b.getFullYear()&&a.getMonth()===b.getMonth()&&a.getDate()===b.getDate();
  const formatDate=date=>date?`${date.getDate()} ${MONTHS[date.getMonth()].slice(0,4)} ${date.getFullYear()}`:'Seleccionar fecha';
  const esc=s=>String(s??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,ch=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[ch]));

  let activePicker=null;

  function rangePair(input){
    const id=input?.id||'';
    const base=id.replace(/-(from|to)$/,'');
    const role=id.endsWith('-to')?'to':'from';
    return {
      role,
      from:document.getElementById(`${base}-from`),
      to:document.getElementById(`${base}-to`)
    };
  }
  function beforeDay(a,b){
    return !!a&&!!b&&toIso(a)<toIso(b);
  }

  function calendarIcon(){
    return `<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M8 2v3M16 2v3M3.5 9h17M5.5 4.5h13a2 2 0 0 1 2 2v12a2 2 0 0 1-2 2h-13a2 2 0 0 1-2-2v-12a2 2 0 0 1 2-2Z" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.7" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/></svg>`;
  }
  function chevronIcon(direction){
    const d=direction==='left'?'m15 18-6-6 6-6':'m9 18 6-6-6-6';
    return `<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" aria-hidden="true"><path d="${d}" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/></svg>`;
  }

  function positionMenu(picker){
    const {trigger,menu}=picker;
    if(!menu||menu.hidden)return;
    const rect=trigger.getBoundingClientRect();
    const menuWidth=Math.min(328,window.innerWidth-32);
    menu.style.width=`${menuWidth}px`;
    menu.style.left=`${Math.min(Math.max(16,rect.left),Math.max(16,window.innerWidth-menuWidth-16))}px`;

    menu.style.top='0px';
    const menuHeight=Math.min(menu.scrollHeight,window.innerHeight-32);
    const below=window.innerHeight-rect.bottom-8;
    const top=below>=menuHeight
      ? rect.bottom+8
      : Math.max(16,rect.top-menuHeight-8);
    menu.style.top=`${top}px`;
  }

  function renderCalendar(picker){
    const {menu}=picker;
    const year=picker.viewYear,month=picker.viewMonth;
    const first=new Date(year,month,1,12);
    const mondayIndex=(first.getDay()+6)%7;
    const gridStart=new Date(year,month,1-mondayIndex,12);
    const today=new Date(); today.setHours(12,0,0,0);
    const pair=rangePair(picker.input);
    const start=parseIso(pair.from?.value||'');
    const end=parseIso(pair.to?.value||'');

    const weekdays=WEEKDAYS.map(day=>`<span class="ui-date-weekday" role="columnheader">${day}</span>`).join('');
    let days='';
    for(let i=0;i<42;i++){
      const date=new Date(gridStart);date.setDate(gridStart.getDate()+i);
      const outside=date.getMonth()!==month;
      const isStart=sameDay(date,start);
      const isEnd=sameDay(date,end);
      const selected=isStart||isEnd;
      const inRange=!!start&&!!end&&beforeDay(start,date)&&beforeDay(date,end);
      const isToday=sameDay(date,today);
      const disabled=pair.role==='to'&&!!start&&beforeDay(date,start);
      days+=`<button class="ui-date-day${outside?' is-outside':''}${inRange?' is-in-range':''}${isStart?' is-range-start':''}${isEnd?' is-range-end':''}${selected?' is-selected':''}${isToday?' is-today':''}${disabled?' is-disabled':''}" type="button" role="gridcell" aria-selected="${selected?'true':'false'}" aria-disabled="${disabled?'true':'false'}" ${disabled?'disabled':''} data-date="${toIso(date)}" aria-label="${esc(formatDate(date))}">${date.getDate()}</button>`;
    }

    menu.querySelector('.ui-date-month-label').textContent=`${MONTHS[month]} ${year}`;
    menu.querySelector('.ui-date-grid').innerHTML=weekdays+days;
  }

  function closePicker(picker,{restoreFocus=true}={}){
    if(!picker)return;
    picker.menu.hidden=true;
    picker.wrapper.classList.remove('is-open');
    picker.trigger.setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');
    if(activePicker===picker)activePicker=null;
    if(restoreFocus)picker.trigger.focus({preventScroll:true});
  }

  function openPicker(picker){
    if(activePicker&&activePicker!==picker)closePicker(activePicker,{restoreFocus:false});
    const pair=rangePair(picker.input);
    const existing=parseIso(picker.input.value);
    const start=parseIso(pair.from?.value||'');
    const end=parseIso(pair.to?.value||'');
    picker.pending=existing?new Date(existing):null;
    const base=existing||(pair.role==='to'?start:null)||start||end||new Date();
    picker.viewYear=base.getFullYear();
    picker.viewMonth=base.getMonth();
    picker.menu.hidden=false;
    picker.wrapper.classList.add('is-open');
    picker.trigger.setAttribute('aria-expanded','true');
    activePicker=picker;
    renderCalendar(picker);
    requestAnimationFrame(()=>positionMenu(picker));
  }

  function syncTrigger(picker){
    const selected=parseIso(picker.input.value);
    picker.value.textContent=formatDate(selected);
    picker.trigger.classList.toggle('is-placeholder',!selected);
  }

  function enhance(input,index){
    if(input.dataset.uiDateReady==='true')return;
    input.dataset.uiDateReady='true';
    input.classList.add('ui-date-source');
    input.setAttribute('tabindex','-1');

    const label=input.closest('label');
    if(!label)return;

    const wrapper=document.createElement('div');
    wrapper.className='ui-date-picker';
    input.parentNode.insertBefore(wrapper,input);
    wrapper.appendChild(input);

    const trigger=document.createElement('button');
    const menuId=`ui-date-menu-${index}`;
    trigger.type='button';
    trigger.className='ui-date-trigger is-placeholder';
    trigger.setAttribute('aria-haspopup','dialog');
    trigger.setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');
    trigger.setAttribute('aria-controls',menuId);
    trigger.innerHTML=`<span class="ui-date-trigger-icon">${calendarIcon()}</span><span class="ui-date-trigger-value">Seleccionar fecha</span>`;
    wrapper.appendChild(trigger);

    const menu=document.createElement('div');
    menu.id=menuId;
    menu.className='ui-date-menu';
    menu.hidden=true;
    menu.setAttribute('role','dialog');
    menu.setAttribute('aria-modal','false');
    menu.setAttribute('aria-label','Seleccionar fecha');
    menu.innerHTML=`
      <div class="ui-date-menu-content">
        <div class="ui-date-month-row">
          <button class="ui-date-utility ui-date-prev" type="button" aria-label="Mes anterior">${chevronIcon('left')}</button>
          <div class="ui-date-month-label" aria-live="polite"></div>
          <button class="ui-date-utility ui-date-next" type="button" aria-label="Mes siguiente">${chevronIcon('right')}</button>
        </div>
        <div class="ui-date-grid" role="grid" aria-label="Calendario"></div>
      </div>`;
    document.body.appendChild(menu);

    const picker={
      input,wrapper,trigger,menu,
      value:trigger.querySelector('.ui-date-trigger-value'),
      pending:null,viewYear:new Date().getFullYear(),viewMonth:new Date().getMonth()
    };

    trigger.addEventListener('click',()=>menu.hidden?openPicker(picker):closePicker(picker));
    menu.querySelector('.ui-date-prev').addEventListener('click',()=>{
      picker.viewMonth--;
      if(picker.viewMonth<0){picker.viewMonth=11;picker.viewYear--;}
      renderCalendar(picker);
    });
    menu.querySelector('.ui-date-next').addEventListener('click',()=>{
      picker.viewMonth++;
      if(picker.viewMonth>11){picker.viewMonth=0;picker.viewYear++;}
      renderCalendar(picker);
    });
    menu.querySelector('.ui-date-grid').addEventListener('click',event=>{
      const cell=event.target.closest('[data-date]');
      if(!cell||cell.disabled)return;
      const selected=parseIso(cell.dataset.date);
      if(!selected)return;
      const pair=rangePair(input);

      if(pair.role==='from'){
        input.value=toIso(selected);
        const currentEnd=parseIso(pair.to?.value||'');
        if(currentEnd&&beforeDay(currentEnd,selected)&&pair.to){
          pair.to.value='';
          pair.to.dispatchEvent(new Event('change',{bubbles:true}));
        }
      }else{
        const currentStart=parseIso(pair.from?.value||'');
        if(currentStart&&beforeDay(selected,currentStart))return;
        input.value=toIso(selected);
      }

      picker.pending=selected;
      syncTrigger(picker);
      input.dispatchEvent(new Event('change',{bubbles:true}));
      closePicker(picker,{restoreFocus:false});
    });

    input.addEventListener('change',()=>syncTrigger(picker));
    syncTrigger(picker);
  }

  function init(){
    document.querySelectorAll('input.filter-date-input[type="date"]').forEach(enhance);
  }

  document.addEventListener('click',event=>{
    if(!activePicker)return;
    if(activePicker.menu.contains(event.target)||activePicker.trigger.contains(event.target))return;
    closePicker(activePicker,{restoreFocus:false});
  });
  document.addEventListener('keydown',event=>{
    if(event.key==='Escape'&&activePicker)closePicker(activePicker);
  });
  window.addEventListener('resize',()=>activePicker&&positionMenu(activePicker));
  window.addEventListener('scroll',()=>activePicker&&positionMenu(activePicker),true);

  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',init);
  else init();
})();
</script>


<script id="ux-taxonomy-governance-guard">
(function(){
  function enforce(){
    try{
      const terms=taxonomyProject?.state?.terms;
      if(!Array.isArray(terms))return;
      let changed=false;
      terms.forEach(term=>{
        const origin=String(term?.origin||'').trim();
        if(/^Excel revisado\s*·/i.test(origin)&&term?.governed!==true&&term?.status==='canonical'){
          term.status='pending';
          changed=true;
        }
      });
      if(changed){
        globalThis.__uxSimpleExploreRefresh?.();
        globalThis.__dsSyncSelects?.();
      }
    }catch(_){}
  }
  window.addEventListener('load',()=>{enforce();setTimeout(enforce,400);setTimeout(enforce,1400);});
  document.addEventListener('click',event=>{
    if(event.target.closest('[data-source-action],[data-learning-suggestion-confirm],[data-propose-taxonomy],[data-taxonomy-edit]')){
      setTimeout(enforce,300);
      setTimeout(enforce,1200);
    }
  });
})();
</script>

<script id="ux-dimension-governance">
(function(){
  const DIMENSIONS=['Usabilidad e interacción','Accesibilidad','Arquitectura de información','Contenido y copy','Navegación y flujo','Interfaz y jerarquía visual','Personalización','Negocio y conversión','Tecnología y restricciones','Datos y medición'];
  const LEGACY_HINTS=new Set([
    'arquitectura de información','arquitectura de informacion','claridad y comprensión','claridad y comprension','consistencia','contenido y copy','control y autonomía','control y autonomia','encontrabilidad','feedback y estado','flujo y secuenciación','flujo y secuenciacion','fricción y eficiencia','friccion y eficiencia','jerarquía visual','jerarquia visual','navegación','navegacion','orientación y ayuda','orientacion y ayuda','personalización','personalizacion',
    'above the fold','adaptación por dispositivo','adaptacion por dispositivo','agrupación','agrupacion','arquitectura','autoservicio','ayuda futura','buscador','búsqueda','busqueda','capacidad de resolución','capacidad de resolucion','check-in','claridad','complejidad','condicionantes estratégicos','condicionantes estrategicos','contenido prioritario','contexto','contexto de uso','continuidad','control','conversión','conversion','copy','dependencia de navegación','dependencia de navegacion','dirección estratégica','direccion estrategica','encontrabilidad','escalabilidad','gestión de complejidad','gestion de complejidad','gestión de reserva','gestion de reserva','gestión del viaje','gestion del viaje','identidad','jerarquía','jerarquia','jerarquía del above the fold','jerarquia del above the fold','jerarquía del buscador','jerarquia del buscador','mercados','modelo mental','naming','navegación principal','navegacion principal','necesidades del pasajero','ofertas','orientación','orientacion','orientación a tareas','orientacion a tareas','primera capa','prioridad estratégica','prioridad estrategica','prioridad operativa','promoción','promocion','propósito del header','proposito del header','responsive','restricciones de negocio','restricciones técnicas y datos','restricciones tecnicas y datos','restricción técnica del componente','restriccion tecnica del componente','rendimiento','secuenciación','secuenciacion','suma','sistema home','tareas operativas','territorios ux','uso del header','utilidades','personalización y reducción de fricción','personalizacion y reduccion de friccion','login contextual y recuperación de sesión','login contextual y recuperacion de sesion','login contextual y propuesta de valor','captación contextual y recompensa potencial','captacion contextual y recompensa potencial','fidelización en fare families','fidelizacion en fare families','beneficios por nivel en fare families','fidelización en upsell','fidelizacion en upsell','fidelización multipasajero','fidelizacion multipasajero','valor económico de millas','valor economico de millas','beneficios suma en asientos','beneficios suma en equipaje','fidelización en ancillaries','fidelizacion en ancillaries','continuidad de fidelización','continuidad de fidelizacion','proveniencia y comunicación de beneficios','proveniencia y comunicacion de beneficios','acceso e identificación suma','acceso e identificacion suma','beneficios suma en fare families','canje de millas','beneficios suma en ancillaries','resultado económico y millas','resultado economico y millas'
  ]);
  const norm=value=>String(value||'').trim().toLocaleLowerCase('es');
  let patching=false;

  function findDimensionCard(){
    const root=document.getElementById('taxonomy-controlled');
    if(!root)return null;
    const headings=[...root.querySelectorAll('h2,h3,h4,strong,[class*=title],[class*=heading]')];
    const heading=headings.find(node=>['línea ux','linea ux','dimensión ux','dimension ux','dimensión','dimension'].includes(norm(node.textContent)));
    if(!heading)return null;
    let card=heading.parentElement;
    while(card&&card!==root){
      const text=norm(card.textContent);
      const legacyHits=[...LEGACY_HINTS].filter(value=>text.includes(value)).length;
      if(legacyHits>=3||card.querySelector('button'))break;
      card=card.parentElement;
    }
    return card&&card!==root?{card,heading}:null;
  }

  function chipContainer(card){
    const candidates=[...card.querySelectorAll('div,section')].filter(node=>{
      if(node.querySelector('h2,h3,h4'))return false;
      const children=[...node.children];
      if(children.length<3)return false;
      const short=children.filter(child=>{
        const text=String(child.textContent||'').trim();
        return text&&text.length<70&&!/^\+?\s*añadir$/i.test(text);
});
      return short.length>=3;
    });
    const scored=candidates.map(node=>({node,hits:[...node.children].filter(child=>LEGACY_HINTS.has(norm(child.textContent))||DIMENSIONS.some(value=>norm(value)===norm(child.textContent))).length})).sort((a,b)=>b.hits-a.hits);
    return scored[0]?.hits?scored[0].node:null;
  }

  function patchVocabularyCard(){
    if(patching)return;
    const found=findDimensionCard();
    if(!found)return;
    patching=true;
    try{
      if(found.heading.textContent!=='Dimensión UX') found.heading.textContent='Dimensión UX';
      const container=chipContainer(found.card);
      if(!container)return;
      const existing=[...container.children];
      const managed=existing.filter(child=>{
        const text=norm(child.textContent);
        return LEGACY_HINTS.has(text)||DIMENSIONS.some(value=>norm(value)===text);
      });
      const current=managed.map(child=>String(child.textContent||'').trim());
      const alreadyCanonical=current.length===DIMENSIONS.length&&DIMENSIONS.every(label=>current.some(value=>norm(value)===norm(label)))&&!current.some(value=>LEGACY_HINTS.has(norm(value))&&!DIMENSIONS.some(label=>norm(label)===norm(value)));
      if(alreadyCanonical)return;
      const template=managed[0];
      if(!template)return;
      managed.forEach(child=>child.remove());
      DIMENSIONS.forEach(label=>{
        const chip=template.cloneNode(true);
        chip.removeAttribute('data-value');
        chip.removeAttribute('data-taxonomy-controlled-value');
        chip.textContent=label;
        container.appendChild(chip);
      });
    }finally{
      patching=false;
    }
  }

  // Mantiene compatibilidad con taxonomy.js sin permitir que el vocabulario histórico vuelva a Explorar.
  const previous=globalThis.taxonomyControlledValues;
  if(typeof previous==='function'){
    globalThis.taxonomyControlledValues=function(label){
      if(['línea ux','linea ux','dimensión ux','dimension ux','dimensión','dimension'].includes(norm(label)))return DIMENSIONS.slice();
      return previous.apply(this,arguments);
    };
  }

  const observe=()=>{
    const root=document.getElementById('taxonomy-controlled');
    if(!root)return;
    patchVocabularyCard();
    new MutationObserver(()=>patchVocabularyCard()).observe(root,{childList:true,subtree:true,characterData:true});
  };
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>setTimeout(observe,0));
  else setTimeout(observe,0);
  window.addEventListener('load',()=>{setTimeout(patchVocabularyCard,250);setTimeout(patchVocabularyCard,1000);});
})();
</script>


<script id="ux-origin-governance">
(function(){
  const ORIGINS=['Test con usuarios','Quantum Metric','A/B Test','CRO','Auditoría UX','Heurística','Benchmark','Desk research','Data','Negocio','CSAT'];
  const LEGACY_HINTS=new Set([
    'ux research / test de usuarios','quantum metric','a/b test / cro','auditoría ux / heurística','auditoria ux / heuristica',
    'benchmark','desk research','análisis conductual / sesgos','analisis conductual / sesgos','análisis conductual','analisis conductual','data','negocio','seo / contenidos',
    'csat','soporte / atención al cliente','soporte / atencion al cliente','síntesis ux','sintesis ux','auditoría ux','auditoria ux',
    'quantum metric + análisis heurístico','quantum metric + analisis heuristico','quantum metric / data','mckinsey + bcg',
    'deloitte + mckinsey','mckinsey','mckinsey + iata','investigación académica sobre frequent flyer',
    'investigacion academica sobre frequent flyer','collinson + caso km malta','condiciones suma revisadas en el desk research',
    'desk research suma','revisión de contenidos oficiales air europa','revision de contenidos oficiales air europa',
    'síntesis del desk research','sintesis del desk research','conclusiones del desk research',
    'aplicación propuesta en el desk research','aplicacion propuesta en el desk research','caso km malta / proveedor tecnológico',
    'caso km malta / proveedor tecnologico','condición air europa recogida en el desk research',
    'condicion air europa recogida en el desk research','desk research suma / condicionante técnico',
    'desk research suma / condicionante tecnico'
  ]);
  const norm=value=>String(value||'').trim().toLocaleLowerCase('es');
  let patching=false;

  function findOriginCard(){
    const root=document.getElementById('taxonomy-controlled');
    if(!root)return null;
    const headings=[...root.querySelectorAll('h2,h3,h4,strong,[class*=title],[class*=heading]')];
    const heading=headings.find(node=>['origen del hallazgo'].includes(norm(node.textContent)));
    if(!heading)return null;
    let card=heading.parentElement;
    while(card&&card!==root){
      const text=norm(card.textContent);
      const hits=[...LEGACY_HINTS].filter(value=>text.includes(value)).length;
      if(hits>=3||card.querySelector('button'))break;
      card=card.parentElement;
    }
    return card&&card!==root?{card,heading}:null;
  }

  function chipContainer(card){
    const candidates=[...card.querySelectorAll('div,section')].filter(node=>{
      if(node.querySelector('h2,h3,h4'))return false;
      const children=[...node.children];
      if(children.length<3)return false;
      const short=children.filter(child=>{
        const text=String(child.textContent||'').trim();
        return text&&text.length<80&&!/^\+?\s*añadir$/i.test(text);
      });
      return short.length>=3;
    });
    const scored=candidates.map(node=>({
      node,
      hits:[...node.children].filter(child=>LEGACY_HINTS.has(norm(child.textContent))||ORIGINS.some(value=>norm(value)===norm(child.textContent))).length
    })).sort((a,b)=>b.hits-a.hits);
    return scored[0]?.hits?scored[0].node:null;
  }

  function patchVocabularyCard(){
    if(patching)return;
    const found=findOriginCard();
    if(!found)return;
    patching=true;
    try{
      const container=chipContainer(found.card);
      if(!container)return;
      const existing=[...container.children];
      const managed=existing.filter(child=>{
        const text=norm(child.textContent);
        return LEGACY_HINTS.has(text)||ORIGINS.some(value=>norm(value)===text);
      });
      const current=managed.map(child=>String(child.textContent||'').trim());
      const alreadyCanonical=current.length===ORIGINS.length&&ORIGINS.every(label=>current.some(value=>norm(value)===norm(label)))&&!current.some(value=>LEGACY_HINTS.has(norm(value))&&!ORIGINS.some(label=>norm(label)===norm(value)));
      if(alreadyCanonical)return;
      const template=managed[0];
      if(!template)return;
      managed.forEach(child=>child.remove());
      ORIGINS.forEach(label=>{
        const chip=template.cloneNode(true);
        chip.removeAttribute('data-value');
        chip.removeAttribute('data-taxonomy-controlled-value');
        chip.textContent=label;
        container.appendChild(chip);
      });
    }finally{
      patching=false;
    }
  }

  // Fuerza el vocabulario controlado para filtros y evita que etiquetas históricas vuelvan a aparecer.
  const previous=globalThis.taxonomyControlledValues;
  if(typeof previous==='function'){
    globalThis.taxonomyControlledValues=function(label){
      if(norm(label)==='origen del hallazgo')return ORIGINS.slice();
      return previous.apply(this,arguments);
    };
  }

  const observe=()=>{
    const root=document.getElementById('taxonomy-controlled');
    if(!root)return;
    patchVocabularyCard();
    new MutationObserver(()=>patchVocabularyCard()).observe(root,{childList:true,subtree:true,characterData:true});
  };
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>setTimeout(observe,0));
  else setTimeout(observe,0);
  window.addEventListener('load',()=>{setTimeout(patchVocabularyCard,250);setTimeout(patchVocabularyCard,1000);});
})();
</script>

<script id="ux-controlled-vocabulary-governance-v2">
(function(){
  const VOCABULARIES={
    'tipo de registro':['Hecho','Evidencia interna','Evidencia externa','Hipótesis','Inferencia','Oportunidad','Restricción','Decisión','Pregunta abierta'],
    'origen del hallazgo':['Test con usuarios','Quantum Metric','A/B Test','CRO','Auditoría UX','Heurística','Benchmark','Desk research','Data','Negocio','CSAT'],
    'dispositivo':['Desktop','Mobile','Desktop + mobile','App','No especificado'],
    'mercado':['Alemania','Argentina','Bolivia','Brasil','Bélgica','Chile','Colombia','Ecuador','España','Estados Unidos','Francia','Israel','Italia','México','Panamá','Paraguay','Países Bajos','Perú','Portugal','Reino Unido','República Dominicana','Suiza','Turquía','Uruguay','World (EUR)','World (USD)','Multimercado','No especificado'],
    'región':['España','Europa','África','Oriente Medio','Norteamérica','Caribe','América Central','América del Sur','Global','No especificado'],
    'region':['España','Europa','África','Oriente Medio','Norteamérica','Caribe','América Central','América del Sur','Global','No especificado'],
    'estado usuario':['No logado','Logado no SUMA','Logado SUMA','Logado (SUMA no especificado)','Ambos','No especificado'],
    'sistema / plataforma':['Air Europa / propio','Amadeus','Magnolia','Comarch','Pluto','Responsys','Proveedor externo','No especificado'],
    'resultado':['No aplica','No testado','Positivo','Neutral','Negativo','Mixto','Inconcluso'],
    'implementación':['Implementado','Parcialmente implementado','No implementado','Implementación desconocida'],
    'implementacion':['Implementado','Parcialmente implementado','No implementado','Implementación desconocida'],
    'dimensión':['Usabilidad e interacción','Accesibilidad','Arquitectura de información','Contenido y copy','Navegación y flujo','Interfaz y jerarquía visual','Personalización','Negocio y conversión','Tecnología y restricciones','Datos y medición'],
    'dimension':['Usabilidad e interacción','Accesibilidad','Arquitectura de información','Contenido y copy','Navegación y flujo','Interfaz y jerarquía visual','Personalización','Negocio y conversión','Tecnología y restricciones','Datos y medición'],
    'dimensión ux':['Usabilidad e interacción','Accesibilidad','Arquitectura de información','Contenido y copy','Navegación y flujo','Interfaz y jerarquía visual','Personalización','Negocio y conversión','Tecnología y restricciones','Datos y medición'],
    'dimension ux':['Usabilidad e interacción','Accesibilidad','Arquitectura de información','Contenido y copy','Navegación y flujo','Interfaz y jerarquía visual','Personalización','Negocio y conversión','Tecnología y restricciones','Datos y medición'],
    'impacto ux':['Alto','Medio','Bajo']
  };
  const DISPLAY_LABELS={'dimensión ux':'Dimensión UX','dimension ux':'Dimensión UX'};
  const norm=value=>String(value||'').trim().normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,'').toLocaleLowerCase('es');
  const keyFor=label=>norm(label);
  const vocabularyFor=label=>VOCABULARIES[keyFor(label)]||null;
  let patching=false;

  // Policy metadata for future AI/agent classification. It is deliberately machine-readable and independent of UI copy.
  globalThis.UXControlledVocabularyPolicy={
    version:'2026-10-22-v2',
    rules:{
      recordType:'Una sola categoría epistemológica por registro. No usar Insight UX como cajón de sastre.',
      origin:'Uno o varios orígenes cuando la evidencia realmente combine métodos o fuentes.',
      dimension:'Una dimensión principal; máximo dos solo cuando existan dos mecanismos independientes. Producto, dispositivo, mercado, SUMA y sistema no son dimensiones.',
      market:'Usar un mercado canónico. Si el conocimiento cruza varios mercados, usar Multimercado; no crear combinaciones ad hoc.',
      region:'Derivar de Mercado siempre que sea posible. Global describe alcance, no una restricción técnica.',
      userState:'Clasificar el estado de autenticación/relación SUMA sin mezclarlo con segmento, mercado o producto.',
      result:'Resultado de validación o experimento. No confundir No testado con No aplica ni con implementación.',
      implementation:'Estado real de producción, separado del resultado del test y de la recomendación.',
      impactUx:'Magnitud del impacto en la experiencia, no prioridad de negocio ni nivel de evidencia.'
    },
    dimensions:{
      'Usabilidad e interacción':'Interacción local, comprensión de uso, feedback, control, esfuerzo y eficiencia.',
      'Accesibilidad':'Barreras y requisitos de acceso, percepción, operación y comprensión inclusiva.',
      'Arquitectura de información':'Organización, agrupación, estructura y modelo mental del contenido o funcionalidad.',
      'Contenido y copy':'Naming, microcopy, mensajes, instrucciones y comprensión lingüística.',
      'Navegación y flujo':'Movimiento entre pasos, continuidad, orientación, encontrabilidad y secuenciación del journey.',
      'Interfaz y jerarquía visual':'Prioridad visual, composición, consistencia UI, legibilidad estructural y saliencia.',
      'Personalización':'Adaptación al usuario, historial, perfil, contexto o comportamiento previo.',
      'Negocio y conversión':'Mecánicas comerciales, presión promocional, monetización y objetivos de conversión cuando son la causa principal.',
      'Tecnología y restricciones':'Limitaciones o capacidades de sistemas, proveedores, rendimiento e implementación técnica cuando condicionan la solución.',
      'Datos y medición':'Instrumentación, calidad de métrica, tracking, atribución y disponibilidad de datos.'
    }
  };

  const previous=globalThis.taxonomyControlledValues;
  if(typeof previous==='function'){
    globalThis.taxonomyControlledValues=function(label){
      const vocabulary=vocabularyFor(label);
      if(vocabulary)return vocabulary.slice();
      return previous.apply(this,arguments);
    };
  }

  function findCard(root,heading){
    let card=heading.parentElement;
    while(card&&card!==root){
      if(card.querySelector('button')&&card.children.length>=2)return card;
      card=card.parentElement;
    }
    return null;
  }
  function findChipContainer(card,vocabulary){
    const canonical=new Set(vocabulary.map(norm));
    const candidates=[...card.querySelectorAll('div,section')].filter(node=>{
      if(node.querySelector('h2,h3,h4'))return false;
      const children=[...node.children];
      if(children.length<1)return false;
      const chipLike=children.filter(child=>{
        const text=String(child.textContent||'').trim();
        return text&&text.length<90&&!/^\+?\s*añadir$/i.test(text)&&!/^gestionar/i.test(text);
      });
      return chipLike.length>=1;
    });
    const scored=candidates.map(node=>{
      const children=[...node.children];
      const short=children.filter(child=>String(child.textContent||'').trim().length<90);
      const canonicalHits=short.filter(child=>canonical.has(norm(child.textContent))).length;
      return {node,score:canonicalHits*10+Math.min(short.length,12)};
    }).sort((a,b)=>b.score-a.score);
    return scored[0]?.node||null;
  }
  function patchCards(){
    if(patching)return;
    const root=document.getElementById('taxonomy-controlled');if(!root)return;
    patching=true;
    try{
      const headings=[...root.querySelectorAll('h2,h3,h4,strong,[class*=title],[class*=heading]')];
      headings.forEach(heading=>{
        const raw=String(heading.textContent||'').trim();
        const vocabulary=vocabularyFor(raw);if(!vocabulary)return;
        const display=DISPLAY_LABELS[keyFor(raw)];if(display&&raw!==display)heading.textContent=display;
        const card=findCard(root,heading);if(!card)return;
        const container=findChipContainer(card,vocabulary);if(!container)return;
        const children=[...container.children].filter(child=>{
          const text=String(child.textContent||'').trim();
          return text&&text.length<90&&!/^\+?\s*añadir$/i.test(text)&&!/^gestionar/i.test(text);
        });
        if(!children.length)return;
        const current=children.map(child=>String(child.textContent||'').trim());
        const isExact=current.length===vocabulary.length&&vocabulary.every((label,index)=>norm(current[index])===norm(label));
        if(isExact)return;
        const template=children[0];
        children.forEach(child=>child.remove());
        vocabulary.forEach(label=>{
          const chip=template.cloneNode(true);
          chip.removeAttribute('data-value');chip.removeAttribute('data-taxonomy-controlled-value');
          chip.textContent=label;
          container.appendChild(chip);
        });
      });
    }finally{patching=false;}
  }
  const observe=()=>{
    const root=document.getElementById('taxonomy-controlled');if(!root)return;
    patchCards();
    new MutationObserver(()=>patchCards()).observe(root,{childList:true,subtree:true,characterData:true});
  };
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>setTimeout(observe,0));
  else setTimeout(observe,0);
  window.addEventListener('load',()=>{setTimeout(patchCards,250);setTimeout(patchCards,1100);});
})();
</script>



<script id="ux-taxonomy-intro-cleanup">
(function(){
  const norm=value=>String(value||'').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim().toLocaleLowerCase('es');
  let patching=false;

  function removeVocabularyDuplicate(){
    const root=document.getElementById('taxonomy-controlled');
    if(!root)return;

    const candidates=[...root.querySelectorAll('section,article,div,header')];
    const duplicate=candidates
      .filter(node=>{
        const text=norm(node.textContent);
        return text.includes('vocabularios controlados') &&
               text.includes('listas de referencia para mantener consistencia entre excel, filtros y conocimiento.');
      })
      .sort((a,b)=>a.querySelectorAll('*').length-b.querySelectorAll('*').length)[0];

    if(duplicate)duplicate.remove();
  }

  function pruneEmptyAncestors(node,stopAt){
    let current=node;
    while(current && current!==stopAt){
      const parent=current.parentElement;
      const hasMeaningfulText=String(current.textContent||'').trim().length>0;
      const hasMeaningfulChildren=[...current.children].some(child=>{
        if(child.matches('script,style'))return false;
        return String(child.textContent||'').trim().length>0 ||
               child.matches('button,input,select,textarea,table,ul,ol,details');
      });

      if(!hasMeaningfulText && !hasMeaningfulChildren){
        current.remove();
      }
      current=parent;
    }
  }

  function mergeGlossaryIntro(){
    const panel=document.getElementById('taxonomy-view-glossary');
    const root=document.getElementById('taxonomy-glossary');
    if(!panel||!root)return;

    const intro=panel.querySelector(':scope > .ux-taxonomy-view-intro');
    if(!intro)return;

    const isCreateTermButton=button=>{
      const text=norm(button?.textContent);
      return text.includes('crear término') || text.includes('crear termino');
    };

    // El glosario se vuelve a renderizar al editar un término.
    // taxonomy.js crea entonces un botón nuevo dentro de #taxonomy-glossary.
    // Antes se movía cada botón nuevo a la cabecera sin retirar el anterior,
    // por eso "+ Crear término" se multiplicaba.
    const freshButtons=[...root.querySelectorAll('button')].filter(isCreateTermButton);
    const freshButton=freshButtons[freshButtons.length-1]||null;

    let actions=intro.querySelector(':scope > .ux-taxonomy-header-actions');
    if(!actions){
      actions=document.createElement('div');
      actions.className='ux-taxonomy-header-actions';
      [...intro.children]
        .filter(child=>child.tagName==='BUTTON')
        .forEach(button=>actions.appendChild(button));
      intro.appendChild(actions);
    }

    if(freshButton){
      // Conservamos siempre el botón recién creado por taxonomy.js porque
      // contiene los listeners actuales. Cualquier versión anterior se elimina.
      [...intro.querySelectorAll('button')]
        .filter(button=>isCreateTermButton(button))
        .forEach(button=>button.remove());

      const oldParent=freshButton.parentElement;
      actions.appendChild(freshButton);
      pruneEmptyAncestors(oldParent,root);

      // Si por un re-render intermedio aparecieron varios, solo sobrevive el último.
      freshButtons.slice(0,-1).forEach(button=>{
        const parent=button.parentElement;
        button.remove();
        pruneEmptyAncestors(parent,root);
      });
    }else{
      // Estado estable: nunca puede haber más de un "+ Crear término" en cabecera.
      const headerButtons=[...intro.querySelectorAll('button')].filter(isCreateTermButton);
      headerButtons.slice(1).forEach(button=>button.remove());
      if(headerButtons[0]&&headerButtons[0].parentElement!==actions){
        actions.appendChild(headerButtons[0]);
      }
    }

    const candidates=[...root.querySelectorAll('section,article,div,header')];
    const duplicate=candidates
      .filter(node=>{
        const text=norm(node.textContent);
        return text.includes('glosario ux intel') &&
               text.includes('fuente de verdad editable para el lenguaje de la plataforma.');
      })
      .sort((a,b)=>a.querySelectorAll('*').length-b.querySelectorAll('*').length)[0];

    if(duplicate)duplicate.remove();

    [...root.children].forEach(child=>{
      const text=String(child.textContent||'').trim();
      const interactive=child.querySelector('button,input,select,textarea,table,ul,ol,details');
      if(!text && !interactive)child.remove();
    });
  }

  function patch(){
    if(patching)return;
    patching=true;
    try{
      removeVocabularyDuplicate();
      mergeGlossaryIntro();
    }finally{
      patching=false;
    }
  }

  function observe(){
    patch();
    const taxonomy=document.getElementById('taxonomy');
    if(!taxonomy)return;
    new MutationObserver(()=>patch()).observe(taxonomy,{childList:true,subtree:true});
  }

  if(document.readyState==='loading'){
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>setTimeout(observe,0));
  }else{
    setTimeout(observe,0);
  }

  window.addEventListener('load',()=>{
    setTimeout(patch,150);
    setTimeout(patch,500);
    setTimeout(patch,1200);
  });
})();
</script>


<script id="ux-vocabulary-create-entry">
(function(){
  const openButton=document.getElementById('taxonomy-vocabulary-create');
  const modal=document.getElementById('taxonomy-vocabulary-create-modal');
  const select=document.getElementById('taxonomy-vocabulary-create-select');
  const confirm=document.getElementById('taxonomy-vocabulary-create-confirm');
  if(!openButton||!modal||!select||!confirm)return;

  const norm=value=>String(value||'').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim();

  function vocabularyActions(){
    const root=document.getElementById('taxonomy-controlled');
    if(!root)return [];

    const buttons=[...root.querySelectorAll('button')].filter(button=>{
      const text=norm(button.textContent).toLocaleLowerCase('es');
      return text==='+ añadir' || text==='añadir' || text==='+ anadir' || text==='anadir';
    });

    return buttons.map((button,index)=>{
      let card=button.parentElement;
      while(card&&card!==root){
        const heading=card.querySelector('h2,h3,h4,strong,[class*=title],[class*=heading]');
        if(heading && norm(heading.textContent) && norm(heading.textContent)!==norm(button.textContent)){
          return {id:String(index),label:norm(heading.textContent),button};
        }
        card=card.parentElement;
      }
      return {id:String(index),label:`Vocabulario ${index+1}`,button};
    }).filter((item,pos,all)=>all.findIndex(other=>other.label===item.label)===pos);
  }

  function close(){
    modal.hidden=true;
    select.value='';
    confirm.disabled=true;
  }
